# The Psalms

*[World English Bible (Catholic)](README.md) — Text version: signed by eBible.org 2026-07-24 (pulled 2026-07-26).*

## Psalm 1

**BOOK 1**

<sup>1</sup> Blessed is the man who doesn’t walk in the counsel of the wicked,  
&emsp;nor stand on the path of sinners,  
&emsp;nor sit in the seat of scoffers;  
<sup>2</sup> but his delight is in the LORD’s[^1] law.  
&emsp;On his law he meditates day and night.  
<sup>3</sup> He will be like a tree planted by the streams of water,  
&emsp;that produces its fruit in its season,  
&emsp;whose leaf also does not wither.  
&emsp;Whatever he does shall prosper.  
<sup>4</sup> The wicked are not so,  
&emsp;but are like the chaff which the wind drives away.  
<sup>5</sup> Therefore the wicked shall not stand in the judgment,  
&emsp;nor sinners in the congregation of the righteous.  
<sup>6</sup> For the LORD knows the way of the righteous,  
&emsp;but the way of the wicked shall perish.

## Psalm 2

<sup>1</sup> Why do the nations rage,  
&emsp;and the peoples plot a vain thing?  
<sup>2</sup> The kings of the earth take a stand,  
&emsp;and the rulers take counsel together,  
&emsp;against the LORD, and against his Anointed,[^2] saying,  
<sup>3</sup> “Let’s break their bonds apart,  
&emsp;and cast their cords from us.”  
<sup>4</sup> He who sits in the heavens will laugh.  
&emsp;The Lord[^3] will have them in derision.  
<sup>5</sup> Then he will speak to them in his anger,  
&emsp;and terrify them in his wrath:  
<sup>6</sup> “Yet I have set my King on my holy hill of Zion.”  
&emsp;<sup>7</sup> I will tell of the decree:  
The LORD said to me, “You are my son.  
&emsp;Today I have become your father.  
<sup>8</sup> Ask of me, and I will give the nations for your inheritance,  
&emsp;the uttermost parts of the earth for your possession.  
<sup>9</sup> You shall break them with a rod of iron.  
&emsp;You shall dash them in pieces like a potter’s vessel.”  
<sup>10</sup> Now therefore be wise, you kings.  
&emsp;Be instructed, you judges of the earth.  
<sup>11</sup> Serve the LORD with fear,  
&emsp;and rejoice with trembling.  
<sup>12</sup> ~~Give sincere homage to the Son~~ <ins>Take hold of discipline</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c1"></a>[c1](#c1)</sup>,<sup><a name="ref-e1"></a>[e1](#e1)</sup>[^4] lest he be angry, and you perish on the way,  
&emsp;for his wrath will soon be kindled.  
&emsp;Blessed are all those who take refuge in him.

## Psalm 3

*A Psalm by David, when he fled from Absalom his son.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, how my adversaries have increased!  
&emsp;Many are those who rise up against me.  
<sup>2</sup> Many there are who say of my soul,  
&emsp;“There is no help for him in God.”[^5] *Selah.*  
<sup>3</sup> But you, LORD, are a shield around me,  
&emsp;my glory, and the one who lifts up my head.  
<sup>4</sup> I cry to the LORD with my voice,  
&emsp;and he answers me out of his holy hill. *Selah.*  
<sup>5</sup> I laid myself down and slept.  
&emsp;I awakened, for the LORD sustains me.  
<sup>6</sup> I will not be afraid of tens of thousands of people  
&emsp;who have set themselves against me on every side.  
<sup>7</sup> Arise, LORD!  
&emsp;Save me, my God!  
For you have struck all of my enemies on the cheek bone.  
&emsp;You have broken the teeth of the wicked.  
<sup>8</sup> Salvation belongs to the LORD.  
&emsp;May your blessing be on your people. *Selah.*

## Psalm 4

*For the Chief Musician; on stringed instruments. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> ~~Answer me when I call, God of my righteousness.~~ <ins>When I called, the God of my righteousness answered me.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c2"></a>[c2](#c2)</sup>  
&emsp;Give me relief from my distress.  
&emsp;Have mercy on me, and hear my prayer.  
<sup>2</sup> You sons of men, ~~how long shall my glory be turned into dishonor~~ <ins>how long will you be heavy of heart</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c3"></a>[c3](#c3)</sup>?  
&emsp;Will you love vanity and seek after falsehood? *Selah.*  
<sup>3</sup> But know that the LORD has ~~set apart for himself him who is godly~~ <ins>made wondrous his holy one</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c4"></a>[c4](#c4)</sup>;  
&emsp;The LORD will hear when I call to him.  
<sup>4</sup> Stand in awe, and don’t sin.  
&emsp;Search your own heart on your bed, and be still. *Selah.*  
<sup>5</sup> Offer the sacrifices of righteousness.  
&emsp;Put your trust in the LORD.  
<sup>6</sup> Many say, “Who will show us any good?”  
&emsp;LORD, let the light of your face shine on us.  
<sup>7</sup> You have put gladness in my heart,  
&emsp;more than when their grain and their new wine are increased.  
<sup>8</sup> In peace I will both lay myself down and sleep,  
&emsp;for you alone, LORD, ~~make me live in safety~~ <ins>have established me in hope</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c5"></a>[c5](#c5)</sup>.

## Psalm 5

*For the Chief Musician, with the flutes. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> Give ear to my words, LORD.  
&emsp;Consider my meditation.  
<sup>2</sup> Listen to the voice of my cry, my King and my God,  
&emsp;for I pray to you.  
<sup>3</sup> LORD, in the morning you will hear my voice.  
&emsp;In the morning I will ~~lay my requests before you~~ <ins>stand before you</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c6"></a>[c6](#c6)</sup>, and will watch expectantly.  
<sup>4</sup> For you are not a God who has pleasure in wickedness.  
&emsp;Evil can’t live with you.  
<sup>5</sup> ~~The arrogant~~ <ins>The unjust</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c7"></a>[c7](#c7)</sup> will not stand in your sight.  
&emsp;You hate all workers of iniquity.  
<sup>6</sup> You will destroy those who speak lies.  
&emsp;The LORD abhors the bloodthirsty and deceitful man.  
<sup>7</sup> But as for me, in the abundance of your loving kindness I will come into your house.  
&emsp;I will bow toward your holy temple in reverence of you.  
<sup>8</sup> Lead me, LORD, in your righteousness because of my enemies.  
&emsp;Make your way straight before my face.  
<sup>9</sup> For there is no faithfulness in their mouth.  
&emsp;Their heart is ~~destruction~~ <ins>a pit</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c8"></a>[c8](#c8)</sup>.  
&emsp;Their throat is an open tomb.  
&emsp;They flatter with their tongue.  
<sup>10</sup> Hold them guilty, God.  
&emsp;Let them fall by their own counsels.  
Thrust them out in the multitude of their transgressions,  
&emsp;for they have rebelled against you.  
<sup>11</sup> But let all those who take refuge in you rejoice.  
&emsp;Let them always shout for joy, because ~~you defend them~~ <ins>you overshadow them</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c9"></a>[c9](#c9)</sup>.  
Let them also who love your name be joyful in you.  
&emsp;<sup>12</sup> For you will bless the righteous.  
LORD, you will ~~surround~~ <ins>crown</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c10"></a>[c10](#c10)</sup> him with favor as with a shield.

## Psalm 6

*For the Chief Musician; on stringed instruments, upon the eight-stringed lyre. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, don’t rebuke me in your anger,  
&emsp;neither discipline me in your wrath.  
<sup>2</sup> Have mercy on me, LORD, for I am faint.  
&emsp;LORD, heal me, for my bones are troubled.  
<sup>3</sup> My soul is also in great anguish.  
&emsp;But you, LORD—how long?  
<sup>4</sup> Return, LORD. Deliver my soul,  
&emsp;and save me for your loving kindness’ sake.  
<sup>5</sup> For in death there is no memory of you.  
&emsp;In Sheol,[^6] who shall give you thanks?  
<sup>6</sup> I am weary with my groaning.  
&emsp;Every night I flood my bed.  
&emsp;I drench my couch with my tears.  
<sup>7</sup> My eye wastes away because of grief.  
&emsp;~~It grows old because of~~ <ins>I have grown old among</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c11"></a>[c11](#c11)</sup> all my adversaries.  
<sup>8</sup> Depart from me, all you workers of iniquity,  
&emsp;for the LORD has heard the voice of my weeping.  
<sup>9</sup> The LORD has heard my supplication.  
&emsp;The LORD accepts my prayer.  
<sup>10</sup> May all my enemies be ashamed and dismayed.  
&emsp;They shall turn back, they shall be disgraced suddenly.

## Psalm 7

*A meditation by David, which he sang to the LORD, concerning the words of Cush, the Benjamite.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, my God, I ~~take refuge~~ <ins>trust</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c12"></a>[c12](#c12)</sup> in you.  
&emsp;Save me from all those who pursue me, and deliver me,  
<sup>2</sup> lest they ~~tear apart my soul~~ <ins>seize my soul</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c13"></a>[c13](#c13)</sup> like a lion,  
&emsp;ripping it in pieces, while there is no one to deliver.  
<sup>3</sup> LORD, my God, if I have done this,  
&emsp;if there is iniquity in my hands,  
<sup>4</sup> if I have rewarded evil to him who was at peace with me  
&emsp;(yes, I have plundered ~~him who without cause was my adversary~~ <ins>my adversary, sending him away empty</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c14"></a>[c14](#c14)</sup>),  
&emsp;<sup>5</sup> let the enemy pursue my soul, and overtake it;  
yes, let him tread my life down to the earth,  
&emsp;and lay my glory in the dust. *Selah.*  
<sup>6</sup> Arise, LORD, in your anger.  
&emsp;Lift up yourself against the rage of my adversaries.  
Awake for me. You have commanded judgment.  
&emsp;<sup>7</sup> Let the congregation of the peoples surround you.  
&emsp;Rule over them on high.  
<sup>8</sup> The LORD administers judgment to the peoples.  
&emsp;Judge me, LORD, according to my righteousness,  
&emsp;and to my integrity that is in me.  
<sup>9</sup> Oh let the wickedness of the wicked come to an end,  
&emsp;but establish the righteous;  
&emsp;their minds and hearts are searched by the righteous God.  
<sup>10</sup> My shield is with God,  
&emsp;who saves the upright in heart.  
<sup>11</sup> God is a righteous judge,  
&emsp;yes, a God who has indignation every day.<sup><a name="ref-x1"></a>[x1](#x1)</sup>  
<sup>12</sup> If a man doesn’t repent, he will sharpen his sword;  
&emsp;he has bent and strung his bow.  
<sup>13</sup> He has also prepared for himself the instruments of death.  
&emsp;He makes ready his flaming arrows.  
<sup>14</sup> Behold,[^7] he travails with iniquity.  
&emsp;Yes, he has conceived ~~mischief~~ <ins>sorrow</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c15"></a>[c15](#c15)</sup>,  
&emsp;~~and brought out falsehood.~~ <ins>and brought forth iniquity.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c16"></a>[c16](#c16)</sup>  
<sup>15</sup> He has dug a hole,  
&emsp;and has fallen into the pit which he made.  
<sup>16</sup> The trouble he causes shall return to his own head.  
&emsp;His violence shall come down on the crown of his own head.  
<sup>17</sup> I will give thanks to the LORD according to his righteousness,  
&emsp;and will sing praise to the name of the LORD Most High.

## Psalm 8

*For the Chief Musician; on an instrument of Gath. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, our Lord, how majestic is your name in all the earth!  
&emsp;You have set your glory above the heavens!  
<sup>2</sup> From the lips of babes and infants you have ~~established strength~~ <ins>perfected praise</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c17"></a>[c17](#c17)</sup>,  
&emsp;because of your adversaries, that you might ~~silence~~ <ins>destroy</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c18"></a>[c18](#c18)</sup> the enemy and the avenger.  
<sup>3</sup> When I consider your heavens, the work of your fingers,  
&emsp;the moon and the stars, which you have ordained,  
<sup>4</sup> what is man, that you think of him?  
&emsp;What is the son of man, that you care for him?  
<sup>5</sup> For you have made him a little lower than the angels,[^8]  
&emsp;and crowned him with glory and honor.  
<sup>6</sup> You make him ruler over the works of your hands.  
&emsp;You have put all things under his feet:  
<sup>7</sup> All sheep and cattle,  
&emsp;yes, and the animals of the field,  
&emsp;<sup>8</sup> the birds of the sky, the fish of the sea,  
&emsp;and whatever passes through the paths of the seas.  
<sup>9</sup> LORD, our Lord,  
&emsp;how majestic is your name in all the earth!

## Psalm 9

*For the Chief Musician. Set to “The Death of the Son.” A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> I will give thanks to the LORD with my whole heart.  
&emsp;I will tell of all your marvelous works.  
<sup>2</sup> I will be glad and rejoice in you.  
&emsp;I will sing praise to your name, O Most High.  
<sup>3</sup> When my enemies turn back,  
&emsp;they stumble and perish in your presence.  
<sup>4</sup> For you have maintained my just cause.  
&emsp;You sit on the throne judging righteously.  
<sup>5</sup> You have rebuked the nations.  
&emsp;You have destroyed the wicked.  
&emsp;You have blotted out their name forever and ever.  
<sup>6</sup> The enemy is overtaken by endless ruin.  
&emsp;The very memory of the cities which you have overthrown has perished.  
<sup>7</sup> But the LORD reigns forever.  
&emsp;He has prepared his throne for judgment.  
<sup>8</sup> He will judge the world in righteousness.  
&emsp;He will administer judgment to the peoples in uprightness.  
<sup>9</sup> The LORD will also be a high tower for the oppressed;  
&emsp;a high tower in times of trouble.  
<sup>10</sup> Those who know your name will put their trust in you,  
&emsp;for you, LORD, have not forsaken those who seek you.  
<sup>11</sup> Sing praises to the LORD, who dwells in Zion,  
&emsp;and declare among the people what he has done.  
<sup>12</sup> For he who avenges blood remembers them.  
&emsp;He doesn’t forget the cry of the afflicted.  
<sup>13</sup> Have mercy on me, LORD.  
&emsp;See my affliction by those who hate me,  
and lift me up from the gates of death,  
&emsp;<sup>14</sup> that I may show all of your praise.  
&emsp;I will rejoice in your salvation in the gates of the daughter of Zion.  
<sup>15</sup> The nations have sunk down in the pit that they made.  
&emsp;In the net which they hid, their own foot is taken.  
<sup>16</sup> The LORD has made himself known.  
&emsp;He has executed judgment.  
&emsp;The wicked is snared by the work of his own hands. *Meditation. Selah.*  
<sup>17</sup> The wicked shall be turned back to Sheol,[^9]  
&emsp;even all the nations that forget God.  
<sup>18</sup> For the needy shall not always be forgotten,  
&emsp;nor the hope of the poor perish forever.  
<sup>19</sup> Arise, LORD! Don’t let man prevail.  
&emsp;Let the nations be judged in your sight.  
<sup>20</sup> Put them in fear, LORD.  
&emsp;Let the nations know that they are only men. *Selah.*

## Psalm 10

<sup>1</sup> Why do you stand far off, LORD?  
&emsp;Why do you hide yourself in times of trouble?  
<sup>2</sup> In arrogance, the wicked hunt down the weak.  
&emsp;They are caught in the schemes that they devise.  
<sup>3</sup> For the wicked boasts of his heart’s cravings.  
&emsp;~~He blesses the greedy and condemns the LORD.~~ <ins>The greedy one blesses himself; the sinner despises the LORD.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c19"></a>[c19](#c19)</sup>  
<sup>4</sup> The wicked, in the pride of his face,  
&emsp;~~has no room in his thoughts for God.~~ <ins>thinks, “He will not seek; there is no God.”</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c20"></a>[c20](#c20)</sup>  
<sup>5</sup> His ways are prosperous at all times.  
&emsp;He is arrogant, and your laws are far from his sight.  
As for all his adversaries, he sneers at them.  
&emsp;<sup>6</sup> He says in his heart, “I shall not be shaken.  
&emsp;For generations I shall have no trouble.”  
<sup>7</sup> His mouth is full of cursing, deceit, and ~~oppression~~ <ins>guile</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c21"></a>[c21](#c21)</sup>.  
&emsp;Under his tongue is mischief and iniquity.  
<sup>8</sup> He lies in wait near the villages.  
&emsp;From ambushes, he murders the innocent.  
His eyes are secretly set against the ~~helpless~~ <ins>poor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c22"></a>[c22](#c22)</sup>.  
<sup>9</sup> He lurks in secret as a lion in his ambush.  
&emsp;He lies in wait to catch the helpless.  
&emsp;He catches the helpless when he draws him in his net.  
<sup>10</sup> ~~The helpless are crushed.~~ <ins>He rushes in and crouches down,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c23"></a>[c23](#c23)</sup><sup><a name="ref-e2"></a>[e2](#e2)</sup>  
&emsp;~~They collapse.~~<sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c24"></a>[c24](#c24)</sup>  
&emsp;~~They fall under his strength.~~ <ins>and the helpless fall by the strength of his arms.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c25"></a>[c25](#c25)</sup>  
<sup>11</sup> He says in his heart, “God has forgotten.  
&emsp;He hides his face.  
&emsp;He will never see it.”

<sup>12</sup> Arise, LORD!  
&emsp;God, lift up your hand!  
&emsp;Don’t forget the helpless.  
<sup>13</sup> Why does the wicked person condemn God,  
&emsp;and say in his heart, “God won’t call me into account”?  
<sup>14</sup> But you do see trouble and grief.  
&emsp;You consider it to take it into your hand.  
&emsp;You help ~~the victim~~ <ins>the poor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c26"></a>[c26](#c26)</sup> and the fatherless.  
<sup>15</sup> Break the arm of the wicked.  
&emsp;As for the evil man, seek out his wickedness until you find none.  
<sup>16</sup> The LORD is King forever and ever!  
&emsp;The nations will perish out of his land.  
<sup>17</sup> LORD, you have heard the desire of the ~~humble~~ <ins>poor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c27"></a>[c27](#c27)</sup>.  
&emsp;You will prepare their heart.  
&emsp;You will cause your ear to hear,  
&emsp;<sup>18</sup> to judge the fatherless and the ~~oppressed~~ <ins>lowly</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c28"></a>[c28](#c28)</sup>,  
&emsp;that man who is of the earth may terrify no more.

## Psalm 11

*For the Chief Musician. By David.*

<sup>1</sup> In the LORD, I ~~take refuge~~ <ins>trust</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c29"></a>[c29](#c29)</sup>.  
&emsp;How can you say to my soul, “Flee as a bird to your mountain”?  
<sup>2</sup> For, behold, the wicked bend their bows.  
&emsp;They set their arrows on the strings,  
&emsp;that they may shoot in darkness at the upright in heart.  
<sup>3</sup> If the foundations are destroyed,  
&emsp;what can the righteous do?  
<sup>4</sup> The LORD is in his holy temple.  
&emsp;The LORD is on his throne in heaven.  
~~His eyes observe~~ <ins>His eyes look upon the poor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c30"></a>[c30](#c30)</sup>.  
&emsp;His eyes examine the children of men.  
<sup>5</sup> The LORD examines ~~the righteous,~~ <ins>the righteous and the wicked,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c33"></a>[c33](#c33)</sup>  
&emsp;~~but his soul hates the wicked and him who loves~~ <ins>but his soul hates him who loves</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c31"></a>[c31](#c31)</sup> ~~violence~~ <ins>iniquity</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c32"></a>[c32](#c32)</sup>.  
<sup>6</sup> On the wicked he will rain blazing coals;  
&emsp;fire, sulfur, and ~~scorching wind~~ <ins>stormy wind</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c34"></a>[c34](#c34)</sup> shall be the portion of their cup.  
<sup>7</sup> For the LORD is righteous.  
&emsp;He loves righteousness.  
&emsp;The upright shall see his face.

## Psalm 12

*For the Chief Musician; upon an eight-stringed lyre. A Psalm of David.*

<sup>1</sup> Help, LORD; for the godly man ceases.  
&emsp;For the faithful fail from among the children of men.  
<sup>2</sup> Everyone lies to his neighbor.  
&emsp;They speak with flattering lips, and with a double heart.  
<sup>3</sup> May the LORD cut off all ~~flattering~~ <ins>deceitful</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c35"></a>[c35](#c35)</sup> lips,  
&emsp;and the tongue that boasts,  
<sup>4</sup> who have said, “With our tongue we will prevail.  
&emsp;Our lips are our own.  
&emsp;Who is lord over us?”  
<sup>5</sup> “Because of ~~the oppression of the weak~~ <ins>the misery of the weak</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c36"></a>[c36](#c36)</sup> and because of the groaning of the needy,  
&emsp;I will now arise,” says the LORD;  
“~~I will set him in safety from those who malign him.~~ <ins>I will place in safety him whom they despise.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c37"></a>[c37](#c37)</sup>”  
<sup>6</sup> The LORD’s words are flawless words,  
&emsp;~~as silver refined in a clay furnace~~ <ins>as silver tried by fire, separated from the earth</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c38"></a>[c38](#c38)</sup>, purified seven times.  
<sup>7</sup> ~~You will keep them, LORD.~~ <ins>You will keep us, LORD.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c39"></a>[c39](#c39)</sup>  
&emsp;~~You will preserve them from this generation forever.~~ <ins>You will guard us from this generation forever.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c40"></a>[c40](#c40)</sup>  
<sup>8</sup> The wicked walk on every side,  
&emsp;when what is vile is exalted among the sons of men.

## Psalm 13

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> How long, LORD?  
&emsp;Will you forget me forever?  
&emsp;How long will you hide your face from me?  
<sup>2</sup> How long shall I take counsel in my soul,  
&emsp;having sorrow in my heart every day?  
&emsp;How long shall my enemy triumph over me?  
<sup>3</sup> Behold, and answer me, LORD, my God.  
&emsp;Give light to my eyes, lest I sleep in death;  
&emsp;<sup>4</sup> lest my enemy say, “I have prevailed against him;”  
&emsp;lest my adversaries rejoice when I fall.

<sup>5</sup> But I trust in your loving kindness.  
&emsp;My heart rejoices in your salvation.  
<sup>6</sup> I will sing to the LORD,  
&emsp;because he has been good to me.

## Psalm 14

*For the Chief Musician. By David.*

<sup>1</sup> The fool has said in his heart, “There is no God.”  
&emsp;They are corrupt.  
&emsp;They have done abominable deeds.  
&emsp;There is no one who does good.  
<sup>2</sup> The LORD looked down from heaven on the children of men,  
&emsp;to see if there were any who understood,  
&emsp;who sought after God.  
<sup>3</sup> They have all gone aside.  
&emsp;They have together become corrupt.  
&emsp;There is no one who does good, no, not one.  
<sup>4</sup> Have all the workers of iniquity no knowledge,  
&emsp;who eat up my people as they eat bread,  
&emsp;and don’t call on the LORD?  
<sup>5</sup> There they were in great fear,  
&emsp;for God is in the generation of the righteous.  
<sup>6</sup> You frustrate the plan of the poor,  
&emsp;because the LORD is his refuge.  
<sup>7</sup> ~~Oh that the salvation of Israel would come out of Zion!~~ <ins>Who will grant out of Zion the salvation of Israel?</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c42"></a>[c42](#c42)</sup>  
&emsp;When the LORD ~~restores the fortunes~~ <ins>brings back the captivity</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c41"></a>[c41](#c41)</sup> of his people,  
&emsp;then Jacob shall rejoice, and Israel shall be glad.

## Psalm 15

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, who shall dwell in your sanctuary?  
&emsp;Who shall live on your holy hill?  
<sup>2</sup> He who walks blamelessly and does what is right,  
&emsp;and speaks truth in his heart;  
<sup>3</sup> he who doesn’t ~~slander~~ <ins>act deceitfully</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c43"></a>[c43](#c43)</sup> with his tongue,  
&emsp;nor does evil to his friend,  
&emsp;nor casts slurs against his fellow man;  
<sup>4</sup> in whose eyes a vile man is despised,  
&emsp;but who honors those who fear the LORD;  
&emsp;he who keeps an oath even when it hurts, and doesn’t change;  
<sup>5</sup> he who doesn’t lend out his money for usury,  
&emsp;nor take a bribe against the innocent.

He who does these things shall never be shaken.

## Psalm 16

*A Poem by David.*

<sup>1</sup> Preserve me, God, for I take refuge in you.  
<sup>2</sup> My soul, you have said to the LORD, “You are my Lord.  
&emsp;Apart from you I have no good thing.”  
<sup>3</sup> As for the saints who are on the earth,  
&emsp;they are the excellent ones in whom is all my delight.

<sup>4</sup> Their sorrows shall be multiplied who give gifts to another god.  
&emsp;Their drink offerings of blood I will not offer,  
&emsp;nor take their names on my lips.  
<sup>5</sup> ~~The LORD assigned my portion and my cup.~~ <ins>The LORD is the portion of my inheritance and of my cup.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c44"></a>[c44](#c44)</sup>  
&emsp;You made my lot secure.

<sup>6</sup> The lines have fallen to me in pleasant places.  
&emsp;Yes, I have a good inheritance.  
<sup>7</sup> I will bless the LORD, who has given me counsel.  
&emsp;Yes, my heart instructs me in the night seasons.  
<sup>8</sup> I have set the LORD always before me.  
&emsp;Because he is at my right hand, I shall not be moved.  
<sup>9</sup> Therefore my heart is glad, and my tongue rejoices.  
&emsp;My body shall also ~~dwell in safety~~ <ins>rest in hope</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c45"></a>[c45](#c45)</sup>.  
<sup>10</sup> For you will not leave my soul in Sheol,[^10]  
&emsp;neither will you allow your holy one to see corruption.  
<sup>11</sup> You will show me the path of life.  
&emsp;In your presence is fullness of joy.  
In your right hand there are pleasures forever more.

## Psalm 17

*A Prayer by David.*

<sup>1</sup> Hear, LORD, my righteous plea.  
&emsp;Give ear to my prayer that doesn’t go out of deceitful lips.  
<sup>2</sup> Let my sentence come out of your presence.  
&emsp;Let your eyes look on equity.  
<sup>3</sup> You have proved my heart.  
&emsp;You have visited me in the night.  
&emsp;~~You have tried me, and found nothing.~~ <ins>Try me by fire, and you will find no iniquity.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c46"></a>[c46](#c46)</sup>  
&emsp;I have resolved that my mouth shall not disobey.  
<sup>4</sup> ~~As for the deeds of men, by the word of your lips,~~ <ins>My mouth does not transgress as men do. By the word of your lips,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c47"></a>[c47](#c47)</sup>  
&emsp;I have kept myself from the ways of the violent.  
<sup>5</sup> My steps have held fast to your paths.  
&emsp;My feet have not slipped.  
<sup>6</sup> I have called on you, for you will answer me, God.  
&emsp;Turn your ear to me.  
&emsp;Hear my speech.  
<sup>7</sup> Show your marvelous loving kindness,  
&emsp;you who save those who take refuge by your right hand from their enemies.  
<sup>8</sup> Keep me as the apple of your eye.  
&emsp;Hide me under the shadow of your wings,  
<sup>9</sup> from the wicked who oppress me,  
&emsp;my ~~deadly~~ <ins>enraged</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c48"></a>[c48](#c48)</sup> enemies, who surround me.  
<sup>10</sup> They close up their callous hearts.  
&emsp;With their mouth they speak proudly.  
<sup>11</sup> They have now surrounded us in our steps.  
&emsp;They set their eyes to cast us down to the earth.  
<sup>12</sup> He is like a lion that is greedy of his prey,  
&emsp;as it were a young lion lurking in secret places.  
<sup>13</sup> Arise, LORD, confront him.  
&emsp;Cast him down.  
Deliver my soul from the wicked by your sword,  
&emsp;<sup>14</sup> from men by your hand, LORD,  
&emsp;from men of the world, ~~whose portion is in this life~~ <ins>whose portion of life has failed</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c50"></a>[c50](#c50)</sup>.  
~~You fill the belly of your cherished ones.~~ <ins>May you fill their belly with what you have laid away.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c51"></a>[c51](#c51)</sup>  
&emsp;~~Your sons have plenty,~~ <ins>May their children be satisfied,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c49"></a>[c49](#c49)</sup>  
&emsp;and ~~they store up wealth for their children.~~ <ins>may they leave what remains to their little ones.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c52"></a>[c52](#c52)</sup>  
<sup>15</sup> As for me, I shall see your face in righteousness.  
&emsp;I shall be satisfied, when I awake, with seeing your form.

## Psalm 18

*For the Chief Musician. By David the servant of the LORD, who spoke to the LORD the words of this song in the day that the LORD delivered him from the hand of all his enemies, and from the hand of Saul. He said,*

<sup>1</sup> I love you, LORD, my strength.  
<sup>2</sup> The LORD is my ~~rock~~ <ins>support</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c53"></a>[c53](#c53)</sup>, my fortress, and my deliverer;  
&emsp;my God, my ~~rock~~ <ins>helper</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c54"></a>[c54](#c54)</sup>, in whom I take refuge;  
&emsp;my shield, and the horn of my salvation, my high tower.  
<sup>3</sup> I call on the LORD, who is worthy to be praised;  
&emsp;and I am saved from my enemies.  
<sup>4</sup> The ~~cords~~ <ins>waves</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c55"></a>[c55](#c55)</sup> of death surrounded me.  
&emsp;The floods of ~~ungodliness~~ <ins>Belial</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c56"></a>[c56](#c56)</sup> made me afraid.  
<sup>5</sup> The cords of Sheol[^11] were around me.  
&emsp;The snares of death came on me.  
<sup>6</sup> In my distress I called on the LORD,  
&emsp;and cried to my God.  
He heard my voice out of his temple.  
&emsp;My cry before him came into his ears.  
<sup>7</sup> Then the earth shook and trembled.  
&emsp;The foundations also of the mountains quaked and were shaken,  
&emsp;because he was angry.  
<sup>8</sup> Smoke went out of his nostrils.  
&emsp;Consuming fire came out of his mouth.  
&emsp;Coals were kindled by it.  
<sup>9</sup> He bowed the heavens also, and came down.  
&emsp;Thick darkness was under his feet.  
<sup>10</sup> He rode on a cherub, and flew.  
&emsp;Yes, he soared on the wings of the wind.  
<sup>11</sup> He made darkness his hiding place, his pavilion around him,  
&emsp;darkness of waters, thick clouds of the skies.  
<sup>12</sup> At the brightness before him his thick clouds passed,  
&emsp;hailstones and coals of fire.  
<sup>13</sup> The LORD also thundered in the sky.  
&emsp;The Most High uttered his voice:  
&emsp;hailstones and coals of fire.  
<sup>14</sup> He sent out his arrows, and scattered them.  
&emsp;He routed them with great lightning bolts.  
<sup>15</sup> Then the ~~channels of waters~~ <ins>springs of waters</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c58"></a>[c58](#c58)</sup> appeared.  
&emsp;The foundations of the world were laid bare at your rebuke, LORD,  
&emsp;at the ~~blast of the breath of your nostrils~~ <ins>blast of your wrathful breath</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c57"></a>[c57](#c57)</sup>.  
<sup>16</sup> He sent from on high.  
&emsp;He took me.  
&emsp;He drew me out of many waters.  
<sup>17</sup> He delivered me from my strong enemy,  
&emsp;from those who hated me; for they were too mighty for me.  
<sup>18</sup> They came on me in the day of my calamity,  
&emsp;but the LORD was my support.  
<sup>19</sup> He brought me out also into a large place.  
&emsp;He delivered me, because he delighted in me.  
<sup>20</sup> The LORD has rewarded me according to my righteousness.  
&emsp;According to the cleanness of my hands, he has recompensed me.  
<sup>21</sup> For I have kept the ways of the LORD,  
&emsp;and have not wickedly departed from my God.  
<sup>22</sup> For all his ordinances were before me.  
&emsp;I didn’t put away his statutes from me.  
<sup>23</sup> I was also blameless with him.  
&emsp;I kept myself from my iniquity.  
<sup>24</sup> Therefore the LORD has rewarded me according to my righteousness,  
&emsp;according to the cleanness of my hands in his eyesight.  
<sup>25</sup> With the ~~merciful~~ <ins>holy</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c59"></a>[c59](#c59)</sup> you will show yourself ~~merciful~~ <ins>holy</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c60"></a>[c60](#c60)</sup>.  
&emsp;With the perfect man, you will show yourself perfect.  
<sup>26</sup> ~~With the pure, you will show yourself pure.~~ <ins>With the elect, you will show yourself elect.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c62"></a>[c62](#c62)</sup>  
&emsp;With the ~~crooked~~ <ins>perverse</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c61"></a>[c61](#c61)</sup> you will show yourself shrewd.  
<sup>27</sup> For you will save the afflicted people,  
&emsp;but the arrogant eyes you will bring down.  
<sup>28</sup> For you will light my lamp, LORD.  
&emsp;My God will light up my darkness.  
<sup>29</sup> For by you, I advance through a troop.  
&emsp;By my God, I leap over a wall.  
<sup>30</sup> As for God, his way is perfect.  
&emsp;~~The LORD’s word is tried.~~ <ins>The LORD’s words are tried by fire.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c63"></a>[c63](#c63)</sup>  
&emsp;He is a shield to all those who take refuge in him.  
<sup>31</sup> For who is God, except the LORD?  
&emsp;Who is a ~~rock~~ <ins>fortress</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c64"></a>[c64](#c64)</sup>, besides our God,  
&emsp;<sup>32</sup> the God who arms me with strength, and makes my way perfect?  
<sup>33</sup> He makes my feet like deer’s feet,  
&emsp;and sets me on my high places.  
<sup>34</sup> He teaches my hands to war,  
&emsp;so that my arms bend a bow of bronze.  
<sup>35</sup> You have also given me the shield of your salvation.  
&emsp;Your right hand sustains me.  
&emsp;Your ~~gentleness~~ <ins>favorable hearing</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c65"></a>[c65](#c65)</sup> has made me great.  
<sup>36</sup> You have enlarged my steps under me,  
&emsp;My feet have not slipped.  
<sup>37</sup> I will pursue my enemies, and overtake them.  
&emsp;I won’t turn away until they are consumed.  
<sup>38</sup> ~~I will strike them through, so that they will not be able to rise.~~ <ins>I crushed them, so that they were not able to rise.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c66"></a>[c66](#c66)</sup>  
&emsp;~~They shall fall under my feet.~~ <ins>They fell under my feet.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c67"></a>[c67](#c67)</sup>  
<sup>39</sup> For you have armed me with strength to the battle.  
&emsp;You have subdued under me those who rose up against me.  
<sup>40</sup> You have also made my enemies turn their backs to me,  
&emsp;that I might cut off those who hate me.  
<sup>41</sup> They cried, but there was no one to save;  
&emsp;even to the LORD, but he didn’t answer them.  
<sup>42</sup> Then I beat them small as the dust before the wind.  
&emsp;I ~~cast them out~~ <ins>crushed them</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c68"></a>[c68](#c68)</sup> as the mire of the streets.  
<sup>43</sup> You have delivered me from the strivings of the people.  
&emsp;You have made me the head of the nations.  
A people whom I have not known shall serve me.  
&emsp;<sup>44</sup> ~~As soon as they hear of me they shall obey me.~~ <ins>At the hearing of the ear they obeyed me.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c70"></a>[c70](#c70)</sup>  
&emsp;~~The foreigners shall submit themselves to me.~~ <ins>The foreigners flattered me.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c69"></a>[c69](#c69)</sup>  
<sup>45</sup> The foreigners shall fade away,  
&emsp;and shall come trembling out of their strongholds.  
<sup>46</sup> The LORD lives! Blessed be my ~~rock~~ <ins>helper</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c71"></a>[c71](#c71)</sup>.  
&emsp;Exalted be the God of my salvation,  
<sup>47</sup> even the God who executes vengeance for me,  
&emsp;and subdues peoples under me.  
<sup>48</sup> He rescues me from my ~~enemies~~ <ins>enraged enemies</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c72"></a>[c72](#c72)</sup>.  
&emsp;Yes, you lift me up above those who rise up against me.  
&emsp;You deliver me from the violent man.  
<sup>49</sup> Therefore I will give thanks to you, LORD, among the nations,  
&emsp;and will sing praises to your name.  
<sup>50</sup> He gives great deliverance to his king,  
&emsp;and shows loving kindness to his anointed,  
&emsp;to David and to his offspring,[^12] forever more.

## Psalm 19

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> The heavens declare the glory of God.  
&emsp;The expanse shows his handiwork.  
<sup>2</sup> Day after day they pour out speech,  
&emsp;and night after night they display knowledge.  
<sup>3</sup> There is no speech nor language  
&emsp;where their voice is not heard.  
<sup>4</sup> Their voice has gone out through all the earth,  
&emsp;their words to the end of the world.  
In them he has set a tent for the sun,  
&emsp;<sup>5</sup> which is as a bridegroom coming out of his room,  
&emsp;like a ~~strong man~~ <ins>giant</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c73"></a>[c73](#c73)</sup> rejoicing to run his course.  
<sup>6</sup> His going out is from the end of the heavens,  
&emsp;his circuit to its ends.  
&emsp;There is nothing hidden from its heat.

<sup>7</sup> The LORD’s law is perfect, restoring the soul.  
&emsp;The LORD’s covenant is sure, making wise the simple.  
<sup>8</sup> The LORD’s precepts are right, rejoicing the heart.  
&emsp;The LORD’s commandment is pure, enlightening the eyes.  
<sup>9</sup> The fear of the LORD is clean, enduring forever.  
&emsp;The LORD’s ordinances are true, and righteous altogether.  
<sup>10</sup> They are more to be desired than gold, yes, ~~than much fine gold~~ <ins>than many precious stones</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c74"></a>[c74](#c74)</sup>,  
&emsp;sweeter also than honey and the extract of the honeycomb.  
<sup>11</sup> Moreover your servant is ~~warned~~ <ins>instructed</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c75"></a>[c75](#c75)</sup> by them.  
&emsp;In keeping them there is great reward.  
<sup>12</sup> Who can discern his errors?  
&emsp;Forgive me from hidden errors.

<sup>13</sup> Keep back your servant also from presumptuous sins.  
&emsp;Let them not have dominion over me.  
Then I will be upright.  
&emsp;I will be blameless and ~~innocent~~ <ins>cleansed</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c76"></a>[c76](#c76)</sup> of great transgression.  
<sup>14</sup> Let the words of my mouth and the meditation of my heart  
&emsp;be acceptable in your sight,  
&emsp;LORD, my ~~rock~~ <ins>helper</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c77"></a>[c77](#c77)</sup>, and my redeemer.

## Psalm 20

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> May the LORD answer you in the day of trouble.  
&emsp;May the name of the God of Jacob set you up on high,  
&emsp;<sup>2</sup> send you help from the sanctuary,  
&emsp;grant you support from Zion,  
&emsp;<sup>3</sup> remember all your offerings,  
&emsp;and ~~accept your burned sacrifice~~ <ins>regard your burned sacrifice with favor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c78"></a>[c78](#c78)</sup>. *Selah.*  
<sup>4</sup> May he grant you your heart’s desire,  
&emsp;and fulfill all your counsel.  
<sup>5</sup> We will triumph in your salvation.  
&emsp;In the name of our God, we will set up our banners.  
&emsp;May the LORD grant all your requests.  
<sup>6</sup> Now I know that the LORD saves his anointed.  
&emsp;~~He will answer him from his holy heaven,~~ <ins>He has answered him from his holy heaven,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c79"></a>[c79](#c79)</sup>  
&emsp;with the saving strength of his right hand.  
<sup>7</sup> Some trust in chariots, and some in horses,  
&emsp;~~but we trust in the name of the LORD our God.~~ <ins>but we have called upon the name of the LORD our God.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c80"></a>[c80](#c80)</sup>  
<sup>8</sup> They are bowed down and fallen,  
&emsp;but we rise up, and stand upright.  
<sup>9</sup> ~~Save, LORD!~~ <ins>LORD, save the king!</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c82"></a>[c82](#c82)</sup>  
&emsp;~~Let the King answer us when we call!~~ <ins>And answer us in the day we call on you!</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c81"></a>[c81](#c81)</sup>

## Psalm 21

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> The king rejoices in your strength, LORD!  
&emsp;How greatly he rejoices in your salvation!  
<sup>2</sup> You have given him his heart’s desire,  
&emsp;and have not withheld the request of his lips. *Selah.*  
<sup>3</sup> For you meet him with the blessings of goodness.  
&emsp;You set a crown of fine gold on his head.  
<sup>4</sup> He asked life of you and you gave it to him,  
&emsp;even length of days forever and ever.  
<sup>5</sup> His glory is great in your salvation.  
&emsp;You lay honor and majesty on him.  
<sup>6</sup> For you make him most blessed forever.  
&emsp;You make him glad with joy in your presence.  
<sup>7</sup> For the king trusts in the LORD.  
&emsp;Through the loving kindness of the Most High, he shall not be moved.  
<sup>8</sup> Your hand will find out all of your enemies.  
&emsp;Your right hand will find out those who hate you.  
<sup>9</sup> You will make them as a fiery furnace in the time of your anger.  
&emsp;The LORD will swallow them up in his wrath.  
&emsp;The fire shall devour them.  
<sup>10</sup> You will destroy their descendants from the earth,  
&emsp;their posterity from among the children of men.  
<sup>11</sup> For they intended evil against you.  
&emsp;They plotted evil against you which cannot succeed.  
<sup>12</sup> For you will make them turn their back,  
&emsp;when you aim drawn bows at their face.  
<sup>13</sup> Be exalted, LORD, in your strength,  
&emsp;so we will sing and praise your power.

## Psalm 22

*For the Chief Musician; set to “The Doe of the Morning.” A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> My God, my God, why have you forsaken me?  
&emsp;Why are you so far from helping me, and from the words of my groaning?  
<sup>2</sup> My God, I cry in the daytime, but you don’t answer;  
&emsp;in the night season, and am not silent.  
<sup>3</sup> But you are holy,  
&emsp;you who inhabit the praises of Israel.  
<sup>4</sup> Our fathers trusted in you.  
&emsp;They trusted, and you delivered them.  
<sup>5</sup> They cried to you, and were delivered.  
&emsp;They trusted in you, and were not disappointed.  
<sup>6</sup> But I am a worm, and no man;  
&emsp;a reproach of men, and despised by the people.  
<sup>7</sup> All those who see me mock me.  
&emsp;They insult me with their lips. They shake their heads, saying,  
&emsp;<sup>8</sup> “He trusts in the LORD.  
&emsp;Let him deliver him.  
&emsp;Let him rescue him, since he delights in him.”  
<sup>9</sup> But you brought me out of the womb.  
&emsp;~~You made me trust while at my mother’s breasts.~~ <ins>You were my hope while I was at my mother’s breasts.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c83"></a>[c83](#c83)</sup>  
<sup>10</sup> I was thrown on you from my mother’s womb.  
&emsp;You are my God since my mother bore me.  
<sup>11</sup> Don’t be far from me, for trouble is near.  
&emsp;For there is no one to help.  
<sup>12</sup> Many bulls have surrounded me.  
&emsp;Strong bulls of Bashan have encircled me.  
<sup>13</sup> They open their mouths wide against me,  
&emsp;lions tearing prey and roaring.  
<sup>14</sup> I am poured out like water.  
&emsp;All my bones are out of joint.  
My heart is like wax.  
&emsp;It is melted within me.  
<sup>15</sup> My ~~strength~~ <ins>palate</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c84"></a>[c84](#c84)</sup> is dried up like a potsherd.  
&emsp;My tongue sticks to the roof of my mouth.  
You have brought me into the dust of death.  
<sup>16</sup> For dogs have surrounded me.  
&emsp;A company of evildoers have enclosed me.  
&emsp;They have pierced my hands and feet.[^13]  
<sup>17</sup> I can count all of my bones.  
They look and stare at me.  
<sup>18</sup> They divide my garments among them.  
&emsp;They cast lots for my clothing.

<sup>19</sup> But don’t be far off, LORD.  
&emsp;You are my help. Hurry to help me!  
<sup>20</sup> Deliver my soul from the sword,  
&emsp;my precious life from the power of the dog.  
<sup>21</sup> Save me from the lion’s mouth!  
&emsp;~~Yes, you have rescued me from the horns of the wild oxen~~ <ins>Save my afflicted life from the horns of the unicorns</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c85"></a>[c85](#c85)</sup>.

<sup>22</sup> I will declare your name to my brothers.  
&emsp;Among the assembly, I will praise you.  
<sup>23</sup> You who fear the LORD, praise him!  
&emsp;All you descendants of Jacob, glorify him!  
&emsp;Stand in awe of him, all you descendants of Israel!  
<sup>24</sup> For he has not despised nor abhorred the affliction of the ~~afflicted~~ <ins>poor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c86"></a>[c86](#c86)</sup>,  
&emsp;neither has he hidden his face from him;  
&emsp;but when he cried to him, he heard.

<sup>25</sup> ~~My praise of you comes in the great assembly.~~ <ins>You are my praise in the great assembly.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c87"></a>[c87](#c87)</sup>  
&emsp;I will pay my vows before those who fear him.  
<sup>26</sup> The ~~humble~~ <ins>poor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c88"></a>[c88](#c88)</sup> shall eat and be satisfied.  
&emsp;They shall praise the LORD who seek after him.  
&emsp;Let your hearts live forever.  
<sup>27</sup> All the ends of the earth shall remember and turn to the LORD.  
&emsp;All the relatives of the nations shall worship before you.  
<sup>28</sup> For the kingdom is the LORD’s.  
&emsp;He is the ruler over the nations.  
<sup>29</sup> ~~All the rich ones of the earth shall eat and worship.~~ <ins>All who sleep in the earth shall worship him alone.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c89"></a>[c89](#c89)</sup>  
&emsp;All those who go down to the dust shall bow before him,  
&emsp;~~even he who can’t keep his soul alive~~ <ins>but my soul shall live for him</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c90"></a>[c90](#c90)</sup>.  
<sup>30</sup> Posterity shall serve him.  
&emsp;Future generations shall be told about the Lord.  
<sup>31</sup> They shall come and shall declare his righteousness to a people that shall be born,  
&emsp;for he has done it.

## Psalm 23

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> The LORD is my shepherd;  
&emsp;I shall lack nothing.  
<sup>2</sup> He makes me lie down in green pastures.  
&emsp;He leads me beside still waters.  
<sup>3</sup> He restores my soul.  
&emsp;He guides me in the paths of righteousness for his name’s sake.  
<sup>4</sup> Even though I walk through the valley of the shadow of death,  
&emsp;I will fear no evil, for you are with me.  
Your rod and your staff,  
&emsp;they comfort me.  
<sup>5</sup> You prepare a table before me  
&emsp;in the presence of my enemies.  
You anoint my head with oil.  
&emsp;My cup runs over.  
<sup>6</sup> Surely goodness and loving kindness shall follow me all the days of my life,  
&emsp;and I will dwell in the LORD’s house forever.

## Psalm 24

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> The earth is the LORD’s, with its fullness;  
&emsp;the world, and those who dwell in it.  
<sup>2</sup> For he has founded it on the seas,  
&emsp;and established it on the floods.

<sup>3</sup> Who may ascend to the LORD’s hill?  
&emsp;Who may stand in his holy place?  
<sup>4</sup> He who has clean hands and a pure heart;  
&emsp;who has not lifted up his soul to falsehood,  
&emsp;and has not sworn deceitfully.  
<sup>5</sup> He shall receive a blessing from the LORD,  
&emsp;righteousness from the God of his salvation.  
<sup>6</sup> This is the generation of those who seek Him,  
&emsp;who seek your face—even Jacob. *Selah.*

<sup>7</sup> Lift up your heads, you gates!  
&emsp;Be lifted up, you everlasting doors,  
&emsp;and the King of glory will come in.  
<sup>8</sup> Who is the King of glory?  
&emsp;The LORD strong and mighty,  
&emsp;the LORD mighty in battle.  
<sup>9</sup> Lift up your heads, you gates;  
&emsp;yes, lift them up, you everlasting doors,  
&emsp;and the King of glory will come in.  
<sup>10</sup> Who is this King of glory?  
&emsp;The LORD of Armies is the King of glory! *Selah.*

## Psalm 25

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> To you, LORD, I lift up my soul.  
<sup>2</sup> My God, I have trusted in you.  
&emsp;Don’t let me be shamed.  
&emsp;Don’t let my enemies triumph over me.  
<sup>3</sup> Yes, no one who waits for you will be shamed.  
&emsp;They will be shamed who deal treacherously without cause.

<sup>4</sup> Show me your ways, LORD.  
&emsp;Teach me your paths.  
<sup>5</sup> Guide me in your truth, and teach me,  
&emsp;for you are the God of my salvation.  
&emsp;I wait for you all day long.  
<sup>6</sup> LORD, remember your tender mercies and your loving kindness,  
&emsp;for they are from old times.  
<sup>7</sup> Don’t remember the sins of my youth, nor my transgressions.  
&emsp;Remember me according to your loving kindness,  
&emsp;for your goodness’ sake, LORD.  
<sup>8</sup> Good and upright is the LORD,  
&emsp;therefore he will instruct sinners in the way.  
<sup>9</sup> He will guide the humble in justice.  
&emsp;He will teach the humble his way.  
<sup>10</sup> All the paths of the LORD are loving kindness and truth  
&emsp;to such as keep his covenant and his testimonies.  
<sup>11</sup> For your name’s sake, LORD,  
&emsp;pardon my iniquity, for it is great.  
<sup>12</sup> What man is he who fears the LORD?  
&emsp;He shall instruct him in the way that he shall choose.  
<sup>13</sup> His soul will dwell at ease.  
&emsp;His offspring will inherit the land.  
<sup>14</sup> The friendship of the LORD is with those who fear him.  
&emsp;He will show them his covenant.

<sup>15</sup> My eyes are ever on the LORD,  
&emsp;for he will pluck my feet out of the net.  
<sup>16</sup> Turn to me, and have mercy on me,  
&emsp;for I am desolate and afflicted.  
<sup>17</sup> The troubles of my heart are enlarged.  
&emsp;Oh bring me out of my distresses.  
<sup>18</sup> Consider my ~~affliction~~ <ins>lowliness</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c91"></a>[c91](#c91)</sup> and my travail.  
&emsp;Forgive all my sins.  
<sup>19</sup> Consider my enemies, for they are many.  
&emsp;They hate me with cruel hatred.  
<sup>20</sup> Oh keep my soul, and deliver me.  
&emsp;Let me not be disappointed, for I take refuge in you.  
<sup>21</sup> Let integrity and uprightness preserve me,  
&emsp;for I wait for you.  
<sup>22</sup> God, redeem Israel  
&emsp;out of all his troubles.

## Psalm 26

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> Judge me, LORD, for I have walked in my integrity.  
&emsp;I have trusted also in the LORD without wavering.  
<sup>2</sup> Examine me, LORD, and prove me.  
&emsp;Try my heart and my mind.  
<sup>3</sup> For your loving kindness is before my eyes.  
&emsp;I have walked in your truth.  
<sup>4</sup> I have not sat with ~~deceitful men~~ <ins>worthless men</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c93"></a>[c93](#c93)</sup>,  
&emsp;neither will I go in with ~~hypocrites~~ <ins>those who act in secret</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c92"></a>[c92](#c92)</sup>.  
<sup>5</sup> I hate the assembly of evildoers,  
&emsp;and will not sit with the wicked.  
<sup>6</sup> I will wash my hands in innocence,  
&emsp;so I will go about your altar, LORD,  
&emsp;<sup>7</sup> that I may make the voice of thanksgiving to be heard  
&emsp;and tell of all your wondrous deeds.  
<sup>8</sup> LORD, I love the habitation of your house,  
&emsp;the place where your glory dwells.  
<sup>9</sup> Don’t gather my soul with sinners,  
&emsp;nor my life with bloodthirsty men  
&emsp;<sup>10</sup> in whose hands is wickedness;  
&emsp;their right hand is full of bribes.

<sup>11</sup> But as for me, I will walk in my integrity.  
&emsp;Redeem me, and be merciful to me.  
<sup>12</sup> My foot stands in an even place.  
&emsp;In the congregations I will bless the LORD.

## Psalm 27

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> The LORD is my light and my salvation.  
&emsp;Whom shall I fear?  
The LORD is the strength of my life.  
&emsp;Of whom shall I be afraid?  
<sup>2</sup> When evildoers came at me to eat up my flesh,  
&emsp;even my adversaries and my foes, they stumbled and fell.  
<sup>3</sup> Though an army should encamp against me,  
&emsp;my heart shall not fear.  
Though war should rise against me,  
&emsp;even then I will be confident.  
<sup>4</sup> One thing I have asked of the LORD, that I will seek after:  
&emsp;that I may dwell in the LORD’s house all the days of my life,  
&emsp;to see the LORD’s ~~beauty~~ <ins>delight</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c94"></a>[c94](#c94)</sup>,  
&emsp;and to ~~inquire in his temple~~ <ins>visit his temple</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c95"></a>[c95](#c95)</sup>.  
<sup>5</sup> For in the day of trouble, he will keep me secretly in his pavilion.  
&emsp;In the secret place of his tabernacle, he will hide me.  
&emsp;He will lift me up on a rock.  
<sup>6</sup> Now my head will be lifted up above my enemies around me.  
I will offer sacrifices of joy in his tent.  
&emsp;I will sing, yes, ~~I will sing praises to the LORD~~ <ins>I will say a psalm to the LORD</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c96"></a>[c96](#c96)</sup>.

<sup>7</sup> Hear, LORD, when I cry with my voice.  
&emsp;Have mercy also on me, and answer me.  
<sup>8</sup> ~~When you said, “Seek my face,”~~ <ins>Of you my heart has spoken: “Seek my face!”</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c97"></a>[c97](#c97)</sup>  
&emsp;~~my heart said to you, “I will seek your face, LORD.”~~ <ins>Your face, LORD, I will seek.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c98"></a>[c98](#c98)</sup>  
<sup>9</sup> Don’t hide your face from me.  
&emsp;Don’t put your servant away in anger.  
You have been my help.  
&emsp;Don’t abandon me,  
&emsp;neither forsake me, God of my salvation.  
<sup>10</sup> When my father and my mother forsake me,  
&emsp;then the LORD will take me up.  
<sup>11</sup> Teach me your way, LORD.  
&emsp;Lead me in a straight path, because of my enemies.  
<sup>12</sup> Don’t deliver me over to the desire of my adversaries,  
&emsp;for false witnesses have risen up against me,  
&emsp;such as breathe out cruelty.  
<sup>13</sup> I am still confident of this:  
&emsp;I will see the goodness of the LORD in the land of the living.  
<sup>14</sup> Wait for the LORD.  
&emsp;Be ~~strong~~ <ins>brave</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c99"></a>[c99](#c99)</sup>, and let your heart ~~take courage~~ <ins>be strengthened</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c100"></a>[c100](#c100)</sup>.  
Yes, wait for the LORD.

## Psalm 28

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> To you, LORD, I call.  
&emsp;My ~~rock~~ <ins>God</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c101"></a>[c101](#c101)</sup>, don’t be ~~deaf~~ <ins>silent</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c102"></a>[c102](#c102)</sup> to me,  
&emsp;lest, if you are silent to me,  
&emsp;I would become like those who go down into the pit.  
<sup>2</sup> Hear the voice of my petitions, when I cry to you,  
&emsp;when I lift up my hands toward your Most Holy Place.  
<sup>3</sup> Don’t draw me away with the wicked,  
&emsp;with the workers of iniquity who speak peace with their neighbors,  
&emsp;but mischief is in their hearts.  
<sup>4</sup> Give them according to their work, and according to the wickedness of their doings.  
&emsp;Give them according to the operation of their hands.  
&emsp;Bring back on them what they deserve.  
<sup>5</sup> Because they don’t respect the works of the LORD,  
&emsp;nor the operation of his hands,  
&emsp;he will break them down and not build them up.

<sup>6</sup> Blessed be the LORD,  
&emsp;because he has heard the voice of my petitions.  
<sup>7</sup> The LORD is my ~~strength~~ <ins>helper</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c103"></a>[c103](#c103)</sup> and my shield.  
&emsp;My heart has trusted in him, and I am helped.  
Therefore my heart greatly rejoices.  
&emsp;With my song I will thank him.  
<sup>8</sup> The LORD is their strength.  
&emsp;He is a stronghold of salvation to his anointed.  
<sup>9</sup> Save your people,  
&emsp;and bless your inheritance.  
Be their shepherd also,  
&emsp;and bear them up forever.

## Psalm 29

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> Ascribe to the LORD, you sons of the mighty,  
&emsp;ascribe to the LORD glory and strength.  
<sup>2</sup> Ascribe to the LORD the glory due to his name.  
&emsp;Worship the LORD in holy array.

<sup>3</sup> The LORD’s voice is on the waters.  
&emsp;The God of glory thunders, even the LORD on many waters.  
<sup>4</sup> The LORD’s voice is powerful.  
&emsp;The LORD’s voice is full of majesty.  
<sup>5</sup> The LORD’s voice breaks the cedars.  
&emsp;Yes, the LORD breaks in pieces the cedars of Lebanon.  
<sup>6</sup> He makes them also to skip like a calf;  
&emsp;Lebanon and Sirion like ~~a young, wild ox~~ <ins>the son of unicorns</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c104"></a>[c104](#c104)</sup>.  
<sup>7</sup> The LORD’s voice ~~strikes with~~ <ins>cuts through</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c105"></a>[c105](#c105)</sup> flashes of lightning.  
&emsp;<sup>8</sup> The LORD’s voice shakes the wilderness.  
&emsp;The LORD shakes the wilderness of Kadesh.  
<sup>9</sup> The LORD’s voice makes the deer calve,  
&emsp;and strips the forests bare.  
&emsp;In his temple everything says, “Glory!”

<sup>10</sup> The LORD sat enthroned at the Flood.  
&emsp;Yes, the LORD sits as King forever.  
<sup>11</sup> The LORD will give strength to his people.  
&emsp;The LORD will bless his people with peace.

## Psalm 30

*A Psalm. A Song for the Dedication of the Temple. By David.*

<sup>1</sup> I will extol you, LORD, for you have raised me up,  
&emsp;and have not made my foes to rejoice over me.  
<sup>2</sup> LORD my God, I cried to you,  
&emsp;and you have healed me.  
<sup>3</sup> LORD, you have brought up my soul from Sheol.[^14]  
&emsp;You have kept me alive, that I should not go down to the pit.  
<sup>4</sup> Sing praise to the LORD, you saints of his.  
&emsp;Give thanks to his holy name.  
<sup>5</sup> For his anger is but for a moment.  
&emsp;His favor is for a lifetime.  
Weeping may stay for the night,  
&emsp;but joy comes in the morning.  
<sup>6</sup> As for me, I said in my prosperity,  
&emsp;“I shall never be moved.”  
<sup>7</sup> You, LORD, when you favored me, ~~made my mountain stand strong~~ <ins>granted strength to my splendor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c106"></a>[c106](#c106)</sup>;  
&emsp;but when you hid your face, I was troubled.  
<sup>8</sup> I cried to you, LORD.  
&emsp;I made supplication to the Lord:  
<sup>9</sup> “What profit is there in my destruction, if I go down to the pit?  
&emsp;Shall the dust praise you?  
&emsp;Shall it declare your truth?  
<sup>10</sup> Hear, LORD, and have mercy on me.  
&emsp;LORD, be my helper.”  
<sup>11</sup> You have turned my mourning into dancing for me.  
&emsp;You have removed my sackcloth, and clothed me with gladness,  
&emsp;<sup>12</sup> to the end that my heart may sing praise to you, and not be silent.  
LORD my God, I will give thanks to you forever!

## Psalm 31

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> In you, LORD, I take refuge.  
&emsp;Let me never be disappointed.  
&emsp;Deliver me in your righteousness.  
<sup>2</sup> Bow down your ear to me.  
&emsp;Deliver me speedily.  
Be to me a strong rock,  
&emsp;a house of defense to save me.  
<sup>3</sup> For you are my ~~rock and my fortress~~ <ins>strength and my refuge</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c107"></a>[c107](#c107)</sup>,  
&emsp;~~therefore for your name’s sake lead me and guide me.~~ <ins>therefore for your name’s sake you will lead me and pasture me.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c108"></a>[c108](#c108)</sup>  
<sup>4</sup> Pluck me out of the net that they have laid secretly for me,  
&emsp;for you are ~~my stronghold~~ <ins>my strength</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c109"></a>[c109](#c109)</sup>.  
<sup>5</sup> Into your hand I commend my spirit.  
&emsp;You redeem me, LORD, God of truth.  
<sup>6</sup> I hate those who regard lying vanities,  
&emsp;but I trust in the LORD.  
<sup>7</sup> I will be glad and rejoice in your loving kindness,  
&emsp;for you have seen my ~~affliction~~ <ins>humiliation</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c110"></a>[c110](#c110)</sup>.  
&emsp;You have known my soul in adversities.  
<sup>8</sup> You have not shut me up into the hand of the enemy.  
&emsp;You have set my feet in a large place.  
<sup>9</sup> Have mercy on me, LORD, for I am in distress.  
&emsp;My eye, my soul, and my body waste away with grief.  
<sup>10</sup> For my life is spent with sorrow,  
&emsp;my years with sighing.  
My strength fails because of my iniquity.  
&emsp;My bones are wasted away.  
<sup>11</sup> ~~Because of all my adversaries~~ <ins>Among all my adversaries</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c112"></a>[c112](#c112)</sup> I have become utterly contemptible to my neighbors,  
&emsp;a horror to my acquaintances.  
&emsp;Those who saw me ~~on the street~~ <ins>outdoors</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c111"></a>[c111](#c111)</sup> fled from me.  
<sup>12</sup> I am forgotten from their hearts like a dead man.  
&emsp;I am like broken pottery.  
<sup>13</sup> For I have heard the slander of many, terror on every side,  
&emsp;while they ~~conspire~~ <ins>gather</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c113"></a>[c113](#c113)</sup> together against me,  
&emsp;they plot to take away my life.  
<sup>14</sup> But I trust in you, LORD.  
&emsp;I said, “You are my God.”  
<sup>15</sup> My times are in your hand.  
&emsp;Deliver me from the hand of my enemies, and from those who persecute me.  
<sup>16</sup> Make your face to shine on your servant.  
&emsp;Save me in your loving kindness.  
<sup>17</sup> Let me not be disappointed, LORD, for I have called on you.  
&emsp;Let the wicked be disappointed.  
&emsp;Let them be silent in Sheol.[^15]  
<sup>18</sup> Let the lying lips be mute,  
&emsp;which speak against the righteous insolently, with pride and contempt.  
<sup>19</sup> Oh how great is your goodness,  
&emsp;which you have laid up for those who fear you,  
&emsp;which you have worked for those who take refuge in you,  
&emsp;before the sons of men!  
<sup>20</sup> In the shelter of your presence you will hide them from ~~the plotting of man~~ <ins>the turmoil of man</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c114"></a>[c114](#c114)</sup>.  
&emsp;You will keep them secretly in ~~a dwelling~~ <ins>a tent</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c115"></a>[c115](#c115)</sup> away from the strife of tongues.  
<sup>21</sup> Praise be to the LORD,  
&emsp;for he has shown me his marvelous loving kindness in a strong city.  
<sup>22</sup> As for me, I said in my haste, “I am cut off from before your eyes.”  
&emsp;Nevertheless you heard the voice of my petitions when I cried to you.  
<sup>23</sup> Oh love the LORD, all you his saints!  
&emsp;The LORD preserves the faithful,  
and fully recompenses him who behaves arrogantly.  
<sup>24</sup> Be strong, and let your heart take courage,  
&emsp;all you who hope in the LORD.

## Psalm 32

*By David. A contemplative psalm.*

<sup>1</sup> Blessed is he whose disobedience is forgiven,  
&emsp;whose sin is covered.  
<sup>2</sup> Blessed is the man to whom the LORD doesn’t impute iniquity,  
&emsp;in whose spirit there is no deceit.  
<sup>3</sup> When I kept silence, my bones wasted away through my groaning all day long.  
<sup>4</sup> For day and night your hand was heavy on me.  
&emsp;My strength was sapped in the heat of summer. *Selah.*  
<sup>5</sup> I acknowledged my sin to you.  
&emsp;I didn’t hide my iniquity.  
I said, I will confess my transgressions to the LORD,  
&emsp;and you forgave the iniquity of my sin. *Selah.*  
<sup>6</sup> For this, let everyone who is godly pray to you in a time when you may be found.  
&emsp;Surely when the great waters overflow, they shall not reach to him.  
<sup>7</sup> You are my hiding place.  
&emsp;You will preserve me from trouble.  
&emsp;You will surround me with songs of deliverance. *Selah.*  
<sup>8</sup> I will instruct you and teach you in the way which you shall go.  
&emsp;I will ~~counsel you with~~ <ins>fix</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c116"></a>[c116](#c116)</sup> my eye on you.  
<sup>9</sup> Don’t be like the horse, or like the mule, which have no understanding,  
&emsp;who are controlled by bit and bridle, or else they will not come near to you.  
<sup>10</sup> Many sorrows come to the wicked,  
&emsp;but loving kindness shall surround him who trusts in the LORD.  
<sup>11</sup> Be glad in the LORD, and rejoice, you righteous!  
&emsp;~~Shout for joy~~ <ins>Boast</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c117"></a>[c117](#c117)</sup>, all you who are upright in heart!

## Psalm 33

<sup>1</sup> Rejoice in the LORD, you righteous!  
&emsp;Praise is fitting for the upright.  
<sup>2</sup> Give thanks to the LORD with the lyre.  
&emsp;Sing praises to him with the harp of ten strings.  
<sup>3</sup> Sing to him a new song.  
&emsp;Play skillfully with a shout of joy!  
<sup>4</sup> For the LORD’s word is right.  
&emsp;All his work is done in faithfulness.  
<sup>5</sup> He loves righteousness and justice.  
&emsp;The earth is full of the loving kindness of the LORD.  
<sup>6</sup> By the LORD’s word, the heavens were made:  
&emsp;all their army by the breath of his mouth.  
<sup>7</sup> He gathers the waters of the sea together as ~~a heap~~ <ins>in a wineskin</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c118"></a>[c118](#c118)</sup>.  
&emsp;He lays up the deeps in storehouses.  
<sup>8</sup> Let all the earth fear the LORD.  
&emsp;Let all the inhabitants of the world stand in awe of him.  
<sup>9</sup> For he spoke, and it was done.  
&emsp;~~He commanded, and it stood firm.~~ <ins>He commanded, and all things were created.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c119"></a>[c119](#c119)</sup>  
<sup>10</sup> The LORD brings the counsel of the nations to nothing.  
&emsp;He makes the thoughts of the peoples to be of no effect.  
<sup>11</sup> The counsel of the LORD stands fast forever,  
&emsp;the thoughts of his heart to all generations.  
<sup>12</sup> Blessed is the nation whose God is the LORD,  
&emsp;the people whom he has chosen for his own inheritance.  
<sup>13</sup> The LORD looks from heaven.  
&emsp;He sees all the sons of men.  
<sup>14</sup> From the place of his habitation he looks out on all the inhabitants of the earth,  
&emsp;<sup>15</sup> he who fashions all of their hearts;  
&emsp;and he ~~considers~~ <ins>understands</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c120"></a>[c120](#c120)</sup> all of their works.  
<sup>16</sup> There is no king saved by ~~the multitude of an army~~ <ins>great might</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c121"></a>[c121](#c121)</sup>.  
&emsp;A mighty man is not delivered by great strength.  
<sup>17</sup> A horse is a vain thing for safety,  
&emsp;neither does he deliver any by his great power.  
<sup>18</sup> Behold, the LORD’s eye is on those who fear him,  
&emsp;on those who hope in his loving kindness,  
&emsp;<sup>19</sup> to deliver their soul from death,  
&emsp;to keep them alive in famine.  
<sup>20</sup> Our soul has waited for the LORD.  
&emsp;He is our help and our shield.  
<sup>21</sup> For our heart rejoices in him,  
&emsp;because we have trusted in his holy name.  
<sup>22</sup> Let your loving kindness be on us, LORD,  
&emsp;since we have hoped in you.

## Psalm 34

*By David; when he pretended to be insane before Abimelech, who drove him away, and he departed.*

<sup>1</sup> [^16]I will bless the LORD at all times.  
&emsp;His praise will always be in my mouth.  
<sup>2</sup> My soul shall boast in the LORD.  
&emsp;The humble shall hear of it and be glad.  
<sup>3</sup> Oh magnify the LORD with me.  
&emsp;Let’s exalt his name together.  
<sup>4</sup> I sought the LORD, and he answered me,  
&emsp;and delivered me from all my fears.  
<sup>5</sup> They looked to him, and were radiant.  
&emsp;Their faces shall never be covered with shame.  
<sup>6</sup> This poor man cried, and the LORD heard him,  
&emsp;and saved him out of all his troubles.  
<sup>7</sup> The LORD’s angel encamps around those who fear him,  
&emsp;and delivers them.  
<sup>8</sup> Oh taste and see that the LORD is good.  
&emsp;Blessed is the man who takes refuge in him.  
<sup>9</sup> Oh fear the LORD, you his saints,  
&emsp;for there is no lack with those who fear him.  
<sup>10</sup> The ~~young lions~~ <ins>rich</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c122"></a>[c122](#c122)</sup> do lack, and suffer hunger,  
&emsp;but those who seek the LORD shall not lack any good thing.

<sup>11</sup> Come, you children, listen to me.  
&emsp;I will teach you the fear of the LORD.  
<sup>12</sup> Who is someone who desires life,  
&emsp;and loves many days, that he may see good?  
<sup>13</sup> Keep your tongue from evil,  
&emsp;and your lips from speaking lies.  
<sup>14</sup> Depart from evil, and do good.  
&emsp;Seek peace, and pursue it.  
<sup>15</sup> The LORD’s eyes are toward the righteous.  
&emsp;His ears listen to their cry.  
<sup>16</sup> The LORD’s face is against those who do evil,  
&emsp;to cut off their memory from the earth.  
<sup>17</sup> The righteous cry, and the LORD hears,  
&emsp;and delivers them out of all their troubles.  
<sup>18</sup> The LORD is near to those who have a broken heart,  
&emsp;and saves those who have a crushed spirit.  
<sup>19</sup> Many are the afflictions of the righteous,  
&emsp;but the LORD delivers him out of them all.  
<sup>20</sup> He protects all of his bones.  
&emsp;Not one of them is broken.  
<sup>21</sup> Evil shall kill the wicked.  
&emsp;Those who hate the righteous shall be condemned.  
<sup>22</sup> The LORD redeems the soul of his servants.  
&emsp;None of those who take refuge in him shall be condemned.

## Psalm 35

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> ~~Contend, LORD, with those who contend with me.~~ <ins>Judge, LORD, those who judge me.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c123"></a>[c123](#c123)</sup>  
&emsp;Fight against those who fight against me.  
<sup>2</sup> Take hold of shield and buckler,  
&emsp;and stand up for my help.  
<sup>3</sup> Brandish the spear and block those who pursue me.  
&emsp;Tell my soul, “I am your salvation.”  
<sup>4</sup> Let those who seek after my soul be ~~disappointed~~ <ins>confounded</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c125"></a>[c125](#c125)</sup> and ~~brought to dishonor~~ <ins>put to shame</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c124"></a>[c124](#c124)</sup>.  
&emsp;Let those who plot my ruin be turned back and confounded.  
<sup>5</sup> Let them be as chaff before the wind,  
&emsp;the LORD’s angel driving them on.  
<sup>6</sup> Let their way be dark and slippery,  
&emsp;the LORD’s angel pursuing them.  
<sup>7</sup> For without cause they have hidden their net in a pit for me.  
&emsp;Without cause they have dug a pit for my soul.  
<sup>8</sup> Let destruction come on him unawares.  
&emsp;Let his net that he has hidden catch himself.  
&emsp;Let him fall into that destruction.

<sup>9</sup> My soul shall be joyful in the LORD.  
&emsp;It shall rejoice in his salvation.  
<sup>10</sup> All my bones shall say, “LORD, who is like you,  
&emsp;who delivers the ~~poor~~ <ins>helpless</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c126"></a>[c126](#c126)</sup> from him who is too strong for him;  
&emsp;yes, the poor and the needy from him who robs him?”  
<sup>11</sup> Unrighteous witnesses rise up.  
&emsp;They ask me about things that I don’t know about.  
<sup>12</sup> They reward me evil for good,  
&emsp;to the bereaving of my soul.

<sup>13</sup> But as for me, when they were sick, my clothing was sackcloth.  
&emsp;I ~~afflicted~~ <ins>humbled</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c127"></a>[c127](#c127)</sup> my soul with fasting.  
&emsp;My prayer returned into my own bosom.  
<sup>14</sup> I behaved myself as though it had been my friend or my brother.  
&emsp;I bowed down mourning, as one who mourns his mother.  
<sup>15</sup> But in my adversity, they rejoiced, and gathered themselves together.  
&emsp;The attackers gathered themselves together against me, and I didn’t know it.  
&emsp;They tore at me, and didn’t cease.  
<sup>16</sup> ~~Like the profane mockers in feasts,~~ <ins>They tore at me and did not desist; they tested me; they mocked me with scorn;</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c128"></a>[c128](#c128)</sup>  
&emsp;they gnashed their teeth at me.  
<sup>17</sup> Lord, how long will you look on?  
&emsp;Rescue my soul from their destruction,  
&emsp;my precious life from the lions.  
<sup>18</sup> I will give you thanks in the great assembly.  
&emsp;I will praise you among many people.  
<sup>19</sup> Don’t let those who are ~~my enemies wrongfully~~ <ins>my lying enemies</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c129"></a>[c129](#c129)</sup> rejoice over me;  
&emsp;neither let those who hate me without a cause wink their eyes.  
<sup>20</sup> For they don’t speak peace,  
&emsp;but they devise deceitful words against those who are quiet in the land.  
<sup>21</sup> Yes, they opened their mouth wide against me.  
&emsp;They said, “Aha! Aha! Our eye has seen it!”  
<sup>22</sup> You have seen it, LORD. Don’t keep silent.  
&emsp;Lord, don’t be far from me.  
<sup>23</sup> Wake up! Rise up to defend me, my God!  
&emsp;My Lord, contend for me!  
<sup>24</sup> Vindicate me, LORD my God, according to your righteousness.  
&emsp;Don’t let them gloat over me.  
<sup>25</sup> Don’t let them say in their heart, “Aha! That’s the way we want it!”  
&emsp;Don’t let them say, “We have swallowed him up!”  
<sup>26</sup> Let them be ~~disappointed~~ <ins>ashamed</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c130"></a>[c130](#c130)</sup> and confounded together who rejoice at my calamity.  
&emsp;Let them be clothed with shame and dishonor who ~~magnify themselves~~ <ins>speak great things</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c131"></a>[c131](#c131)</sup> against me.

<sup>27</sup> Let those who favor my righteous cause shout for joy and be glad.  
&emsp;Yes, let them say continually, “May the LORD be magnified,  
&emsp;who has pleasure in the prosperity of his servant!”  
<sup>28</sup> My tongue shall talk about your righteousness and about your praise all day long.

## Psalm 36

*For the Chief Musician. By David, the servant of the LORD.*

<sup>1</sup> ~~A revelation is within my heart about the disobedience of the wicked:~~ <ins>Sin whispers to the wicked within his heart:</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c132"></a>[c132](#c132)</sup>  
&emsp;There is no fear of God before his eyes.  
<sup>2</sup> For he flatters himself in his own eyes,  
&emsp;too much to detect and hate his sin.  
<sup>3</sup> The words of his mouth are iniquity and deceit.  
&emsp;He has ceased to ~~be wise and to do good~~ <ins>understand in order to do good</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c133"></a>[c133](#c133)</sup>.  
<sup>4</sup> He plots iniquity on his bed.  
&emsp;He sets himself in a way that is not good.  
&emsp;He doesn’t abhor evil.

<sup>5</sup> Your loving kindness, LORD, is in the heavens.  
&emsp;Your faithfulness reaches to the skies.  
<sup>6</sup> Your righteousness is like the mountains of God.  
&emsp;Your judgments are like a great deep.  
&emsp;LORD, you preserve man and animal.  
<sup>7</sup> How precious is your loving kindness, God!  
&emsp;The children of men take refuge under the shadow of your wings.  
<sup>8</sup> They shall be ~~abundantly satisfied with~~ <ins>intoxicated by</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c135"></a>[c135](#c135)</sup> the abundance of your house.  
&emsp;You will make them drink of the ~~river~~ <ins>torrent</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c134"></a>[c134](#c134)</sup> of your pleasures.  
<sup>9</sup> For with you is the spring of life.  
&emsp;In your light we will see light.  
<sup>10</sup> Oh continue your loving kindness to those who know you,  
&emsp;your righteousness to the upright in heart.  
<sup>11</sup> Don’t let the foot of pride come against me.  
&emsp;Don’t let the hand of the wicked ~~drive me away~~ <ins>move me</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c136"></a>[c136](#c136)</sup>.  
<sup>12</sup> There the workers of iniquity are fallen.  
&emsp;They are ~~thrust down~~ <ins>cast out</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c137"></a>[c137](#c137)</sup>, and shall not be able to rise.

## Psalm 37

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> Don’t ~~fret because of~~ <ins>be jealous of</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c138"></a>[c138](#c138)</sup> evildoers,  
&emsp;neither be envious against those who work unrighteousness.  
<sup>2</sup> For they shall soon be cut down like the grass,  
&emsp;and wither like the green herb.  
<sup>3</sup> Trust in the LORD, and do good.  
&emsp;Dwell in the land, and enjoy safe pasture.  
<sup>4</sup> Also delight yourself in the LORD,  
&emsp;and he will give you the ~~desires~~ <ins>requests</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c139"></a>[c139](#c139)</sup> of your heart.  
<sup>5</sup> Commit your way to the LORD.  
&emsp;Trust also in him, and he will do this:  
<sup>6</sup> he will make your righteousness shine out like light,  
&emsp;and your justice as the noon day sun.  
<sup>7</sup> Rest in the LORD, and wait patiently for him.  
&emsp;~~Don’t fret~~ <ins>Don’t be envious</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c140"></a>[c140](#c140)</sup> because of him who prospers in his way,  
&emsp;because of the man who ~~makes wicked plots happen~~ <ins>devises plots</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c141"></a>[c141](#c141)</sup>.  
<sup>8</sup> Cease from anger, and forsake wrath.  
&emsp;~~Don’t fret~~ <ins>Don’t be envious</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c142"></a>[c142](#c142)</sup>; it leads only to evildoing.  
<sup>9</sup> For evildoers shall be ~~cut off~~ <ins>destroyed</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c143"></a>[c143](#c143)</sup>,  
&emsp;but those who wait for the LORD shall inherit the land.  
<sup>10</sup> For yet a little while, and the wicked will be no more.  
&emsp;Yes, though you look for his place, ~~he isn’t there~~ <ins>you will not find it</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c144"></a>[c144](#c144)</sup>.  
<sup>11</sup> But the humble shall inherit the land,  
&emsp;and shall delight themselves in the abundance of peace.  
<sup>12</sup> The wicked plots against the just,  
&emsp;and gnashes at him with his teeth.  
<sup>13</sup> The Lord will laugh at him,  
&emsp;for he sees that his day is coming.  
<sup>14</sup> The wicked have drawn out the sword, and have bent their bow,  
&emsp;to cast down the poor and needy,  
&emsp;to kill those who are upright on the path.  
<sup>15</sup> Their sword shall enter into their own heart.  
&emsp;Their bows shall be broken.  
<sup>16</sup> Better is a little that the righteous has,  
&emsp;than the abundance of many wicked.  
<sup>17</sup> For the arms of the wicked shall be broken,  
&emsp;but the LORD upholds the righteous.  
<sup>18</sup> The LORD knows the days of the perfect.  
&emsp;Their inheritance shall be forever.  
<sup>19</sup> They shall not be disappointed in the time of evil.  
&emsp;In the days of famine they shall be satisfied.

<sup>20</sup> But the wicked shall perish.  
&emsp;The enemies of the LORD shall be like the beauty of the fields.  
&emsp;They will vanish—  
&emsp;vanish like smoke.  
<sup>21</sup> The wicked borrow, and don’t pay back,  
&emsp;but the righteous give generously.  
<sup>22</sup> For such as are blessed by him shall inherit the land.  
&emsp;Those who are cursed by him shall be cut off.  
<sup>23</sup> A man’s steps are established by the LORD.  
&emsp;He delights in his way.  
<sup>24</sup> Though he stumble, he shall not fall,  
&emsp;for the LORD holds him up with his hand.  
<sup>25</sup> I have been young, and now am old,  
&emsp;yet I have not seen the righteous forsaken,  
&emsp;nor his children begging for bread.  
<sup>26</sup> All day long he ~~deals graciously~~ <ins>is merciful</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c145"></a>[c145](#c145)</sup>, and lends.  
&emsp;His offspring is blessed.
