<sup>11</sup> They say, “How does God know?  
&emsp;Is there knowledge in the Most High?”  
<sup>12</sup> Behold, these are the wicked.  
&emsp;~~Being always at ease~~ <ins>Abounding in the world</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c267"></a>[c267](#c267)</sup>, they increase in riches.  
<sup>13</sup> Surely I have cleansed my heart in vain,  
&emsp;and washed my hands in innocence,  
<sup>14</sup> For all day long I have been plagued,  
&emsp;and punished every morning.  
<sup>15</sup> If I had said, “I will speak thus”,  
&emsp;behold, I would have betrayed the generation of your children.  
<sup>16</sup> When I tried to understand this,  
&emsp;it was too painful for me—  
<sup>17</sup> until I entered God’s sanctuary,  
&emsp;and considered their latter end.  
<sup>18</sup> Surely you set them in slippery places.  
&emsp;You throw them down to destruction.  
<sup>19</sup> How they are suddenly destroyed!  
&emsp;They are completely swept away with terrors.  
<sup>20</sup> As a dream when one wakes up,  
&emsp;so, Lord,[^30] when you awake, you will despise ~~their fantasies~~ <ins>their image</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c268"></a>[c268](#c268)</sup>.  
<sup>21</sup> For my soul was grieved.  
&emsp;I was embittered in my heart.  
<sup>22</sup> I was so senseless and ignorant.  
&emsp;I was a brute beast before you.  
<sup>23</sup> Nevertheless, I am continually with you.  
&emsp;You have held my right hand.  
<sup>24</sup> You will guide me with your counsel,  
&emsp;and afterward receive me to glory.  
<sup>25</sup> Whom do I have in heaven?  
&emsp;There is no one on earth whom I desire besides you.  
<sup>26</sup> My flesh and my heart fails,  
&emsp;but God is the strength of my heart and my portion forever.  
<sup>27</sup> For, behold, those who are far from you shall perish.  
&emsp;You have destroyed all those who are unfaithful to you.  
<sup>28</sup> But it is good for me to come close to God.  
&emsp;I have made the Lord GOD[^31] my refuge,  
&emsp;that I may tell of all your works.

## Psalm 74

*A contemplation by Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> God, why have you rejected us forever?  
&emsp;Why does your anger smolder against the sheep of your pasture?  
<sup>2</sup> Remember your congregation, which you purchased of old,  
&emsp;which you have redeemed to be the tribe of your inheritance:  
&emsp;Mount Zion, in which you have lived.  
<sup>3</sup> Lift up your feet to the perpetual ruins,  
&emsp;all the evil that the enemy has done in the sanctuary.  
<sup>4</sup> Your adversaries have roared in the middle of your assembly.  
&emsp;They have set up their standards as signs.  
<sup>5</sup> They behaved like men ~~wielding axes~~ <ins>brandishing axes on high</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c269"></a>[c269](#c269)</sup>,  
&emsp;cutting through a thicket of trees.  
<sup>6</sup> Now they break all its ~~carved work~~ <ins>doors</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c271"></a>[c271](#c271)</sup> down with hatchet and ~~hammers~~ <ins>axe</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c270"></a>[c270](#c270)</sup>.  
&emsp;<sup>7</sup> They have burned your sanctuary to the ground.  
&emsp;They have profaned the dwelling place of your Name.  
<sup>8</sup> They said in their heart, “We will crush them completely.”  
&emsp;They have burned up all the places in the land where God was worshiped.  
<sup>9</sup> We see no miraculous signs.  
&emsp;There is no longer any prophet,  
&emsp;neither is there among us anyone who knows how long.  
<sup>10</sup> How long, God, shall the adversary reproach?  
&emsp;Shall the enemy blaspheme your name forever?  
<sup>11</sup> Why do you draw back your hand, even your right hand?  
&emsp;Take it from your chest and consume them!

<sup>12</sup> Yet God is my King of old,  
&emsp;working salvation throughout the earth.  
<sup>13</sup> You divided the sea by your strength.  
&emsp;You broke the heads of the sea monsters in the waters.  
<sup>14</sup> You broke the heads of Leviathan in pieces.  
&emsp;You gave him as food ~~to people and desert creatures~~ <ins>to the monsters of the sea</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c272"></a>[c272](#c272)</sup>.  
<sup>15</sup> You opened up spring and stream.  
&emsp;You dried up mighty rivers.  
<sup>16</sup> The day is yours, the night is also yours.  
&emsp;You have prepared the light and the sun.  
<sup>17</sup> You have set all the boundaries of the earth.  
&emsp;You have made summer and winter.

<sup>18</sup> Remember this, that the enemy has mocked you, LORD.  
&emsp;Foolish people have blasphemed your name.  
<sup>19</sup> Don’t ~~deliver the soul of your dove to wild beasts~~ <ins>deliver to wild beasts the souls of those who confess you</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c273"></a>[c273](#c273)</sup>.  
&emsp;Don’t forget the life of your poor forever.  
<sup>20</sup> Honor your covenant,  
&emsp;for haunts of violence fill the dark places of the earth.  
<sup>21</sup> Don’t let the oppressed return ashamed.  
&emsp;Let the poor and needy praise your name.  
<sup>22</sup> Arise, God! Plead your own cause.  
&emsp;Remember how the foolish man mocks you all day.  
<sup>23</sup> Don’t forget the voice of your adversaries.  
&emsp;The tumult of those who rise up against you ascends continually.

## Psalm 75

*For the Chief Musician. To the tune of “Do Not Destroy.” A Psalm by Asaph. A song.*

<sup>1</sup> We give thanks to you, God.  
&emsp;We give thanks, ~~for your Name is near~~ <ins>and invoke your Name</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c274"></a>[c274](#c274)</sup>.  
&emsp;Men tell about your wondrous works.

<sup>2</sup> When I choose the appointed time,  
&emsp;I will judge blamelessly.  
<sup>3</sup> The earth and all its inhabitants ~~quake~~ <ins>dissolve</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c275"></a>[c275](#c275)</sup>.  
&emsp;I firmly hold its pillars. *Selah.*  
<sup>4</sup> I said to the arrogant, “Don’t boast!”  
&emsp;I said to the wicked, “Don’t lift up the horn.  
<sup>5</sup> Don’t lift up your horn on high.  
&emsp;Don’t speak ~~with a stiff neck~~ <ins>insolently against God</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c276"></a>[c276](#c276)</sup>.”  
<sup>6</sup> For neither from the east, nor from the west,  
&emsp;nor yet from the south, comes exaltation.  
<sup>7</sup> But God is the judge.  
&emsp;He puts down one, and lifts up another.  
<sup>8</sup> For in the LORD’s hand there is a cup,  
&emsp;full of ~~foaming~~ <ins>undiluted</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c277"></a>[c277](#c277)</sup> wine mixed with spices.  
He pours it out.  
&emsp;Indeed the wicked of the earth drink and drink it to its very dregs.

<sup>9</sup> But I will declare this forever:  
&emsp;I will sing praises to the God of Jacob.  
<sup>10</sup> I will cut off all the horns of the wicked,  
&emsp;but the horns of the righteous shall be lifted up.

## Psalm 76

*For the Chief Musician. On stringed instruments. A Psalm by Asaph. A song.*

<sup>1</sup> In Judah, God is known.  
&emsp;His name is great in Israel.  
<sup>2</sup> His tabernacle is also in Salem.  
&emsp;His dwelling place in Zion.  
<sup>3</sup> There he broke the flaming arrows of the bow,  
&emsp;the shield, and the sword, and the weapons of war. *Selah.*  
<sup>4</sup> Glorious are you, and excellent,  
&emsp;more than mountains of game.  
<sup>5</sup> Valiant men lie plundered,  
&emsp;they have slept their last sleep.  
&emsp;None of the men of war can lift their hands.  
<sup>6</sup> At your rebuke, God of Jacob,  
&emsp;both chariot and horse are cast into a dead sleep.  
<sup>7</sup> You, even you, are to be feared.  
&emsp;Who can stand in your sight when you are angry?  
<sup>8</sup> You pronounced judgment from heaven.  
&emsp;The earth feared, and was silent,  
&emsp;<sup>9</sup> when God arose to judgment,  
&emsp;to save all the afflicted ones of the earth. *Selah.*  
<sup>10</sup> Surely the wrath of man praises you.  
&emsp;The survivors of your wrath ~~are restrained~~ <ins>shall keep a festival for you</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c278"></a>[c278](#c278)</sup>.  
<sup>11</sup> Make vows to the LORD your God, and fulfill them!  
&emsp;Let all of his neighbors bring presents to him who is to be feared.  
<sup>12</sup> He will cut off the spirit of princes.  
&emsp;He is feared by the kings of the earth.

## Psalm 77

*For the Chief Musician. To Jeduthun. A Psalm by Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> My cry goes to God!  
&emsp;Indeed, I cry to God for help,  
&emsp;and for him to listen to me.  
<sup>2</sup> In the day of my trouble I sought the Lord.  
&emsp;My hand was stretched out in the night, and didn’t get tired.  
&emsp;My soul refused to be comforted.  
<sup>3</sup> I remember God, and I groan.  
&emsp;I complain, and my spirit is overwhelmed. *Selah.*

<sup>4</sup> You hold my eyelids open.  
&emsp;I am so troubled that I can’t speak.  
<sup>5</sup> I have considered the days of old,  
&emsp;the years of ancient times.  
<sup>6</sup> I remember my song in the night.  
&emsp;I consider in my own heart;  
&emsp;my spirit diligently inquires:  
<sup>7</sup> “Will the Lord reject us forever?  
&emsp;Will he be favorable no more?  
<sup>8</sup> Has his loving kindness vanished forever?  
&emsp;Does his promise fail for generations?  
<sup>9</sup> Has God forgotten to be gracious?  
&emsp;Has he, in anger, withheld his compassion?” *Selah.*  
<sup>10</sup> Then I thought, “I will appeal to this:  
&emsp;the years of the right hand of the Most High.”  
<sup>11</sup> I will remember the LORD’s deeds;  
&emsp;for I will remember your wonders of old.  
<sup>12</sup> I will also meditate on all your work,  
&emsp;and consider your doings.  
<sup>13</sup> Your way, God, is in the sanctuary.  
&emsp;What god is great like God?  
<sup>14</sup> You are the God who does wonders.  
&emsp;You have made your strength known among the peoples.  
<sup>15</sup> You have redeemed your people with your arm,  
&emsp;the sons of Jacob and Joseph. *Selah.*  
<sup>16</sup> The waters saw you, God.  
&emsp;The waters saw you, and they writhed.  
&emsp;The depths also convulsed.  
<sup>17</sup> The clouds poured out water.  
&emsp;The skies resounded with thunder.  
&emsp;Your arrows also flashed around.  
<sup>18</sup> The voice of your thunder was in the whirlwind.  
&emsp;The lightnings lit up the world.  
&emsp;The earth trembled and shook.  
<sup>19</sup> Your way was through the sea,  
&emsp;your paths through the great waters.  
&emsp;Your footsteps were not known.  
<sup>20</sup> You led your people like a flock,  
&emsp;by the hand of Moses and Aaron.

## Psalm 78

*A contemplation by Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> Hear my teaching, my people.  
&emsp;Turn your ears to the words of my mouth.  
<sup>2</sup> I will open my mouth in a parable.  
&emsp;I will utter ~~dark sayings~~ <ins>the secrets</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c279"></a>[c279](#c279)</sup> of old,  
<sup>3</sup> which we have heard and known,  
&emsp;and our fathers have told us.  
<sup>4</sup> We will not hide them from their children,  
&emsp;telling to the generation to come the praises of the LORD,  
&emsp;his strength, and his wondrous deeds that he has done.  
<sup>5</sup> For he established a covenant in Jacob,  
&emsp;and appointed a teaching in Israel,  
&emsp;which he commanded our fathers,  
&emsp;that they should make them known to their children;  
<sup>6</sup> that the generation to come might know, even the children who should be born;  
&emsp;who should arise and tell their children,  
<sup>7</sup> that they might set their hope in God,  
&emsp;and not forget God’s deeds,  
&emsp;but keep his commandments,  
<sup>8</sup> and might not be as their fathers—  
&emsp;a stubborn and rebellious generation,  
&emsp;a generation that didn’t make their hearts loyal,  
&emsp;whose spirit was not steadfast with God.  
<sup>9</sup> The children of Ephraim, being armed and carrying bows,  
&emsp;turned back in the day of battle.  
<sup>10</sup> They didn’t keep God’s covenant,  
&emsp;and refused to walk in his law.  
<sup>11</sup> They forgot his doings,  
&emsp;his wondrous deeds that he had shown them.  
<sup>12</sup> He did marvelous things in the sight of their fathers,  
&emsp;in the land of Egypt, in the field of Zoan.  
<sup>13</sup> He split the sea, and caused them to pass through.  
&emsp;He made the waters stand ~~as a heap~~ <ins>as in a water skin</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c280"></a>[c280](#c280)</sup>.  
<sup>14</sup> In the daytime he also led them with a cloud,  
&emsp;and all night with a light of fire.  
<sup>15</sup> He split rocks in the wilderness,  
&emsp;and gave them drink abundantly as out of the depths.  
<sup>16</sup> He brought streams also out of the rock,  
&emsp;and caused waters to run down like rivers.  
<sup>17</sup> Yet they still went on to sin against him,  
&emsp;~~to rebel against~~ <ins>to provoke</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c281"></a>[c281](#c281)</sup> the Most High in the desert.  
<sup>18</sup> They tempted God in their heart  
&emsp;by asking food according to their desire.  
<sup>19</sup> Yes, they spoke against God.  
&emsp;They said, “Can God prepare a table in the wilderness?  
<sup>20</sup> Behold, he struck the rock, so that waters gushed out,  
&emsp;and streams overflowed.  
Can he give bread also?  
&emsp;Will he provide meat for his people?”  
<sup>21</sup> Therefore the LORD heard, and was angry.  
&emsp;A fire was kindled against Jacob,  
&emsp;anger also went up against Israel,  
<sup>22</sup> because they didn’t believe in God,  
&emsp;and didn’t trust in his salvation.  
<sup>23</sup> Yet he commanded the skies above,  
&emsp;and opened the doors of heaven.  
<sup>24</sup> He rained down manna on them to eat,  
&emsp;and gave them food from the sky.  
<sup>25</sup> Man ate the bread of angels.  
&emsp;He sent them food to the full.  
<sup>26</sup> He caused the east wind to blow in the sky.  
&emsp;By his power he guided the south wind.  
<sup>27</sup> He also rained meat on them as the dust,  
&emsp;winged birds as the sand of the seas.  
<sup>28</sup> He let them fall in the middle of their camp,  
&emsp;around their habitations.  
<sup>29</sup> So they ate, and were well filled.  
&emsp;He gave them their own desire.  
<sup>30</sup> They didn’t turn from their cravings.  
&emsp;Their food was yet in their mouths,  
&emsp;<sup>31</sup> when the anger of God went up against them,  
&emsp;killed some of their strongest,  
&emsp;and struck down the young men of Israel.  
<sup>32</sup> For all this they still sinned,  
&emsp;and didn’t believe in his wondrous works.  
<sup>33</sup> Therefore he consumed their days in vanity,  
&emsp;and their years in ~~terror~~ <ins>haste</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c282"></a>[c282](#c282)</sup>.  
<sup>34</sup> When he killed them, then they inquired after him.  
&emsp;They returned and sought God earnestly.  
<sup>35</sup> They remembered that God was their ~~rock~~ <ins>helper</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c283"></a>[c283](#c283)</sup>,  
&emsp;the Most High God, their redeemer.  
<sup>36</sup> But they ~~flattered~~ <ins>coaxed</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c284"></a>[c284](#c284)</sup> him with their mouth,  
&emsp;and lied to him with their tongue.  
<sup>37</sup> For their heart was not right with him,  
&emsp;neither were they faithful in his covenant.  
<sup>38</sup> But he, being merciful, forgave iniquity, and didn’t destroy them.  
&emsp;Yes, many times he turned his anger away,  
&emsp;and didn’t stir up all his wrath.  
<sup>39</sup> He remembered that they were but flesh,  
&emsp;a ~~wind~~ <ins>breath</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c285"></a>[c285](#c285)</sup> that passes away, and doesn’t come again.  
<sup>40</sup> How often they ~~rebelled against~~ <ins>provoked</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c286"></a>[c286](#c286)</sup> him in the wilderness,  
&emsp;and grieved him in the desert!  
<sup>41</sup> They turned again and tempted God,  
&emsp;and provoked the Holy One of Israel.  
<sup>42</sup> They didn’t remember his hand,  
&emsp;nor the day when he redeemed them from the adversary;  
<sup>43</sup> how he set his signs in Egypt,  
&emsp;his wonders in the field of Zoan,  
<sup>44</sup> he turned their rivers into blood,  
&emsp;and their streams, so that they could not drink.  
<sup>45</sup> He sent among them swarms of flies, which devoured them;  
&emsp;and frogs, which destroyed them.  
<sup>46</sup> He also gave their increase to the caterpillar,  
&emsp;and their labor to the locust.  
<sup>47</sup> He destroyed their vines with hail,  
&emsp;their sycamore fig trees with frost.  
<sup>48</sup> He also gave over their livestock to the hail,  
&emsp;and their flocks to hot thunderbolts.  
<sup>49</sup> He threw on them the fierceness of his anger,  
&emsp;wrath, indignation, and trouble,  
&emsp;and a band of angels of evil.  
<sup>50</sup> He made a path for his anger.  
&emsp;He didn’t spare their soul from death,  
&emsp;but gave their life over to the pestilence,  
<sup>51</sup> and struck all the firstborn in Egypt,  
&emsp;the chief of their strength in the tents of Ham.  
<sup>52</sup> But he led out his own people like sheep,  
&emsp;and guided them in the wilderness like a flock.  
<sup>53</sup> He led them ~~safely~~ <ins>in hope</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c287"></a>[c287](#c287)</sup>, so that they weren’t afraid,  
&emsp;but the sea overwhelmed their enemies.  
<sup>54</sup> He brought them to the border of his sanctuary,  
&emsp;to this mountain, which his right hand had taken.  
<sup>55</sup> He also drove out the nations before them,  
&emsp;allotted them for an inheritance by line,  
&emsp;and made the tribes of Israel to dwell in their tents.  
<sup>56</sup> Yet they tempted and ~~rebelled against~~ <ins>provoked</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c288"></a>[c288](#c288)</sup> the Most High God,  
&emsp;and didn’t keep his testimonies,  
<sup>57</sup> but turned back, and dealt treacherously like their fathers.  
&emsp;They were twisted like a deceitful bow.  
<sup>58</sup> For they provoked him to anger with their high places,  
&emsp;and moved him to jealousy with their engraved images.  
<sup>59</sup> When God heard this, he was angry,  
&emsp;and greatly abhorred Israel,  
<sup>60</sup> so that he abandoned ~~the tent of Shiloh~~ <ins>the dwelling of Shiloh</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c289"></a>[c289](#c289)</sup>,  
&emsp;the tent which he placed among men,  
<sup>61</sup> and delivered his strength into captivity,  
&emsp;his glory into the adversary’s hand.  
<sup>62</sup> He also gave his people over to the sword,  
&emsp;and was angry with his inheritance.  
<sup>63</sup> Fire devoured their young men.  
&emsp;Their virgins had no wedding song.  
<sup>64</sup> Their priests fell by the sword,  
&emsp;and their widows couldn’t weep.  
<sup>65</sup> Then the Lord awakened as one out of sleep,  
&emsp;like a mighty man who shouts by reason of wine.  
<sup>66</sup> He struck his adversaries ~~backward~~ <ins>in the rear</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c290"></a>[c290](#c290)</sup>.  
&emsp;He put them to a perpetual reproach.  
<sup>67</sup> Moreover he rejected the tent of Joseph,  
&emsp;and didn’t choose the tribe of Ephraim,  
<sup>68</sup> But chose the tribe of Judah,  
&emsp;Mount Zion which he loved.  
<sup>69</sup> He built his sanctuary like the heights,  
&emsp;like the earth which he has established forever.  
<sup>70</sup> He also chose David his servant,  
&emsp;and took him from the sheepfolds;  
<sup>71</sup> from following the ewes that have their young,  
&emsp;he brought him to be the shepherd of Jacob, his people,  
&emsp;and Israel, his inheritance.  
<sup>72</sup> So he was their shepherd according to the integrity of his heart,  
&emsp;and guided them by the skillfulness of his hands.

## Psalm 79

*A Psalm by Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> God, the nations have come into your inheritance.  
&emsp;They have defiled your holy temple.  
&emsp;They have laid Jerusalem in heaps.  
<sup>2</sup> They have given the dead bodies of your servants to be food for the birds of the sky,  
&emsp;the flesh of your saints to the animals of the earth.  
<sup>3</sup> They have shed their blood like water around Jerusalem.  
&emsp;There was no one to bury them.  
<sup>4</sup> We have become a reproach to our neighbors,  
&emsp;a scoffing and derision to those who are around us.  
<sup>5</sup> How long, LORD?  
&emsp;Will you be angry forever?  
&emsp;Will your jealousy burn like fire?  
<sup>6</sup> Pour out your wrath on the nations that don’t know you,  
&emsp;on the kingdoms that don’t call on your name,  
<sup>7</sup> for they have devoured Jacob,  
&emsp;and destroyed his homeland.  
<sup>8</sup> Don’t hold the iniquities of our forefathers against us.  
&emsp;Let your tender mercies speedily meet us,  
&emsp;for we are in desperate need.  
<sup>9</sup> Help us, God of our salvation, for the glory of your name.  
&emsp;Deliver us, and forgive our sins, for your name’s sake.  
<sup>10</sup> Why should the nations say, “Where is their God?”  
&emsp;Let it be known among the nations, before our eyes,  
&emsp;that vengeance for your servants’ blood is being poured out.  
<sup>11</sup> Let the sighing of the prisoner come before you.  
&emsp;According to ~~the greatness of your power~~ <ins>the greatness of your arm</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c291"></a>[c291](#c291)</sup>, preserve those who are sentenced to death.  
<sup>12</sup> Pay back to our neighbors seven times into their bosom  
&emsp;their reproach with which they have reproached you, Lord.  
<sup>13</sup> So we, your people and sheep of your pasture,  
&emsp;will give you thanks forever.  
&emsp;We will praise you forever, to all generations.

## Psalm 80

*For the Chief Musician. To the tune of “The Lilies of the Covenant.” A Psalm by Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> Hear us, Shepherd of Israel,  
&emsp;you who lead Joseph like a flock,  
&emsp;you who sit above the cherubim, shine out.  
<sup>2</sup> Before Ephraim, Benjamin, and Manasseh, stir up your might!  
&emsp;Come to save us!  
<sup>3</sup> Turn us again, God.  
&emsp;Cause your face to shine,  
&emsp;and we will be saved.

<sup>4</sup> LORD God of Armies,  
&emsp;how long will you be angry against the prayer of your people?  
<sup>5</sup> You have fed them with the bread of tears,  
&emsp;and given them tears to drink in large measure.  
<sup>6</sup> You make us a source of contention to our neighbors.  
&emsp;Our enemies ~~laugh among themselves~~ <ins>mock us</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c292"></a>[c292](#c292)</sup>.  
<sup>7</sup> Turn us again, God of Armies.  
&emsp;Cause your face to shine,  
&emsp;and we will be saved.

<sup>8</sup> You brought a vine out of Egypt.  
&emsp;You drove out the nations, and planted it.  
<sup>9</sup> You cleared the ground for it.  
&emsp;It took deep root, and filled the land.  
<sup>10</sup> The mountains were covered with its shadow.  
&emsp;Its boughs were like God’s cedars.  
<sup>11</sup> It sent out its branches to the sea,  
&emsp;its shoots to the River.  
<sup>12</sup> Why have you broken down its walls,  
&emsp;so that all those who pass by the way pluck it?  
<sup>13</sup> The boar out of the wood ravages it.  
&emsp;The wild animals of the field feed on it.  
<sup>14</sup> Turn again, we beg you, God of Armies.  
&emsp;Look down from heaven, and see, and visit this vine,  
<sup>15</sup> ~~the stock which~~ <ins>and protect that which</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c293"></a>[c293](#c293)</sup> your right hand planted,  
&emsp;~~the branch that~~ <ins>and look upon the son of man whom</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c294"></a>[c294](#c294)</sup> you made strong for yourself.  
<sup>16</sup> It’s burned with fire.  
&emsp;It’s cut down.  
&emsp;They perish at your rebuke.  
<sup>17</sup> Let your hand be on the man of your right hand,  
&emsp;on the son of man whom you made strong for yourself.  
<sup>18</sup> So we will not turn away from you.  
&emsp;Revive us, and we will call on your name.  
<sup>19</sup> Turn us again, LORD God of Armies.  
&emsp;Cause your face to shine, and we will be saved.

## Psalm 81

*For the Chief Musician. On an instrument of Gath. By Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> ~~Sing aloud to God~~ <ins>Exult in God</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c296"></a>[c296](#c296)</sup>, our ~~strength~~ <ins>helper</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c295"></a>[c295](#c295)</sup>!  
&emsp;Make a joyful shout to the God of Jacob!  
<sup>2</sup> ~~Raise a song~~ <ins>Take up a psalm</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c297"></a>[c297](#c297)</sup>, and bring here the tambourine,  
&emsp;the pleasant lyre with the harp.  
<sup>3</sup> Blow the trumpet at the New Moon,  
&emsp;at the full moon, on our feast day.  
<sup>4</sup> For it is a statute for Israel,  
&emsp;an ordinance of the God of Jacob.  
<sup>5</sup> He appointed it in Joseph for ~~a covenant~~ <ins>a testimony</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c299"></a>[c299](#c299)</sup>,  
&emsp;when he went out ~~over~~ <ins>from</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c298"></a>[c298](#c298)</sup> the land of Egypt,  
&emsp;I heard a language that I didn’t know.  
<sup>6</sup> “I removed his shoulder from the burden.  
&emsp;His hands were freed from the basket.  
<sup>7</sup> You called in trouble, and I delivered you.  
&emsp;I answered you in ~~the secret place of thunder~~ <ins>the hiding place of the storm</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c300"></a>[c300](#c300)</sup>.  
&emsp;I tested you at the waters of Meribah.” *Selah.*

<sup>8</sup> “Hear, my people, and ~~I will testify to you~~ <ins>I will solemnly warn you</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c301"></a>[c301](#c301)</sup>,  
&emsp;Israel, if you would listen to me!  
<sup>9</sup> There shall be no strange god in you,  
&emsp;neither shall you worship any foreign god.  
<sup>10</sup> I am the LORD, your God,  
&emsp;who brought you up out of the land of Egypt.  
&emsp;Open your mouth wide, and I will fill it.  
<sup>11</sup> But my people didn’t listen to my voice.  
&emsp;Israel desired none of me.  
<sup>12</sup> So I let them go after the stubbornness of their hearts,  
&emsp;that they might walk in their own counsels.  
<sup>13</sup> Oh that my people would listen to me,  
&emsp;that Israel would walk in my ways!  
<sup>14</sup> I would soon ~~subdue~~ <ins>humble</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c302"></a>[c302](#c302)</sup> their enemies,  
&emsp;and turn my hand against their adversaries.  
<sup>15</sup> The haters of the LORD would cringe before him,  
&emsp;and their punishment would last forever.  
<sup>16</sup> But he would have also fed them with the finest of the wheat.  
&emsp;I will satisfy you with honey out of the rock.”

## Psalm 82

*A Psalm by Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> God presides in the great assembly.  
&emsp;He judges among the gods.  
<sup>2</sup> “How long will you judge unjustly,  
&emsp;and show partiality to the wicked?” *Selah.*

<sup>3</sup> “Defend the weak, the poor, and the fatherless.  
&emsp;Maintain the rights of the poor and oppressed.  
<sup>4</sup> Rescue the ~~weak~~ <ins>poor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c303"></a>[c303](#c303)</sup> and needy.  
&emsp;Deliver them out of the hand of the wicked.”  
<sup>5</sup> They don’t know, neither do they understand.  
&emsp;They walk back and forth in darkness.  
&emsp;All the foundations of the earth are shaken.  
<sup>6</sup> I said, “You are gods,  
&emsp;all of you are sons of the Most High.  
<sup>7</sup> Nevertheless you shall die like men,  
&emsp;and fall like one of the rulers.”  
<sup>8</sup> Arise, God, judge the earth,  
&emsp;for you inherit all of the nations.

## Psalm 83

*A song. A Psalm by Asaph.*

<sup>1</sup> God, don’t keep silent.  
&emsp;Don’t keep silent,  
&emsp;and don’t be still, God.  
<sup>2</sup> For, behold, your enemies are stirred up.  
&emsp;Those who hate you have lifted up their heads.  
<sup>3</sup> They conspire with cunning against your people.  
&emsp;They plot against your ~~cherished~~ <ins>hidden</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c304"></a>[c304](#c304)</sup> ones.  
<sup>4</sup> “Come,” they say, “let’s destroy them as a nation,  
&emsp;that the name of Israel may be remembered no more.”  
<sup>5</sup> For they have conspired together with one mind.  
&emsp;They form an alliance against you.  
<sup>6</sup> The tents of Edom and the Ishmaelites;  
&emsp;Moab, and the Hagrites;  
<sup>7</sup> Gebal, Ammon, and Amalek;  
&emsp;Philistia with the inhabitants of Tyre;  
<sup>8</sup> Assyria also is joined with them.  
&emsp;They have helped the children of Lot. *Selah.*  
<sup>9</sup> Do to them as you did to Midian,  
&emsp;as to Sisera, as to Jabin, at the river Kishon;  
<sup>10</sup> who perished at Endor,  
&emsp;who became as dung for the earth.  
<sup>11</sup> Make their nobles like Oreb and Zeeb,  
&emsp;yes, all their princes like Zebah and Zalmunna,  
&emsp;<sup>12</sup> who said, “Let’s take possession of God’s pasture lands.”  
<sup>13</sup> My God, make them ~~like tumbleweed~~ <ins>like a wheel</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c305"></a>[c305](#c305)</sup>,  
&emsp;like chaff before the wind.  
<sup>14</sup> As the fire that burns the forest,  
&emsp;as the flame that sets the mountains on fire,  
&emsp;<sup>15</sup> so pursue them with your tempest,  
&emsp;and terrify them with your storm.  
<sup>16</sup> Fill their faces with confusion,  
&emsp;that they may seek your name, LORD.  
<sup>17</sup> Let them be disappointed and dismayed forever.  
&emsp;Yes, let them be confounded and perish;  
<sup>18</sup> that they may know that you alone, whose name is the LORD,[^32]  
&emsp;are the Most High over all the earth.

## Psalm 84

*For the Chief Musician. On an instrument of Gath. A Psalm by the sons of Korah.*

<sup>1</sup> How lovely are your dwellings,  
&emsp;LORD of Armies!  
<sup>2</sup> My soul longs, and even faints for the courts of the LORD.  
&emsp;My heart and my flesh cry out for the living God.  
<sup>3</sup> Yes, the sparrow has found a home,  
&emsp;and the swallow a nest for herself, where she may have her young,  
&emsp;near your altars, LORD of Armies, my King, and my God.  
<sup>4</sup> Blessed are those who dwell in your house.  
&emsp;They are always praising you. *Selah.*  
<sup>5</sup> Blessed are those whose ~~strength is in you~~ <ins>help is from you</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c306"></a>[c306](#c306)</sup>,  
&emsp;who have set their hearts on a pilgrimage.  
<sup>6</sup> Passing through ~~the valley of Weeping~~ <ins>the thirsty valley</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c307"></a>[c307](#c307)</sup>, they make it a place of springs.  
&emsp;Yes, the autumn rain covers it with blessings.  
<sup>7</sup> They go from strength to strength.  
&emsp;~~Every one of them appears before God in Zion~~ <ins>The God of gods will be seen in Zion</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c308"></a>[c308](#c308)</sup>.  
<sup>8</sup> LORD, God of Armies, hear my prayer.  
&emsp;Listen, God of Jacob. *Selah.*  
<sup>9</sup> Behold, God our shield,  
&emsp;look at the face of your anointed.  
<sup>10</sup> For a day in your courts is better than a thousand.  
&emsp;I would rather ~~be a doorkeeper~~ <ins>be at the threshold</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c309"></a>[c309](#c309)</sup> in the house of my God,  
&emsp;than to dwell in the tents of wickedness.  
<sup>11</sup> For the LORD God is a sun and a shield.  
&emsp;The LORD will give grace and glory.  
&emsp;He withholds no good thing from those who walk blamelessly.  
<sup>12</sup> LORD of Armies,  
&emsp;blessed is the man who trusts in you.

## Psalm 85

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by the sons of Korah.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, you have been favorable to your land.  
&emsp;You have restored the fortunes of Jacob.  
<sup>2</sup> You have forgiven the iniquity of your people.  
&emsp;You have covered all their sin. *Selah.*  
<sup>3</sup> You have taken away all your wrath.  
&emsp;You have turned from the fierceness of your anger.  
<sup>4</sup> Turn us, God of our salvation,  
&emsp;and cause your indignation toward us to cease.  
<sup>5</sup> Will you be angry with us forever?  
&emsp;Will you draw out your anger to all generations?  
<sup>6</sup> Won’t you revive us again,  
&emsp;that your people may rejoice in you?  
<sup>7</sup> Show us your loving kindness, LORD.  
&emsp;Grant us your salvation.  
<sup>8</sup> I will hear what God, the LORD, will speak,  
&emsp;for he will speak peace to his people, his saints;  
&emsp;~~but let them not turn again to folly.~~ <ins>and to those who are converted in heart.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c310"></a>[c310](#c310)</sup>  
<sup>9</sup> Surely his salvation is near those who fear him,  
&emsp;that glory may dwell in our land.  
<sup>10</sup> Mercy and truth meet together.  
&emsp;Righteousness and peace have kissed each other.  
<sup>11</sup> Truth springs out of the earth.  
&emsp;Righteousness has looked down from heaven.  
<sup>12</sup> Yes, the LORD will give that which is good.  
&emsp;Our land will yield its increase.  
<sup>13</sup> Righteousness goes before him,  
&emsp;and prepares the way for his steps.

## Psalm 86

*A Prayer by David.*

<sup>1</sup> ~~Hear, LORD, and answer me~~ <ins>Incline your ear, LORD, and hear me</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c311"></a>[c311](#c311)</sup>,  
&emsp;for I am poor and needy.  
<sup>2</sup> Preserve my soul, for I am ~~godly~~ <ins>holy</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c312"></a>[c312](#c312)</sup>.  
&emsp;You, my God, save your servant who trusts in you.  
<sup>3</sup> Be merciful to me, Lord,  
&emsp;for I call to you all day long.  
<sup>4</sup> Bring joy to the soul of your servant,  
&emsp;for to you, Lord, do I lift up my soul.  
<sup>5</sup> For you, Lord, are ~~good, and ready to forgive~~ <ins>sweet and gentle</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c313"></a>[c313](#c313)</sup>,  
&emsp;abundant in loving kindness to all those who call on you.  
<sup>6</sup> Hear, LORD, my prayer.  
&emsp;Listen to the voice of my petitions.  
<sup>7</sup> In the day of my trouble I will call on you,  
&emsp;for you will answer me.  
<sup>8</sup> There is no one like you among the gods, Lord,  
&emsp;nor any deeds like your deeds.  
<sup>9</sup> All nations you have made will come and worship before you, Lord.  
&emsp;They shall glorify your name.  
<sup>10</sup> For you are great, and do wondrous things.  
&emsp;You are God alone.  
<sup>11</sup> Teach me your way, LORD.  
&emsp;I will walk in your truth.  
&emsp;Make my heart undivided to fear your name.  
<sup>12</sup> I will praise you, Lord my God, with my whole heart.  
&emsp;I will glorify your name forever more.  
<sup>13</sup> For your loving kindness is great toward me.  
&emsp;You have delivered my soul from the lowest Sheol.[^33]  
<sup>14</sup> God, the proud have risen up against me.  
&emsp;A company of ~~violent men~~ <ins>the mighty</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c314"></a>[c314](#c314)</sup> have sought after my soul,  
&emsp;and they don’t hold regard for you before them.  
<sup>15</sup> But you, Lord, are a merciful and ~~gracious~~ <ins>compassionate</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c315"></a>[c315](#c315)</sup> God,  
&emsp;slow to anger, and abundant in loving kindness and truth.  
<sup>16</sup> Turn to me, and have mercy on me!  
&emsp;Give your strength to your servant.  
&emsp;Save the son of your servant.  
<sup>17</sup> Show me a sign of your goodness,  
&emsp;that those who hate me may see it, and be shamed,  
&emsp;because you, LORD, have helped me, and comforted me.

## Psalm 87

*A Psalm by the sons of Korah; a Song.*

<sup>1</sup> His foundation is in the holy mountains.  
&emsp;<sup>2</sup> The LORD loves the gates of Zion more than all the dwellings of Jacob.  
<sup>3</sup> Glorious things are spoken about you, city of God. *Selah.*  
<sup>4</sup> I will record Rahab[^34] and Babylon among those who acknowledge me.  
&emsp;Behold, Philistia, Tyre, and also Ethiopia:  
&emsp;“This one was born there.”  
<sup>5</sup> Yes, of Zion it will be said, “This one and that one was born in her;”  
&emsp;the Most High himself will establish her.  
<sup>6</sup> The LORD will count, when he writes up the peoples,  
&emsp;“This one was born there.” *Selah.*  
<sup>7</sup> Those who sing as well as those who dance say,  
&emsp;“All my springs are in you.”

## Psalm 88

*A Song. A Psalm by the sons of Korah. For the Chief Musician. To the tune of “The Suffering of Affliction.” A contemplation by Heman, the Ezrahite.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, the God of my salvation,  
&emsp;I have cried day and night before you.  
<sup>2</sup> Let my prayer enter into your presence.  
&emsp;Turn your ear to my cry.  
<sup>3</sup> For my soul is full of troubles.  
&emsp;My life draws near to Sheol.[^35]  
<sup>4</sup> I am counted among those who go down into the pit.  
&emsp;I am like a man who has no help,  
&emsp;<sup>5</sup> set apart among the dead,  
&emsp;like the slain who lie in the grave,  
&emsp;whom you remember no more.  
&emsp;They are cut off from your hand.  
<sup>6</sup> You have laid me in the lowest pit,  
&emsp;in the darkest depths.  
<sup>7</sup> Your wrath lies heavily on me.  
&emsp;You have afflicted me with all your waves. *Selah.*  
<sup>8</sup> You have taken my friends from me.  
&emsp;You have made me an abomination to them.  
&emsp;I am confined, and I can’t escape.  
<sup>9</sup> My eyes ~~are dim~~ <ins>languish</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c316"></a>[c316](#c316)</sup> from ~~grief~~ <ins>affliction</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c317"></a>[c317](#c317)</sup>.  
&emsp;I have called on you daily, LORD.  
&emsp;I have spread out my hands to you.  
<sup>10</sup> Do you show wonders to the dead?  
&emsp;Do the departed spirits rise up and praise you? *Selah.*  
<sup>11</sup> Is your loving kindness declared in the grave?  
&emsp;Or your faithfulness in Destruction?  
<sup>12</sup> Are your wonders made known in the dark?  
&emsp;Or your righteousness in the land of forgetfulness?  
<sup>13</sup> But to you, LORD, I have cried.  
&emsp;In the morning, my prayer comes before you.  
<sup>14</sup> LORD, why do you reject my soul?  
&emsp;Why do you hide your face from me?  
<sup>15</sup> I am afflicted and ready to die from my youth up.  
&emsp;While I suffer your terrors, I am ~~distracted~~ <ins>confounded</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c318"></a>[c318](#c318)</sup>.  
<sup>16</sup> Your fierce wrath has gone over me.  
&emsp;Your terrors have cut me off.  
<sup>17</sup> They came around me like water all day long.  
&emsp;They completely engulfed me.  
<sup>18</sup> You have put ~~lover~~ <ins>neighbor</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c319"></a>[c319](#c319)</sup> and friend far from me,  
&emsp;and my friends into darkness.

## Psalm 89

*A contemplation by Ethan, the Ezrahite.*

<sup>1</sup> I will sing of the loving kindness of the LORD forever.  
&emsp;With my mouth, I will make known your faithfulness to all generations.  
<sup>2</sup> ~~I indeed declare,~~ <ins>For you said,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c321"></a>[c321](#c321)</sup> ~~“Love stands firm forever.~~ <ins>“Mercy will be built up forever.”</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c320"></a>[c320](#c320)</sup>  
&emsp;~~You established the heavens.~~<sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c322"></a>[c322](#c322)</sup>  
&emsp;~~Your faithfulness is in them.”~~ <ins>Your truth will be established in the heavens.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c323"></a>[c323](#c323)</sup>

<sup>3</sup> “I have made a covenant with my chosen one,  
&emsp;I have sworn to David, my servant,  
<sup>4</sup> ‘I will establish your offspring forever,  
&emsp;and build up your throne to all generations.’” *Selah.*  
<sup>5</sup> The heavens will praise your wonders, LORD,  
&emsp;your faithfulness also in the assembly of the holy ones.  
<sup>6</sup> For who in the ~~skies~~ <ins>clouds</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c324"></a>[c324](#c324)</sup> can be ~~compared~~ <ins>equal</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c325"></a>[c325](#c325)</sup> to the LORD?  
&emsp;Who among the sons of the heavenly beings is like the LORD,  
<sup>7</sup> a very awesome God in the council of the holy ones,  
&emsp;~~to be feared~~ <ins>great and terrible</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c326"></a>[c326](#c326)</sup> above all those who are around him?  
<sup>8</sup> LORD,[^36] God of Armies, who is a mighty one, like you?  
&emsp;LORD,[^37] your faithfulness is around you.  
<sup>9</sup> You rule the pride of the sea.  
&emsp;When its waves rise up, you calm them.  
<sup>10</sup> You have broken Rahab in pieces, like one of the slain.  
&emsp;You have scattered your enemies with your mighty arm.  
<sup>11</sup> The heavens are yours.  
&emsp;The earth also is yours,  
&emsp;the world and its fullness.  
&emsp;You have founded them.  
<sup>12</sup> You have created the north and the south.  
&emsp;Tabor and Hermon rejoice in your name.  
<sup>13</sup> You have a mighty arm.  
&emsp;Your hand is strong, and your right hand is exalted.  
<sup>14</sup> Righteousness and justice are the foundation of your throne.  
&emsp;Loving kindness and truth go before your face.  
<sup>15</sup> Blessed are the people ~~who learn to acclaim you~~ <ins>who know the joyful shout</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c327"></a>[c327](#c327)</sup>.  
&emsp;They walk in the light of your presence, LORD.  
<sup>16</sup> In your name they rejoice all day.  
&emsp;In your righteousness, they are exalted.  
<sup>17</sup> For you are the glory of their strength.  
&emsp;In your favor, our horn will be exalted.  
<sup>18</sup> For our shield belongs to the LORD,  
&emsp;our king to the Holy One of Israel.

<sup>19</sup> Then you spoke in vision to your saints,  
&emsp;and said, “I have given strength to the warrior.  
&emsp;I have exalted a young man from the people.  
<sup>20</sup> I have found David, my servant.  
&emsp;I have anointed him with my holy oil,  
<sup>21</sup> with whom my hand shall be established.  
&emsp;My arm will also strengthen him.  
<sup>22</sup> ~~No enemy will tax him.~~ <ins>The enemy will have no advantage over him.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c328"></a>[c328](#c328)</sup>  
&emsp;No wicked man will oppress him.  
<sup>23</sup> I will beat down his adversaries before him,  
&emsp;and strike those who hate him.  
<sup>24</sup> But my faithfulness and my loving kindness will be with him.  
&emsp;In my name, his horn will be exalted.  
<sup>25</sup> I will set his hand also on the sea,  
&emsp;and his right hand on the rivers.  
<sup>26</sup> He will call to me, ‘You are my Father,  
&emsp;my God, and the rock of my salvation!’  
<sup>27</sup> I will also appoint him my firstborn,  
&emsp;the highest of the kings of the earth.  
<sup>28</sup> I will keep my loving kindness for him forever more.  
&emsp;My covenant will stand firm with him.  
<sup>29</sup> I will also make his offspring endure forever,  
&emsp;and his throne as the days of heaven.  
<sup>30</sup> If his children forsake my law,  
&emsp;and don’t walk in my ordinances;  
<sup>31</sup> if they ~~break~~ <ins>profane</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c329"></a>[c329](#c329)</sup> my statutes,  
&emsp;and don’t keep my commandments;  
<sup>32</sup> then I will punish their sin with the rod,  
&emsp;and their iniquity with stripes.  
<sup>33</sup> But I will not completely take my loving kindness from him,  
&emsp;~~nor allow my faithfulness to fail.~~ <ins>nor will I be false to my truth.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c330"></a>[c330](#c330)</sup>  
<sup>34</sup> I will not ~~break my covenant~~ <ins>profane my covenant</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c331"></a>[c331](#c331)</sup>,  
&emsp;nor alter what my lips have uttered.  
<sup>35</sup> Once I have sworn by my holiness,  
&emsp;I will not lie to David.  
<sup>36</sup> His offspring will endure forever,  
&emsp;his throne like the sun before me.  
<sup>37</sup> It will be established forever like the moon,  
&emsp;the faithful witness in the sky.” *Selah.*

<sup>38</sup> But you have rejected and spurned.  
&emsp;You have been angry with your anointed.  
<sup>39</sup> You have renounced the covenant of your servant.  
&emsp;You have ~~defiled~~ <ins>profaned</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c332"></a>[c332](#c332)</sup> his crown in the dust.  
<sup>40</sup> You have broken down all his hedges.  
&emsp;You have brought his strongholds to ruin.  
<sup>41</sup> All who pass by the way rob him.  
He has become a reproach to his neighbors.  
<sup>42</sup> You have exalted the right hand of his adversaries.  
&emsp;You have made all of his enemies rejoice.  
<sup>43</sup> Yes, you turn back the edge of his sword,  
&emsp;and haven’t supported him in battle.  
<sup>44</sup> You have ended his splendor,  
&emsp;and thrown his throne down to the ground.  
<sup>45</sup> You have shortened the days of his youth.  
&emsp;You have covered him with shame. *Selah.*  
<sup>46</sup> How long, LORD?  
&emsp;Will you hide yourself forever?  
&emsp;Will your wrath burn like fire?  
<sup>47</sup> Remember how short my time is,  
&emsp;for what vanity you have created all the children of men!  
<sup>48</sup> What man is he who shall live and not see death,  
&emsp;who shall deliver his soul from the power of Sheol?[^38] *Selah.*  
<sup>49</sup> Lord, where are your former loving kindnesses,  
&emsp;which you swore to David in your faithfulness?  
<sup>50</sup> Remember, Lord, the reproach of your servants,  
&emsp;how I bear in my heart the taunts of all the mighty peoples,  
<sup>51</sup> With which your enemies have mocked, LORD,  
&emsp;with which they have mocked the footsteps of your anointed one.

<sup>52</sup> Blessed be the LORD forever more.  
Amen, and Amen.

## Psalm 90

**BOOK 4**

*A Prayer by Moses, the man of God.[^39]*

<sup>1</sup> Lord,[^40] you have been our dwelling place for all generations.  
&emsp;<sup>2</sup> Before the mountains were born,  
&emsp;before you had formed the earth and the world,  
&emsp;even from everlasting to everlasting, you are God.  
<sup>3</sup> You turn man to destruction, saying,  
&emsp;“Return, you children of men.”  
<sup>4</sup> For a thousand years in your sight are just like yesterday when it is past,  
&emsp;like a watch in the night.  
<sup>5</sup> You sweep them away as they sleep.  
&emsp;In the morning they sprout like new grass.  
<sup>6</sup> In the morning it ~~sprouts and springs up~~ <ins>blossoms and grows</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c333"></a>[c333](#c333)</sup>.  
&emsp;By evening, it is withered and dry.  
<sup>7</sup> For we are consumed in your anger.  
&emsp;We are troubled in your wrath.  
<sup>8</sup> You have set our iniquities before you,  
&emsp;our secret sins in the light of your presence.  
<sup>9</sup> For all our days have passed away in your wrath.  
&emsp;We bring our years to an end as a sigh.  
<sup>10</sup> The days of our years are seventy,  
&emsp;or even by reason of strength eighty years;  
&emsp;yet their pride is but labor and sorrow,  
&emsp;for it passes quickly, and we fly away.  
<sup>11</sup> Who knows the power of your anger,  
&emsp;your wrath according to the fear that is due to you?  
<sup>12</sup> So teach us to count our days,  
&emsp;that we may gain a heart of wisdom.  
<sup>13</sup> Relent, LORD![^41]  
&emsp;How long?  
&emsp;Have compassion on your servants!  
<sup>14</sup> Satisfy us in the morning with your loving kindness,  
&emsp;that we may rejoice and be glad all our days.  
<sup>15</sup> Make us glad for as many days as you have afflicted us,  
&emsp;for as many years as we have seen evil.  
<sup>16</sup> Let your work appear to your servants,  
&emsp;your glory to their children.  
<sup>17</sup> Let the favor of the Lord our God be on us.  
&emsp;Establish the work of our hands for us.  
&emsp;Yes, establish the work of our hands.

## Psalm 91

<sup>1</sup> He who dwells in the secret place of the Most High  
&emsp;will rest in the shadow of the Almighty.  
<sup>2</sup> I will say of the LORD, “He is my refuge and my fortress;  
&emsp;my God, in whom I trust.”  
<sup>3</sup> For he will deliver you from the snare of the fowler,  
&emsp;and from the deadly pestilence.  
<sup>4</sup> He will cover you with his feathers.  
&emsp;Under his wings you will take refuge.  
&emsp;His faithfulness is your shield and rampart.  
<sup>5</sup> You shall not be afraid of the terror by night,  
&emsp;nor of the arrow that flies by day,  
&emsp;<sup>6</sup> nor of the pestilence that walks in darkness,  
&emsp;nor of the destruction that wastes at noonday.  
<sup>7</sup> A thousand may fall at your side,  
&emsp;and ten thousand at your right hand;  
&emsp;but it will not come near you.  
<sup>8</sup> You will only look with your eyes,  
&emsp;and see the recompense of the wicked.  
<sup>9</sup> Because you have made the LORD your refuge,  
&emsp;and the Most High your dwelling place,  
<sup>10</sup> no evil shall happen to you,  
&emsp;neither shall any plague come near your dwelling.  
<sup>11</sup> For he will put his angels in charge of you,  
&emsp;to guard you in all your ways.  
<sup>12</sup> They will bear you up in their hands,  
&emsp;so that you won’t dash your foot against a stone.  
<sup>13</sup> You will tread on the lion and cobra.  
&emsp;You will trample the young lion and the serpent underfoot.  
<sup>14</sup> “Because he has set his love on me, therefore I will deliver him.  
&emsp;I will set him on high, because he has known my name.  
<sup>15</sup> He will call on me, and I will answer him.  
&emsp;I will be with him in trouble.  
&emsp;I will deliver him, and honor him.  
<sup>16</sup> I will satisfy him with long life,  
&emsp;and show him my salvation.”

## Psalm 92

*A Psalm. A song for the Sabbath day.*

<sup>1</sup> It is a good thing to give thanks to the LORD,  
&emsp;to sing praises to your name, Most High,  
<sup>2</sup> to proclaim your loving kindness in the morning,  
&emsp;and your faithfulness every night,  
<sup>3</sup> with the ten-stringed lute, with the harp,  
&emsp;and with the melody of the lyre.  
<sup>4</sup> For you, LORD, have made me glad through your work.  
&emsp;I will triumph in the works of your hands.  
<sup>5</sup> How great are your works, LORD!  
&emsp;Your thoughts are very deep.  
<sup>6</sup> A senseless man doesn’t know,  
&emsp;neither does a fool understand this:  
<sup>7</sup> though the wicked spring up as the grass,  
&emsp;and all the evildoers flourish,  
&emsp;they will be destroyed forever.  
<sup>8</sup> But you, LORD, are on high forever more.  
<sup>9</sup> For behold, your enemies, LORD,  
&emsp;for behold, your enemies shall perish.  
&emsp;All the evildoers will be scattered.  
<sup>10</sup> But you have exalted my horn like that of the wild ox.  
&emsp;I am anointed with ~~fresh~~ <ins>rich</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c334"></a>[c334](#c334)</sup> oil.  
<sup>11</sup> My eye has also seen my enemies.  
&emsp;My ears have heard of the wicked enemies who rise up against me.  
<sup>12</sup> The righteous shall flourish like the palm tree.  
&emsp;He will grow like a cedar in Lebanon.  
<sup>13</sup> They are planted in the LORD’s house.  
&emsp;They will flourish in our God’s courts.  
<sup>14</sup> They will still produce fruit in old age.  
&emsp;They will be full of sap and green,  
&emsp;<sup>15</sup> to show that the LORD is upright.  
He is my ~~rock~~ <ins>refuge</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c335"></a>[c335](#c335)</sup>,  
&emsp;and there is no unrighteousness in him.

## Psalm 93

<sup>1</sup> The LORD reigns!  
&emsp;He is clothed with majesty!  
&emsp;The LORD is armed with strength.  
The world also is established.  
&emsp;It can’t be moved.  
<sup>2</sup> Your throne is established from long ago.  
&emsp;You are from everlasting.  
<sup>3</sup> The floods have lifted up, LORD,  
&emsp;the floods have lifted up their voice.  
&emsp;The floods lift up their waves.  
<sup>4</sup> Above the voices of many waters,  
&emsp;the mighty breakers of the sea,  
&emsp;the LORD on high is mighty.  
<sup>5</sup> Your ~~statutes~~ <ins>testimonies</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c336"></a>[c336](#c336)</sup> stand firm.  
&emsp;Holiness adorns your house,  
&emsp;LORD, forever more.

## Psalm 94

<sup>1</sup> LORD,[^42] you God to whom vengeance belongs,  
&emsp;you God to whom vengeance belongs, shine out.  
<sup>2</sup> Rise up, you judge of the earth.  
&emsp;Pay back the proud what they deserve.  
<sup>3</sup> LORD, how long will the wicked,  
&emsp;how long will the wicked triumph?  
<sup>4</sup> They pour out arrogant words.  
&emsp;All the evildoers boast.  
<sup>5</sup> They break your people in pieces, LORD,  
&emsp;and afflict your heritage.  
<sup>6</sup> They kill the widow and the alien,  
&emsp;and murder the fatherless.  
<sup>7</sup> They say, “The LORD[^43] will not see,  
&emsp;neither will Jacob’s God consider.”  
<sup>8</sup> Consider, you senseless among the people;  
&emsp;you fools, when will you be wise?  
<sup>9</sup> He who implanted the ear, won’t he hear?  
&emsp;He who formed the eye, won’t he see?  
<sup>10</sup> He who disciplines the nations, won’t he punish?  
&emsp;He who teaches man knows.  
<sup>11</sup> The LORD knows the thoughts of man,  
&emsp;that they are futile.  
<sup>12</sup> Blessed is the man whom you discipline, LORD,[^44]  
&emsp;and teach out of your law,  
<sup>13</sup> that you may give him rest from the days of adversity,  
&emsp;until the pit is dug for the wicked.  
<sup>14</sup> For the LORD won’t reject his people,  
&emsp;neither will he forsake his inheritance.  
<sup>15</sup> For judgment will return to righteousness.  
&emsp;All the upright in heart shall follow it.  
<sup>16</sup> Who will rise up for me against the wicked?  
&emsp;Who will stand up for me against the evildoers?  
<sup>17</sup> Unless the LORD had been my help,  
&emsp;my soul would have soon lived in silence.  
<sup>18</sup> When I said, “My foot is slipping!”  
&emsp;Your loving kindness, LORD, held me up.  
<sup>19</sup> In the multitude of my thoughts within me,  
&emsp;your comforts delight my soul.  
<sup>20</sup> Shall the throne of wickedness have fellowship with you,  
&emsp;~~which brings about mischief by statute?~~ <ins>which frames trouble against the commandment?</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c337"></a>[c337](#c337)</sup>  
<sup>21</sup> They ~~gather themselves together against~~ <ins>rush upon</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c338"></a>[c338](#c338)</sup> the soul of the righteous,  
&emsp;and condemn the innocent blood.  
<sup>22</sup> But the LORD has been my high tower,  
&emsp;my God, the rock of my refuge.  
<sup>23</sup> He has brought on them their own iniquity,  
&emsp;and will cut them off in their own wickedness.  
&emsp;The LORD, our God, will cut them off.

## Psalm 95

<sup>1</sup> Oh come, let’s sing to the LORD.  
&emsp;Let’s shout aloud to ~~the rock of our salvation~~ <ins>God our Savior</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c339"></a>[c339](#c339)</sup>!  
<sup>2</sup> Let’s come before his presence with thanksgiving.  
&emsp;Let’s extol him with songs!  
<sup>3</sup> For the LORD is a great God,  
&emsp;a great King above all gods.  
<sup>4</sup> In his hand are the deep places of the earth.  
&emsp;The heights of the mountains are also his.  
<sup>5</sup> The sea is his, and he made it.  
&emsp;His hands formed the dry land.  
<sup>6</sup> Oh come, let’s worship and bow down.  
&emsp;Let’s kneel before the LORD, our Maker,  
&emsp;<sup>7</sup> for he is our God.  
We are the people of his pasture,  
&emsp;and the sheep in his care.  
Today, oh that you would hear his voice!  
&emsp;<sup>8</sup> Don’t harden your heart, as at Meribah,  
&emsp;as in the day of Massah in the wilderness,  
<sup>9</sup> when your fathers tempted me,  
&emsp;tested me, and saw my work.  
<sup>10</sup> Forty long years I was grieved with that generation,  
&emsp;and said, “They are a people who err in their heart.  
&emsp;They have not known my ways.”  
<sup>11</sup> Therefore I swore in my wrath,  
&emsp;“They won’t enter into my rest.”

## Psalm 96

<sup>1</sup> Sing to the LORD a new song!  
&emsp;Sing to the LORD, all the earth.  
<sup>2</sup> Sing to the LORD!  
&emsp;Bless his name!  
&emsp;Proclaim his salvation from day to day!  
<sup>3</sup> Declare his glory among the nations,  
&emsp;his marvelous works among all the peoples.  
<sup>4</sup> For the LORD is great, and greatly to be praised!  
&emsp;He is to be feared above all gods.  
<sup>5</sup> For all the gods of the peoples are ~~idols~~ <ins>empty things</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c340"></a>[c340](#c340)</sup>,  
&emsp;but the LORD made the heavens.  
<sup>6</sup> Honor and majesty are before him.  
&emsp;Strength and beauty are in his sanctuary.  
<sup>7</sup> Ascribe to the LORD, you families of nations,  
&emsp;ascribe to the LORD glory and strength.  
<sup>8</sup> Ascribe to the LORD the glory due to his name.  
&emsp;Bring an offering, and come into his courts.  
<sup>9</sup> Worship the LORD in holy array.  
&emsp;Tremble before him, all the earth.  
<sup>10</sup> Say among the nations, “The LORD reigns.”  
&emsp;The world is also established.  
&emsp;It can’t be moved.  
&emsp;He will judge the peoples with equity.  
<sup>11</sup> Let the heavens be glad, and let the earth rejoice.  
&emsp;Let the sea roar, and its fullness!  
&emsp;<sup>12</sup> Let the field and all that is in it exult!  
&emsp;Then all the trees of the woods shall sing for joy  
&emsp;<sup>13</sup> before the LORD; for he comes,  
&emsp;for he comes to judge the earth.  
He will judge the world with righteousness,  
&emsp;the peoples with his truth.

## Psalm 97

<sup>1</sup> The LORD reigns!  
&emsp;Let the earth rejoice!  
&emsp;Let the multitude of islands be glad!  
<sup>2</sup> Clouds and darkness are around him.  
&emsp;Righteousness and justice are the foundation of his throne.  
<sup>3</sup> A fire goes before him,  
&emsp;and burns up his adversaries on every side.  
<sup>4</sup> His lightning lights up the world.  
&emsp;The earth sees, and trembles.  
<sup>5</sup> The mountains melt like wax at the presence of the LORD,  
&emsp;at the presence of the Lord of the whole earth.  
<sup>6</sup> The heavens declare his righteousness.  
&emsp;All the peoples have seen his glory.  
<sup>7</sup> Let all them be shamed who serve engraved images,  
&emsp;who boast in their idols.  
&emsp;Worship him, all ~~you gods~~ <ins>his angels</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c341"></a>[c341](#c341)</sup>![^45]  
<sup>8</sup> Zion heard and was glad.  
&emsp;The daughters of Judah rejoiced  
&emsp;because of your judgments, LORD.  
<sup>9</sup> For you, LORD, are most high above all the earth.  
&emsp;You are exalted far above all gods.  
<sup>10</sup> You who love the LORD, hate evil!  
&emsp;He preserves the souls of his saints.  
&emsp;He delivers them out of the hand of the wicked.  
<sup>11</sup> Light is sown for the righteous,  
&emsp;and gladness for the upright in heart.  
<sup>12</sup> Be glad in the LORD, you righteous people!  
&emsp;Give thanks to his holy Name.

## Psalm 98

*A Psalm.*

<sup>1</sup> Sing to the LORD a new song,  
&emsp;for he has done marvelous things!  
&emsp;His right hand and his holy arm have worked salvation for him.  
<sup>2</sup> The LORD has made known his salvation.  
&emsp;He has openly shown his righteousness in the sight of the nations.  
<sup>3</sup> He has remembered his loving kindness and his faithfulness toward the house of Israel.  
&emsp;All the ends of the earth have seen the salvation of our God.  
<sup>4</sup> Make a joyful noise to the LORD, all the earth!  
&emsp;Burst out and sing for joy, yes, sing praises!  
<sup>5</sup> Sing praises to the LORD with the harp,  
&emsp;with the harp and the voice of melody.  
<sup>6</sup> With trumpets and sound of the ram’s horn,  
&emsp;make a joyful noise before the King, the LORD.  
<sup>7</sup> Let the sea roar with its fullness;  
&emsp;the world, and those who dwell therein.  
<sup>8</sup> Let the rivers clap their hands.  
&emsp;Let the mountains sing for joy together.  
<sup>9</sup> Let them sing before the LORD,  
&emsp;for he comes to judge the earth.  
He will judge the world with righteousness,  
&emsp;and the peoples with equity.

## Psalm 99

<sup>1</sup> The LORD reigns! Let the peoples tremble.  
&emsp;He sits enthroned among the cherubim.  
&emsp;Let the earth be moved.  
<sup>2</sup> The LORD is great in Zion.  
&emsp;He is high above all the peoples.  
<sup>3</sup> Let them praise your great and awesome name.  
&emsp;He is Holy!

<sup>4</sup> The King’s strength also loves justice.  
&emsp;You establish equity.  
&emsp;You execute justice and righteousness in Jacob.  
<sup>5</sup> Exalt the LORD our God.  
&emsp;Worship at his footstool.  
&emsp;He is Holy!

<sup>6</sup> Moses and Aaron were among his priests,  
&emsp;Samuel was among those who call on his name.  
&emsp;They called on the LORD, and he answered them.  
<sup>7</sup> He spoke to them in the pillar of cloud.  
&emsp;They kept his testimonies,  
&emsp;the statute that he gave them.  
<sup>8</sup> You answered them, LORD our God.  
&emsp;You are a God who forgave them,  
&emsp;although you took vengeance for their doings.  
<sup>9</sup> Exalt the LORD, our God.  
&emsp;Worship at his holy hill,  
&emsp;for the LORD, our God, is holy!

## Psalm 100

*A Psalm of thanksgiving.*

<sup>1</sup> Shout for joy to the LORD, all you lands!  
&emsp;<sup>2</sup> Serve the LORD with gladness.  
&emsp;Come before his presence with singing.  
<sup>3</sup> Know that the LORD, he is God.  
&emsp;It is he who has made us, and we are his.  
&emsp;We are his people, and the sheep of his pasture.  
<sup>4</sup> Enter into his gates with thanksgiving,  
&emsp;and into his courts with praise.  
&emsp;Give thanks to him, and bless his name.  
<sup>5</sup> For the LORD is good.  
&emsp;His loving kindness endures forever,  
&emsp;his faithfulness to all generations.

## Psalm 101

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> I will sing of loving kindness and justice.  
&emsp;To you, LORD, I will sing praises.  
<sup>2</sup> I will be careful to live a blameless life.  
&emsp;When will you come to me?  
&emsp;I will walk within my house with a blameless heart.  
<sup>3</sup> I will set no vile thing before my eyes.  
&emsp;I hate the deeds of faithless men.  
&emsp;They will not cling to me.  
<sup>4</sup> A perverse heart will be far from me.  
&emsp;I will have nothing to do with evil.  
<sup>5</sup> I will silence whoever secretly slanders his neighbor.  
&emsp;I won’t tolerate one who is arrogant and conceited.  
<sup>6</sup> My eyes will be on the faithful of the land,  
&emsp;that they may dwell with me.  
He who walks in a perfect way,  
&emsp;he will serve me.  
<sup>7</sup> He who ~~practices deceit~~ <ins>acts proudly</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c342"></a>[c342](#c342)</sup> won’t dwell within my house.  
&emsp;He who speaks ~~falsehood~~ <ins>unjust things</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c343"></a>[c343](#c343)</sup> won’t be established before my eyes.  
<sup>8</sup> Morning by morning, I will destroy all the wicked of the land,  
&emsp;to cut off all the workers of iniquity from the LORD’s city.

## Psalm 102

*A Prayer of the afflicted, when he is overwhelmed and pours out his complaint before the LORD.*

<sup>1</sup> Hear my prayer, LORD![^46]  
&emsp;Let my cry come to you.  
<sup>2</sup> Don’t hide your face from me in the day of my distress.  
&emsp;Turn your ear to me.  
&emsp;Answer me quickly in the day when I call.  
<sup>3</sup> For my days consume away like smoke.  
&emsp;My bones are burned as a torch.  
<sup>4</sup> My heart is blighted like grass, and withered,  
&emsp;for I forget to eat my bread.  
<sup>5</sup> By reason of the voice of my groaning,  
&emsp;my bones stick to my skin.  
<sup>6</sup> I am like a pelican of the wilderness.  
&emsp;I have become as an owl of the waste places.  
&emsp;<sup>7</sup> I watch, and have become like a sparrow that is alone on the housetop.  
<sup>8</sup> My enemies reproach me all day.  
&emsp;Those who are mad at me use my name as a curse.  
<sup>9</sup> For I have eaten ashes like bread,  
&emsp;and mixed my drink with tears,  
&emsp;<sup>10</sup> because of your indignation and your wrath;  
&emsp;for you have taken me up and thrown me away.  
<sup>11</sup> My days ~~are~~ <ins>have declined</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c344"></a>[c344](#c344)</sup> like a long shadow.  
&emsp;I have withered like grass.

<sup>12</sup> But you, LORD, will remain forever;  
&emsp;your renown endures to all generations.  
<sup>13</sup> You will arise and have mercy on Zion,  
&emsp;for it is time to have pity on her.  
&emsp;Yes, the set time has come.  
<sup>14</sup> For your servants take pleasure in her stones,  
&emsp;and have pity on her dust.  
<sup>15</sup> So the nations will fear the LORD’s name,  
&emsp;all the kings of the earth your glory.  
<sup>16</sup> For the LORD has built up Zion.  
&emsp;He has appeared in his glory.  
<sup>17</sup> He has responded to the prayer of the destitute,  
&emsp;and has not despised their prayer.  
<sup>18</sup> This will be written for the generation to come.  
&emsp;A people which will be created will praise the LORD,[^47]  
<sup>19</sup> for he has looked down from the height of his sanctuary.  
&emsp;From heaven, the LORD saw the earth,  
<sup>20</sup> to hear the groans of the prisoner,  
&emsp;to free those who are condemned to death,  
<sup>21</sup> that men may declare the LORD’s name in Zion,  
&emsp;and his praise in Jerusalem,  
<sup>22</sup> when the peoples are gathered together,  
&emsp;the kingdoms, to serve the LORD.

<sup>23</sup> He weakened my strength along the course.  
&emsp;He shortened my days.  
<sup>24</sup> I said, “My God, don’t take me away in the middle of my days.  
&emsp;Your years are throughout all generations.  
<sup>25</sup> Of old, you laid the foundation of the earth.  
&emsp;The heavens are the work of your hands.  
<sup>26</sup> They will perish, but you will endure.  
&emsp;Yes, all of them will wear out like a garment.  
&emsp;You will change them like a cloak, and they will be changed.  
<sup>27</sup> But you are the same.  
&emsp;Your years will have no end.  
<sup>28</sup> The children of your servants will continue.  
&emsp;Their offspring will be established before you.”

## Psalm 103

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD, my soul!  
&emsp;All that is within me, praise his holy name!  
<sup>2</sup> Praise the LORD, my soul,  
&emsp;and don’t forget all his benefits,  
<sup>3</sup> who forgives all your sins,  
&emsp;who heals all your diseases,  
<sup>4</sup> who redeems your life from destruction,  
&emsp;who crowns you with loving kindness and tender mercies,  
<sup>5</sup> who satisfies your desire with good things,  
&emsp;so that your youth is renewed like the eagle’s.  
<sup>6</sup> The LORD executes righteous acts,  
&emsp;and justice for all who are oppressed.  
<sup>7</sup> He made known his ways to Moses,  
&emsp;his deeds to the children of Israel.  
<sup>8</sup> The LORD is merciful and gracious,  
&emsp;slow to anger, and abundant in loving kindness.  
<sup>9</sup> He will not always accuse;  
&emsp;neither will he stay angry forever.  
<sup>10</sup> He has not dealt with us according to our sins,  
&emsp;nor repaid us for our iniquities.  
<sup>11</sup> For as the heavens are high above the earth,  
&emsp;so great is his loving kindness toward those who fear him.  
<sup>12</sup> As far as the east is from the west,  
&emsp;so far has he removed our transgressions from us.  
<sup>13</sup> Like a father has compassion on his children,  
&emsp;so the LORD has compassion on those who fear him.  
<sup>14</sup> For he knows how we are made.  
&emsp;He remembers that we are dust.  
<sup>15</sup> As for man, his days are like grass.  
&emsp;As a flower of the field, so he flourishes.  
<sup>16</sup> For the wind passes over it, and it is gone.  
&emsp;Its place remembers it no more.  
<sup>17</sup> But the LORD’s loving kindness is from everlasting to everlasting with those who fear him,  
&emsp;his righteousness to children’s children,  
<sup>18</sup> to those who keep his covenant,  
&emsp;to those who remember to obey his precepts.  
<sup>19</sup> The LORD has established his throne in the heavens.  
&emsp;His kingdom rules over all.  
<sup>20</sup> Praise the LORD, you angels of his,  
&emsp;who are mighty in strength, who fulfill his word,  
&emsp;obeying the voice of his word.  
<sup>21</sup> Praise the LORD, all you armies of his,  
&emsp;you servants of his, who do his pleasure.  
<sup>22</sup> Praise the LORD, all you works of his,  
&emsp;in all places of his dominion.  
&emsp;Praise the LORD, my soul!

## Psalm 104

<sup>1</sup> Bless the LORD,[^48] my soul.  
&emsp;The LORD, my God, you are very great.  
&emsp;You are clothed with honor and majesty.  
<sup>2</sup> He covers himself with light as with a garment.  
&emsp;He stretches out the heavens like a curtain.  
<sup>3</sup> He lays the beams of his rooms in the waters.  
&emsp;He makes the clouds his chariot.  
&emsp;He walks on the wings of the wind.  
<sup>4</sup> He makes his messengers[^49] winds,  
&emsp;and his servants flames of fire.  
<sup>5</sup> He laid the foundations of the earth,  
&emsp;that it should not be moved forever.  
<sup>6</sup> You covered it with the deep as with a cloak.  
&emsp;The waters stood above the mountains.  
<sup>7</sup> At your rebuke they fled.  
&emsp;At the voice of your thunder they hurried away.  
<sup>8</sup> ~~The mountains rose~~ <ins>They went up to the mountains</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c346"></a>[c346](#c346)</sup>,  
&emsp;~~the valleys sank down~~ <ins>they went down to the valleys</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c345"></a>[c345](#c345)</sup>,  
&emsp;to the place which you had assigned to them.  
<sup>9</sup> You have set a boundary that they may not pass over,  
&emsp;that they don’t turn again to cover the earth.  
<sup>10</sup> He sends springs into the valleys.  
&emsp;They run among the mountains.  
<sup>11</sup> They give drink to every animal of the field.  
&emsp;The wild donkeys quench their thirst.  
<sup>12</sup> The birds of the sky nest by them.  
&emsp;They sing among the branches.  
<sup>13</sup> He waters the mountains from his rooms.  
&emsp;The earth is filled with the fruit of your works.  
<sup>14</sup> He causes the grass to grow for the livestock,  
&emsp;and plants for ~~man to cultivate~~ <ins>the service of man</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c347"></a>[c347](#c347)</sup>,  
&emsp;that he may produce food out of the earth:  
<sup>15</sup> wine that makes the heart of man glad,  
&emsp;oil to make his face to shine,  
&emsp;and bread that strengthens man’s heart.  
<sup>16</sup> The LORD’s trees are well watered,  
&emsp;the cedars of Lebanon, which he has planted,  
<sup>17</sup> where the birds make their nests.  
&emsp;The ~~stork~~ <ins>heron</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c348"></a>[c348](#c348)</sup> makes its home in ~~the cypress trees~~ <ins>their tops</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c349"></a>[c349](#c349)</sup>.  
<sup>18</sup> The high mountains are for the ~~wild goats~~ <ins>deer</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c350"></a>[c350](#c350)</sup>.  
&emsp;The rocks are a refuge for the rock badgers.  
<sup>19</sup> He appointed the moon for seasons.  
&emsp;The sun knows when to set.  
<sup>20</sup> You make darkness, and it is night,  
&emsp;in which all the animals of the forest prowl.  
<sup>21</sup> The young lions roar after their prey,  
&emsp;and seek their food from God.  
<sup>22</sup> The sun rises, and they steal away,  
&emsp;and lie down in their dens.  
<sup>23</sup> Man goes out to his work,  
&emsp;to his labor until the evening.  
<sup>24</sup> LORD, how many are your works!  
&emsp;In wisdom, you have made them all.  
&emsp;The earth is full of your riches.  
<sup>25</sup> There is the sea, great and wide,  
&emsp;in which are innumerable living things,  
&emsp;both small and large animals.  
<sup>26</sup> There the ships go,  
&emsp;and leviathan, whom you formed to play there.  
<sup>27</sup> These all wait for you,  
&emsp;that you may give them their food in due season.  
<sup>28</sup> You give to them; they gather.  
&emsp;You open your hand; they are satisfied with good.  
<sup>29</sup> You hide your face; they are troubled.  
&emsp;You take away their breath; they die and return to the dust.  
<sup>30</sup> You send out your Spirit and they are created.  
&emsp;You renew the face of the ground.  
<sup>31</sup> Let the LORD’s glory endure forever.  
&emsp;Let the LORD rejoice in his works.  
<sup>32</sup> He looks at the earth, and it trembles.  
&emsp;He touches the mountains, and they smoke.  
<sup>33</sup> I will sing to the LORD as long as I live.  
&emsp;I will sing praise to my God while I have any being.  
<sup>34</sup> Let my meditation be sweet to him.  
&emsp;I will rejoice in the LORD.  
<sup>35</sup> Let sinners be consumed out of the earth.  
&emsp;Let the wicked be no more.  
&emsp;Bless the LORD,[^50] my soul.  
&emsp;Praise the LORD![^51]

## Psalm 105

<sup>1</sup> Give thanks to the LORD![^52] Call on his name!  
&emsp;Make his doings known among the peoples.  
<sup>2</sup> Sing to him, sing praises to him!  
&emsp;Tell of all his marvelous works.  
<sup>3</sup> Glory in his holy name.  
&emsp;Let the heart of those who seek the LORD rejoice.  
<sup>4</sup> Seek the LORD and his strength.  
&emsp;Seek his face forever more.  
<sup>5</sup> Remember his marvelous works that he has done:  
&emsp;~~his wonders~~ <ins>his portents</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c351"></a>[c351](#c351)</sup>, and the judgments of his mouth,  
<sup>6</sup> you offspring of Abraham, his servant,  
&emsp;you children of Jacob, his chosen ones.  
<sup>7</sup> He is the LORD, our God.  
&emsp;His judgments are in all the earth.  
<sup>8</sup> He has remembered his covenant forever,  
&emsp;the word which he commanded to a thousand generations,  
<sup>9</sup> the covenant which he made with Abraham,  
&emsp;his oath to Isaac,  
<sup>10</sup> and confirmed it to Jacob for a statute;  
&emsp;to Israel for an everlasting covenant,  
<sup>11</sup> saying, “To you I will give the land of Canaan,  
&emsp;the lot of your inheritance,”  
<sup>12</sup> when they were but a few men in number,  
&emsp;yes, very few, and foreigners in it.  
<sup>13</sup> They went about from nation to nation,  
&emsp;from one kingdom to another people.  
<sup>14</sup> He allowed no one to do them wrong.  
&emsp;Yes, he reproved kings for their sakes,  
<sup>15</sup> “Don’t touch my anointed ones!  
&emsp;Do my prophets no harm!”  
<sup>16</sup> He called for a famine on the land.  
&emsp;He destroyed the food supplies.  
<sup>17</sup> He sent a man before them.  
&emsp;Joseph was sold for a slave.  
<sup>18</sup> They bruised his feet with shackles.  
&emsp;His neck was locked in irons,  
<sup>19</sup> until the time that his word happened,  
&emsp;and the LORD’s word proved him true.  
<sup>20</sup> The king sent and freed him,  
&emsp;even the ruler of peoples, and let him go free.  
<sup>21</sup> He made him lord of his house,  
&emsp;and ruler of all of his possessions,  
<sup>22</sup> to discipline his princes at his pleasure,  
&emsp;and to teach his elders wisdom.  
<sup>23</sup> Israel also came into Egypt.  
&emsp;Jacob lived in the land of Ham.  
<sup>24</sup> He increased his people greatly,  
&emsp;and made them stronger than their adversaries.  
<sup>25</sup> He turned their heart to hate his people,  
&emsp;to conspire against his servants.  
<sup>26</sup> He sent Moses, his servant,  
&emsp;and Aaron, whom he had chosen.  
<sup>27</sup> They performed miracles among them,  
&emsp;and wonders in the land of Ham.  
<sup>28</sup> He sent darkness, and made it dark.  
&emsp;They didn’t rebel against his words.  
<sup>29</sup> He turned their waters into blood,  
&emsp;and killed their fish.  
<sup>30</sup> Their land swarmed with frogs,  
&emsp;even in the rooms of their kings.  
<sup>31</sup> He spoke, and swarms of flies came,  
&emsp;and lice in all their borders.  
<sup>32</sup> He gave them hail for rain,  
&emsp;with ~~lightning~~ <ins>flaming fire</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c352"></a>[c352](#c352)</sup> in their land.  
<sup>33</sup> He struck their vines and also their fig trees,  
&emsp;and shattered the trees of their country.  
<sup>34</sup> He spoke, and the locusts came  
&emsp;with the grasshoppers, without number.  
<sup>35</sup> They ate up every plant in their land,  
&emsp;and ate up the fruit of their ground.  
<sup>36</sup> He struck also all the firstborn in their land,  
&emsp;the first fruits of all their manhood.  
<sup>37</sup> He brought them out with silver and gold.  
&emsp;There was not one feeble person among his tribes.  
<sup>38</sup> Egypt was glad when they departed,  
&emsp;for the fear of them had fallen on them.  
<sup>39</sup> He spread a cloud for a covering,  
&emsp;fire to give light in the night.  
<sup>40</sup> They asked, and he brought quails,  
&emsp;and satisfied them with the bread of the sky.  
<sup>41</sup> He opened the rock, and waters gushed out.  
&emsp;They ran as a river in the dry places.  
<sup>42</sup> For he remembered his holy word,  
&emsp;and Abraham, his servant.  
<sup>43</sup> He brought his people out with joy,  
&emsp;his chosen with ~~singing~~ <ins>gladness</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c353"></a>[c353](#c353)</sup>.  
<sup>44</sup> He gave them the lands of the nations.  
&emsp;They took the labor of the peoples in possession,  
<sup>45</sup> that they might keep his statutes,  
&emsp;and observe his laws.  
&emsp;Praise the LORD![^53]

## Psalm 106

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^54]  
&emsp;Give thanks to the LORD, for he is good,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>2</sup> Who can utter the mighty acts of the LORD,  
&emsp;or fully declare all his praise?  
<sup>3</sup> Blessed are those who keep justice.  
&emsp;Blessed is one who does what is right at all times.  
<sup>4</sup> ~~Remember me~~ <ins>Remember us</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c354"></a>[c354](#c354)</sup>, LORD, with the favor that you show to your people.  
&emsp;~~Visit me with your salvation,~~ <ins>Visit us with your salvation,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c355"></a>[c355](#c355)</sup>  
<sup>5</sup> ~~that I may see~~ <ins>that we may see</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c356"></a>[c356](#c356)</sup> the prosperity of your chosen,  
&emsp;~~that I may rejoice~~ <ins>that we may rejoice</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c357"></a>[c357](#c357)</sup> in the gladness of your nation,  
&emsp;~~that I may glory~~ <ins>that we may glory</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c358"></a>[c358](#c358)</sup> with your inheritance.

<sup>6</sup> We have sinned with our fathers.  
&emsp;We have committed iniquity.  
&emsp;We have done wickedly.  
<sup>7</sup> Our fathers didn’t understand your wonders in Egypt.  
&emsp;They didn’t remember the multitude of your loving kindnesses,  
&emsp;but were rebellious at the sea, even at the Red Sea.  
<sup>8</sup> Nevertheless he saved them for his name’s sake,  
&emsp;that he might make his mighty power known.  
<sup>9</sup> He rebuked the Red Sea also, and it was dried up;  
&emsp;so he led them through the depths, as through a desert.
