<sup>10</sup> He saved them from the hand of him who hated them,  
&emsp;and redeemed them from the hand of the enemy.  
<sup>11</sup> The waters covered their adversaries.  
&emsp;There was not one of them left.  
<sup>12</sup> Then they believed his words.  
&emsp;They sang his praise.

<sup>13</sup> They soon forgot his works.  
&emsp;They didn’t wait for his counsel,  
&emsp;<sup>14</sup> but gave in to craving in the desert,  
&emsp;and tested God in the wasteland.  
<sup>15</sup> He gave them their request,  
&emsp;but sent ~~leanness~~ <ins>satiety</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c359"></a>[c359](#c359)</sup> into their soul.  
<sup>16</sup> They envied Moses also in the camp,  
&emsp;and Aaron, the LORD’s saint.  
<sup>17</sup> The earth opened and swallowed up Dathan,  
&emsp;and covered the company of Abiram.  
<sup>18</sup> A fire was kindled in their company.  
&emsp;The flame burned up the wicked.  
<sup>19</sup> They made a calf in Horeb,  
&emsp;and worshiped a ~~molten image~~ <ins>carved image</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c360"></a>[c360](#c360)</sup>.  
<sup>20</sup> Thus they exchanged their glory  
&emsp;for an image of a bull that eats grass.  
<sup>21</sup> They forgot God, their Savior,  
&emsp;who had done great things in Egypt,  
&emsp;<sup>22</sup> wondrous works in the land of Ham,  
&emsp;and awesome things by the Red Sea.  
<sup>23</sup> Therefore he said that he would destroy them,  
&emsp;had Moses, his chosen, not stood before him in the breach,  
&emsp;to turn away his wrath, so that he wouldn’t destroy them.  
<sup>24</sup> Yes, they despised the pleasant land.  
&emsp;They didn’t believe his word,  
&emsp;<sup>25</sup> but murmured in their tents,  
&emsp;and didn’t listen to the LORD’s voice.  
<sup>26</sup> Therefore he swore to them  
&emsp;that he would overthrow them in the wilderness,  
&emsp;<sup>27</sup> that he would overthrow their offspring among the nations,  
&emsp;and scatter them in the lands.  
<sup>28</sup> They joined themselves also to Baal Peor,  
&emsp;and ate the sacrifices of the dead.  
<sup>29</sup> Thus they provoked him to anger with their deeds.  
&emsp;The plague broke in on them.  
<sup>30</sup> Then Phinehas stood up and executed judgment,  
&emsp;so the plague was stopped.  
<sup>31</sup> That was credited to him for righteousness,  
&emsp;for all generations to come.  
<sup>32</sup> They angered him also at the waters of Meribah,  
&emsp;so that Moses was troubled for their sakes;  
<sup>33</sup> because they were rebellious against his spirit,  
&emsp;he spoke rashly with his lips.  
<sup>34</sup> They didn’t destroy the peoples,  
&emsp;as the LORD commanded them,  
&emsp;<sup>35</sup> but mixed themselves with the nations,  
&emsp;and learned their works.  
<sup>36</sup> They served their idols,  
&emsp;which became a snare to them.  
<sup>37</sup> Yes, they sacrificed their sons and their daughters to demons.  
&emsp;<sup>38</sup> They shed innocent blood,  
&emsp;even the blood of their sons and of their daughters,  
&emsp;whom they sacrificed to the idols of Canaan.  
&emsp;The land was polluted with blood.  
<sup>39</sup> Thus they were defiled with their works,  
&emsp;and prostituted themselves in their deeds.  
<sup>40</sup> Therefore the LORD burned with anger against his people.  
&emsp;He abhorred his inheritance.  
<sup>41</sup> He gave them into the hand of the nations.  
&emsp;Those who hated them ruled over them.  
<sup>42</sup> Their enemies also oppressed them.  
&emsp;They were brought into subjection under their hand.  
<sup>43</sup> He rescued them many times,  
&emsp;but they ~~were rebellious in their counsel~~ <ins>provoked him with their counsel</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c361"></a>[c361](#c361)</sup>,  
&emsp;and were brought low in their iniquity.  
<sup>44</sup> Nevertheless he regarded their distress,  
&emsp;when he heard their cry.  
<sup>45</sup> He remembered for them his covenant,  
&emsp;and repented according to the multitude of his loving kindnesses.  
<sup>46</sup> He made them also to be pitied  
&emsp;by all those who carried them captive.

<sup>47</sup> Save us, LORD, our God,  
&emsp;gather us from among the nations,  
&emsp;to give thanks to your holy name,  
&emsp;to triumph in your praise!

<sup>48</sup> Blessed be the LORD, the God of Israel,  
&emsp;from everlasting even to everlasting!  
Let all the people say, “Amen.”  
&emsp;Praise the LORD![^55]

## Psalm 107

**BOOK 5**

<sup>1</sup> Give thanks to the LORD,[^56] for he is good,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>2</sup> Let the redeemed by the LORD say so,  
&emsp;whom he has redeemed from the hand of the adversary,  
&emsp;<sup>3</sup> and gathered out of the lands,  
&emsp;from the east and from the west,  
&emsp;from the north and from the south.

<sup>4</sup> They wandered in the wilderness in a desert way.  
&emsp;They found no city to live in.  
<sup>5</sup> Hungry and thirsty,  
&emsp;their soul fainted in them.  
<sup>6</sup> Then they cried to the LORD in their trouble,  
&emsp;and he delivered them out of their distresses.  
<sup>7</sup> He led them also by a straight way,  
&emsp;that they might go to a city to live in.  
<sup>8</sup> Let them praise the LORD for his loving kindness,  
&emsp;for his wonderful deeds to the children of men!

<sup>9</sup> For he satisfies the ~~longing~~ <ins>thirsting</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c362"></a>[c362](#c362)</sup> soul.  
&emsp;He fills the hungry soul with good.

<sup>10</sup> Some sat in darkness and in the shadow of death,  
&emsp;being bound in affliction and iron,  
&emsp;<sup>11</sup> because they rebelled against the words of God,[^57]  
&emsp;and condemned the counsel of the Most High.  
<sup>12</sup> Therefore he brought down their heart with labor.  
&emsp;They fell down, and there was no one to help.  
<sup>13</sup> Then they cried to the LORD in their trouble,  
&emsp;and he saved them out of their distresses.  
<sup>14</sup> He brought them out of darkness and the shadow of death,  
&emsp;and broke away their chains.  
<sup>15</sup> Let them praise the LORD for his loving kindness,  
&emsp;for his wonderful deeds to the children of men!

<sup>16</sup> For he has broken the gates of bronze,  
&emsp;and ~~cut through~~ <ins>shattered</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c363"></a>[c363](#c363)</sup> bars of iron.

<sup>17</sup> Fools are afflicted because of their disobedience,  
&emsp;and because of their iniquities.  
<sup>18</sup> Their soul abhors all kinds of food.  
&emsp;They draw near to the gates of death.  
<sup>19</sup> Then they cry to the LORD in their trouble,  
&emsp;and he saves them out of their distresses.  
<sup>20</sup> He sends his word, and heals them,  
&emsp;and delivers them from their graves.  
<sup>21</sup> Let them praise the LORD for his loving kindness,  
&emsp;for his wonderful deeds to the children of men!

<sup>22</sup> Let them offer the sacrifices of thanksgiving,  
&emsp;and declare his deeds with singing.

<sup>23</sup> Those who go down to the sea in ships,  
&emsp;who do business in great waters,  
&emsp;<sup>24</sup> these see the LORD’s deeds,  
&emsp;and his wonders in the deep.  
<sup>25</sup> For he commands, and raises the stormy wind,  
&emsp;which lifts up its waves.  
<sup>26</sup> They mount up to the sky; they go down again to the depths.  
&emsp;Their soul melts away because of trouble.  
<sup>27</sup> They reel back and forth, and stagger like a drunken man,  
&emsp;and are at their wits’ end.  
<sup>28</sup> Then they cry to the LORD in their trouble,  
&emsp;and he brings them out of their distress.  
<sup>29</sup> He makes the storm a calm,  
&emsp;so that its waves are still.  
<sup>30</sup> Then they are glad because it is calm,  
&emsp;so he brings them to their desired haven.  
<sup>31</sup> Let them praise the LORD for his loving kindness,  
&emsp;for his wonderful deeds for the children of men!

<sup>32</sup> Let them exalt him also in the assembly of the people,  
&emsp;and praise him in the seat of the elders.

<sup>33</sup> He turns rivers into a desert,  
&emsp;water springs into a thirsty ground,  
&emsp;<sup>34</sup> and a fruitful land into a salt waste,  
&emsp;for the wickedness of those who dwell in it.  
<sup>35</sup> He turns a desert into a pool of water,  
&emsp;and a dry land into water springs.  
<sup>36</sup> There he makes the hungry live,  
&emsp;that they may prepare a city to live in,  
&emsp;<sup>37</sup> sow fields, plant vineyards,  
&emsp;and reap the fruits of increase.  
<sup>38</sup> He blesses them also, so that they are multiplied greatly.  
&emsp;He doesn’t allow their livestock to decrease.  
<sup>39</sup> Again, they are diminished and bowed down  
&emsp;through oppression, trouble, and sorrow.  
<sup>40</sup> He pours contempt on princes,  
&emsp;and causes them to wander in a trackless waste.  
<sup>41</sup> Yet he lifts the needy out of their affliction,  
&emsp;and increases their families like a flock.  
<sup>42</sup> The upright will see it, and be glad.  
&emsp;All the wicked will shut their mouths.  
<sup>43</sup> Whoever is wise will pay attention to these things.  
&emsp;They will consider the loving kindnesses of the LORD.

## Psalm 108

*A Song. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> My heart is steadfast, God.  
&emsp;I will sing and I will make music with my soul.  
<sup>2</sup> Wake up, harp and lyre!  
&emsp;I will wake up the dawn.  
<sup>3</sup> I will give thanks to you, LORD, among the nations.  
&emsp;I will sing praises to you among the peoples.  
<sup>4</sup> For your loving kindness is great above the heavens.  
&emsp;Your faithfulness reaches to the skies.  
<sup>5</sup> Be exalted, God, above the heavens!  
&emsp;Let your glory be over all the earth.  
<sup>6</sup> That your beloved may be delivered,  
&emsp;save with your right hand, and answer us.  
<sup>7</sup> God has spoken from his sanctuary: “In triumph,  
&emsp;I will divide Shechem, and measure out the valley of Succoth.  
<sup>8</sup> Gilead is mine. Manasseh is mine.  
&emsp;Ephraim also is my helmet.  
&emsp;Judah is my scepter.  
<sup>9</sup> Moab is my wash pot.  
&emsp;I will toss my sandal on Edom.  
&emsp;I will shout over Philistia.”  
<sup>10</sup> Who will bring me into the fortified city?  
&emsp;Who will lead me to Edom?  
<sup>11</sup> Haven’t you rejected us, God?  
&emsp;You don’t go out, God, with our armies.  
<sup>12</sup> Give us help against the enemy,  
&emsp;for the help of man is vain.  
<sup>13</sup> Through God, we will do valiantly,  
&emsp;for it is he who will tread down our enemies.

## Psalm 109

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> God of my praise, don’t remain silent,  
&emsp;<sup>2</sup> for they have opened the mouth of the wicked and the mouth of deceit against me.  
&emsp;They have spoken to me with a lying tongue.  
<sup>3</sup> They have also surrounded me with words of hatred,  
&emsp;and fought against me without a cause.  
<sup>4</sup> In return for my love, they are my adversaries;  
&emsp;but I am in prayer.  
<sup>5</sup> They have rewarded me evil for good,  
&emsp;and hatred for my love.  
<sup>6</sup> Set a wicked man over him.  
&emsp;Let an adversary stand at his right hand.  
<sup>7</sup> When he is judged, let him come out guilty.  
&emsp;Let his prayer be turned into sin.  
<sup>8</sup> Let his days be few.  
&emsp;Let another take his office.  
<sup>9</sup> Let his children be fatherless,  
&emsp;and his wife a widow.  
<sup>10</sup> Let his children be wandering beggars.  
&emsp;Let them be sought from their ruins.  
<sup>11</sup> Let the creditor seize all that he has.  
&emsp;Let strangers plunder the fruit of his labor.  
<sup>12</sup> Let there be no one to extend kindness to him,  
&emsp;neither let there be anyone to have pity on his fatherless children.  
<sup>13</sup> Let his posterity be cut off.  
&emsp;In the generation following let their name be blotted out.  
<sup>14</sup> Let the iniquity of his fathers be remembered by the LORD.  
&emsp;Don’t let the sin of his mother be blotted out.  
<sup>15</sup> Let them be before the LORD continually,  
&emsp;that he may cut off their memory from the earth;  
<sup>16</sup> because he didn’t remember to show kindness,  
&emsp;but persecuted the poor and needy man,  
&emsp;the broken in heart, to kill them.  
<sup>17</sup> Yes, he loved cursing, and it came to him.  
&emsp;He didn’t delight in blessing, and it was far from him.  
<sup>18</sup> He clothed himself also with cursing as with his garment.  
&emsp;It came into his inward parts like water,  
&emsp;like oil into his bones.  
<sup>19</sup> Let it be to him as the clothing with which he covers himself,  
&emsp;for the belt that is always around him.  
<sup>20</sup> This is the reward of my adversaries from the LORD,  
&emsp;of those who speak evil against my soul.

<sup>21</sup> But deal with me, GOD the Lord,[^58] for your name’s sake,  
&emsp;because your loving kindness is good, deliver me;  
&emsp;<sup>22</sup> for I am poor and needy.  
&emsp;My heart is wounded within me.  
<sup>23</sup> I fade away like an evening shadow.  
&emsp;I am shaken off like a locust.  
<sup>24</sup> My knees are weak through fasting.  
&emsp;My body is thin and ~~lacks fat~~ <ins>lacks oil</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c364"></a>[c364](#c364)</sup>.  
<sup>25</sup> I have also become a reproach to them.  
&emsp;When they see me, they shake their head.  
<sup>26</sup> Help me, LORD, my God.  
&emsp;Save me according to your loving kindness;  
<sup>27</sup> that they may know that this is your hand;  
&emsp;that you, LORD, have done it.  
<sup>28</sup> They may curse, but you bless.  
&emsp;When they arise, they will be shamed,  
&emsp;but your servant shall rejoice.  
<sup>29</sup> Let my adversaries be clothed with dishonor.  
&emsp;Let them cover themselves with their own shame as with a robe.  
<sup>30</sup> I will give great thanks to the LORD with my mouth.  
&emsp;Yes, I will praise him among the multitude.  
<sup>31</sup> For he will stand at the right hand of the needy,  
&emsp;to save him from those who judge his soul.

## Psalm 110

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> The LORD says to my Lord, “Sit at my right hand,  
&emsp;until I make your enemies your footstool for your feet.”  
<sup>2</sup> The LORD will send out the rod of your strength out of Zion.  
&emsp;Rule among your enemies.  
<sup>3</sup> ~~Your people offer themselves willingly~~ <ins>Yours is princely rule</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c365"></a>[c365](#c365)</sup> in the day of your power, ~~in holy array~~ <ins>in holy splendors</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c366"></a>[c366](#c366)</sup>.  
&emsp;~~Out of the womb of the morning, you have the dew of your youth.~~ <ins>Out of the womb, before the morning star, I have begotten you.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c367"></a>[c367](#c367)</sup>  
<sup>4</sup> The LORD has sworn, and will not change his mind:  
&emsp;“You are a priest forever in the order of Melchizedek.”  
<sup>5</sup> The Lord is at your right hand.  
&emsp;He will crush kings in the day of his wrath.  
<sup>6</sup> He will judge among the nations.  
&emsp;He will heap up dead bodies.  
&emsp;He will crush the ruler of the whole earth.  
<sup>7</sup> He will drink of the brook on the way;  
&emsp;therefore he will lift up his head.

## Psalm 111

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^59]  
&emsp;I will give thanks to the LORD[^60] with my whole heart,  
&emsp;in the council of the upright, and in the congregation.  
<sup>2</sup> The LORD’s works are great,  
&emsp;pondered by all those who delight in them.  
<sup>3</sup> His work is honor and majesty.  
&emsp;His righteousness endures forever.  
<sup>4</sup> He has caused his wonderful works to be remembered.  
&emsp;The LORD is gracious and merciful.  
<sup>5</sup> He has given food to those who fear him.  
&emsp;He always remembers his covenant.  
<sup>6</sup> He has ~~shown~~ <ins>declared to</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c368"></a>[c368](#c368)</sup> his people the power of his works,  
&emsp;in giving them the heritage of the nations.  
<sup>7</sup> The works of his hands are truth and justice.  
&emsp;All his precepts are sure.  
<sup>8</sup> They are established forever and ever.  
&emsp;They are done in truth and uprightness.  
<sup>9</sup> He has sent redemption to his people.  
&emsp;He has ordained his covenant forever.  
&emsp;His name is holy and awesome!  
<sup>10</sup> The fear of the LORD is the beginning of wisdom.  
&emsp;All those who do his work have a good understanding.  
His praise endures forever!

## Psalm 112

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^61]  
&emsp;Blessed is the man who fears the LORD,[^62]  
&emsp;who delights greatly in his commandments.  
<sup>2</sup> His offspring will be mighty in the land.  
&emsp;The generation of the upright will be blessed.  
<sup>3</sup> Wealth and riches are in his house.  
&emsp;His righteousness endures forever.  
<sup>4</sup> Light dawns in the darkness for the upright,  
&emsp;gracious, merciful, and righteous.  
<sup>5</sup> It is well with the man who deals graciously and lends.  
&emsp;He will maintain his cause in judgment.  
<sup>6</sup> For he will never be shaken.  
&emsp;The righteous will be remembered forever.  
<sup>7</sup> He will not be afraid of evil news.  
&emsp;His heart is steadfast, trusting in the LORD.  
<sup>8</sup> His heart is established.  
&emsp;He will not be afraid in the end when he sees his adversaries.  
<sup>9</sup> He has dispersed, he has given to the poor.  
&emsp;His righteousness endures forever.  
&emsp;His horn will be exalted with honor.  
<sup>10</sup> The wicked will see it, and be grieved.  
&emsp;He shall gnash with his teeth, and melt away.  
&emsp;The desire of the wicked will perish.

## Psalm 113

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^63]  
&emsp;Praise, you servants of the LORD,[^64]  
&emsp;praise the LORD’s name.  
<sup>2</sup> Blessed be the LORD’s name,  
&emsp;from this time forward and forever more.  
<sup>3</sup> From the rising of the sun to its going down,  
&emsp;the LORD’s name is to be praised.  
<sup>4</sup> The LORD is high above all nations,  
&emsp;his glory above the heavens.  
<sup>5</sup> Who is like the LORD, our God,  
&emsp;who has his seat on high,  
&emsp;<sup>6</sup> who stoops down to see in heaven and on the earth?  
<sup>7</sup> He raises up the poor out of the dust,  
&emsp;and lifts up the needy from the ash heap,  
<sup>8</sup> that he may set him with princes,  
&emsp;even with the princes of his people.  
<sup>9</sup> He settles the barren woman in her home  
&emsp;as a joyful mother of children.  
Praise the LORD![^65]

## Psalm 114

<sup>1</sup> When Israel went out of Egypt,  
&emsp;the house of Jacob from a people of foreign language,  
<sup>2</sup> Judah became his sanctuary,  
&emsp;Israel his dominion.  
<sup>3</sup> The sea saw it, and fled.  
&emsp;The Jordan was driven back.  
<sup>4</sup> The mountains skipped like rams,  
&emsp;the little hills like lambs.  
<sup>5</sup> What was it, you sea, that you fled?  
&emsp;You Jordan, that you turned back?  
<sup>6</sup> You mountains, that you skipped like rams?  
&emsp;You little hills, like lambs?  
<sup>7</sup> Tremble, you earth, at the presence of the Lord,  
&emsp;at the presence of the God of Jacob,  
<sup>8</sup> who turned the rock into a pool of water,  
&emsp;the flint into a spring of waters.

## Psalm 115

<sup>1</sup> Not to us, LORD,[^66] not to us,  
&emsp;but to your name give glory,  
&emsp;for your loving kindness, and for your truth’s sake.  
<sup>2</sup> Why should the nations say,  
&emsp;“Where is their God, now?”  
<sup>3</sup> But our God is in the heavens.  
&emsp;He does whatever he pleases.  
<sup>4</sup> Their idols are silver and gold,  
&emsp;the work of men’s hands.  
<sup>5</sup> They have mouths, but they don’t speak.  
&emsp;They have eyes, but they don’t see.  
<sup>6</sup> They have ears, but they don’t hear.  
&emsp;They have noses, but they don’t smell.  
<sup>7</sup> They have hands, but they don’t feel.  
&emsp;They have feet, but they don’t walk,  
&emsp;neither do they speak through their throat.  
<sup>8</sup> Those who make them will be like them;  
&emsp;yes, everyone who trusts in them.  
<sup>9</sup> ~~Israel, trust in the LORD!~~ <ins>The house of Israel has hoped in the LORD.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c369"></a>[c369](#c369)</sup>  
&emsp;He is their help and their shield.  
<sup>10</sup> ~~House of Aaron, trust in the LORD!~~ <ins>The house of Aaron has hoped in the LORD.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c370"></a>[c370](#c370)</sup>  
&emsp;He is their help and their shield.  
<sup>11</sup> ~~You who fear the LORD, trust in the LORD!~~ <ins>Those who fear the LORD have hoped in the LORD.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c371"></a>[c371](#c371)</sup>  
&emsp;He is their help and their shield.  
<sup>12</sup> The LORD remembers us. He will bless us.  
&emsp;He will bless the house of Israel.  
&emsp;He will bless the house of Aaron.  
<sup>13</sup> He will bless those who fear the LORD,  
&emsp;both small and great.  
<sup>14</sup> May the LORD increase you more and more,  
&emsp;you and your children.  
<sup>15</sup> Blessed are you by the LORD,  
&emsp;who made heaven and earth.  
<sup>16</sup> The heavens are the LORD’s heavens,  
&emsp;but he has given the earth to the children of men.  
<sup>17</sup> The dead don’t praise the LORD,[^67]  
&emsp;nor any who go down into silence,  
<sup>18</sup> but ~~we will bless~~ <ins>we who live will bless</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c372"></a>[c372](#c372)</sup> the LORD,[^68]  
&emsp;from this time forward and forever more.  
Praise the LORD![^69]

## Psalm 116

<sup>1</sup> I love the LORD,[^70] because he listens to my voice,  
&emsp;and my cries for mercy.  
<sup>2</sup> Because he has turned his ear to me,  
&emsp;therefore I will call on him as long as I live.  
<sup>3</sup> The cords of death surrounded me,  
&emsp;the pains of Sheol[^71] got a hold of me.  
&emsp;I found trouble and sorrow.  
<sup>4</sup> Then I called on the LORD’s name:  
&emsp;“LORD, I beg you, deliver my soul.”  
<sup>5</sup> The LORD is gracious and righteous.  
&emsp;Yes, our God is merciful.  
<sup>6</sup> The LORD preserves the simple.  
&emsp;I was brought low, and he saved me.  
<sup>7</sup> Return to your rest, my soul,  
&emsp;for the LORD has dealt bountifully with you.  
<sup>8</sup> For you have delivered my soul from death,  
&emsp;my eyes from tears,  
&emsp;and my feet from falling.  
<sup>9</sup> I will walk before the LORD in the land of the living.  
<sup>10</sup> I believed, therefore I said,  
&emsp;“I was greatly afflicted.”  
<sup>11</sup> I said in my haste,  
&emsp;“All people are liars.”  
<sup>12</sup> What will I give to the LORD for all his benefits toward me?  
&emsp;<sup>13</sup> I will take the cup of salvation, and call on the LORD’s name.  
<sup>14</sup> I will pay my vows to the LORD,  
&emsp;yes, in the presence of all his people.  
<sup>15</sup> Precious in the LORD’s sight is the death of his saints.  
<sup>16</sup> LORD, truly I am your servant.  
&emsp;I am your servant, the son of your servant girl.  
&emsp;You have freed me from my chains.  
<sup>17</sup> I will offer to you the sacrifice of thanksgiving,  
&emsp;and will call on the LORD’s name.  
<sup>18</sup> I will pay my vows to the LORD,  
&emsp;yes, in the presence of all his people,  
<sup>19</sup> in the courts of the LORD’s house,  
&emsp;in the middle of you, Jerusalem.  
Praise the LORD![^72]

## Psalm 117

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD,[^73] all you nations!  
&emsp;Extol him, all you peoples!  
<sup>2</sup> For his loving kindness is great toward us.  
&emsp;The LORD’s faithfulness endures forever.  
Praise the LORD![^74]

## Psalm 118

<sup>1</sup> Give thanks to the LORD,[^75] for he is good,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>2</sup> Let Israel now say  
&emsp;that his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>3</sup> Let the house of Aaron now say  
&emsp;that his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>4</sup> Now let those who fear the LORD say  
&emsp;that his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>5</sup> Out of my distress, I called on the LORD.[^76]  
&emsp;The LORD[^77] answered me with freedom.  
<sup>6</sup> The LORD is on my side. I will not be afraid.  
&emsp;What can man do to me?  
<sup>7</sup> The LORD is on my side among those who help me.  
&emsp;Therefore I will look in triumph at those who hate me.  
<sup>8</sup> It is better to take refuge in the LORD,  
&emsp;than to put confidence in man.  
<sup>9</sup> It is better to take refuge in the LORD,  
&emsp;than to put confidence in princes.  
<sup>10</sup> All the nations surrounded me,  
&emsp;but in the LORD’s name I cut them off.  
<sup>11</sup> They surrounded me, yes, they surrounded me.  
&emsp;In the LORD’s name I indeed cut them off.  
<sup>12</sup> They surrounded me like bees.  
&emsp;They are quenched like the burning thorns.  
&emsp;In the LORD’s name I cut them off.  
<sup>13</sup> You pushed me back hard, to make me fall,  
&emsp;but the LORD helped me.  
<sup>14</sup> The LORD[^78] is my strength and song.  
&emsp;He has become my salvation.  
<sup>15</sup> The voice of rejoicing and salvation is in the tents of the righteous.  
&emsp;“The right hand of the LORD does valiantly.  
<sup>16</sup> The right hand of the LORD is exalted!  
&emsp;The right hand of the LORD does valiantly!”  
<sup>17</sup> I will not die, but live,  
&emsp;and declare the LORD’s works.  
<sup>18</sup> The LORD[^79] has punished me severely,  
&emsp;but he has not given me over to death.  
<sup>19</sup> Open to me the gates of righteousness.  
&emsp;I will enter into them.  
&emsp;I will give thanks to the LORD.[^80]  
<sup>20</sup> This is the gate of the LORD;  
&emsp;the righteous will enter into it.  
<sup>21</sup> I will give thanks to you, for you have answered me,  
&emsp;and have become my salvation.  
<sup>22</sup> The stone which the builders rejected  
&emsp;has become the cornerstone.[^81]  
<sup>23</sup> This is the LORD’s doing.  
&emsp;It is marvelous in our eyes.  
<sup>24</sup> This is the day that the LORD has made.  
&emsp;We will rejoice and be glad in it!  
<sup>25</sup> Save us now, we beg you, LORD!  
&emsp;LORD, we beg you, send prosperity now.  
<sup>26</sup> Blessed is he who comes in the LORD’s name!  
&emsp;We have blessed you out of the LORD’s house.  
<sup>27</sup> The LORD is God, and he has given us light.  
&emsp;~~Bind the sacrifice with cords~~ <ins>Arrange the festal procession with leafy branches</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c373"></a>[c373](#c373)</sup>, even to the horns of the altar.  
<sup>28</sup> You are my God, and I will give thanks to you.  
&emsp;You are my God, I will exalt you.  
<sup>29</sup> Oh give thanks to the LORD, for he is good,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.

## Psalm 119

*ALEPH*

<sup>1</sup> Blessed are those whose ways are blameless,  
&emsp;who walk according to the LORD’s law.  
<sup>2</sup> Blessed are those who keep his statutes,  
&emsp;who seek him with their whole heart.  
<sup>3</sup> Yes, they do nothing wrong.  
&emsp;They walk in his ways.  
<sup>4</sup> You have commanded your precepts,  
&emsp;that we should fully obey them.  
<sup>5</sup> Oh that my ways were steadfast  
&emsp;to obey your statutes!  
<sup>6</sup> Then I wouldn’t be disappointed,  
&emsp;when I consider all of your commandments.  
<sup>7</sup> I will give thanks to you with uprightness of heart,  
&emsp;when I learn your righteous judgments.  
<sup>8</sup> I will observe your statutes.  
&emsp;Don’t utterly forsake me.

*BETH*

<sup>9</sup> How can a young man ~~keep his way pure~~ <ins>cleanse his way</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c374"></a>[c374](#c374)</sup>?  
&emsp;By living according to your word.  
<sup>10</sup> With my whole heart I have sought you.  
&emsp;Don’t let me wander from your commandments.  
<sup>11</sup> I have hidden your word in my heart,  
&emsp;that I might not sin against you.  
<sup>12</sup> Blessed are you, LORD.  
&emsp;Teach me your statutes.  
<sup>13</sup> With my lips,  
&emsp;I have declared all the ordinances of your mouth.  
<sup>14</sup> I have rejoiced in the way of your testimonies,  
&emsp;as much as in all riches.  
<sup>15</sup> I will meditate on your precepts,  
&emsp;and consider your ways.  
<sup>16</sup> I will delight myself in your statutes.  
&emsp;I will not forget your word.

*GIMEL*

<sup>17</sup> Do good to your servant.  
&emsp;I will live and I will obey your word.  
<sup>18</sup> Open my eyes,  
&emsp;that I may see wondrous things out of your law.  
<sup>19</sup> I am a stranger on the earth.  
&emsp;Don’t hide your commandments from me.  
<sup>20</sup> My soul is consumed with longing for your ordinances at all times.  
<sup>21</sup> You have rebuked the proud who are cursed,  
&emsp;who wander from your commandments.  
<sup>22</sup> Take reproach and contempt away from me,  
&emsp;for I have kept your statutes.  
<sup>23</sup> Though princes ~~sit and slander me~~ <ins>sit and speak against me</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c375"></a>[c375](#c375)</sup>,  
&emsp;your servant will meditate on your statutes.  
<sup>24</sup> Indeed your statutes are my delight,  
&emsp;and my counselors.

*DALETH*

<sup>25</sup> My soul is laid low in the dust.  
&emsp;Revive me according to your word!  
<sup>26</sup> I declared my ways, and you answered me.  
&emsp;Teach me your statutes.  
<sup>27</sup> Let me understand the teaching of your precepts!  
&emsp;Then I will meditate on your wondrous works.  
<sup>28</sup> My soul ~~is weary~~ <ins>weeps</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c377"></a>[c377](#c377)</sup> with sorrow;  
&emsp;~~strengthen me~~ <ins>raise me up</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c376"></a>[c376](#c376)</sup> according to your word.  
<sup>29</sup> Keep me from the way of deceit.  
&emsp;Grant me your law graciously!  
<sup>30</sup> I have chosen the way of truth.  
&emsp;I have set your ordinances before me.  
<sup>31</sup> I cling to your statutes, LORD.  
&emsp;Don’t let me be disappointed.  
<sup>32</sup> I run in the path of your commandments,  
&emsp;for you have set my heart free.

*HE*

<sup>33</sup> Teach me, LORD, the way of your statutes.  
&emsp;I will keep them to the end.  
<sup>34</sup> Give me understanding, and I will keep your law.  
&emsp;Yes, I will obey it with my whole heart.  
<sup>35</sup> Direct me in the path of your commandments,  
&emsp;for I delight in them.  
<sup>36</sup> Turn my heart toward your statutes,  
&emsp;not toward selfish gain.  
<sup>37</sup> Turn my eyes away from looking at worthless things.  
&emsp;Revive me in your ways.  
<sup>38</sup> Fulfill your promise to your servant,  
&emsp;that you may be feared.  
<sup>39</sup> Take away my disgrace that I dread,  
&emsp;for your ordinances are good.  
<sup>40</sup> Behold, I long for your precepts!  
&emsp;Revive me in your righteousness.

*VAV*

<sup>41</sup> Let your loving kindness also come to me, LORD,  
&emsp;your salvation, according to your word.  
<sup>42</sup> So I will have an answer for him who reproaches me,  
&emsp;for I trust in your word.  
<sup>43</sup> Don’t snatch the word of truth out of my mouth,  
&emsp;for I put my hope in your ordinances.  
<sup>44</sup> So I will obey your law continually,  
&emsp;forever and ever.  
<sup>45</sup> I will walk in liberty,  
&emsp;for I have sought your precepts.  
<sup>46</sup> I will also speak of your statutes before kings,  
&emsp;and will not be disappointed.  
<sup>47</sup> I will delight myself in your commandments,  
&emsp;because I love them.  
<sup>48</sup> I reach out my hands for your commandments, which I love.  
&emsp;I will meditate on your statutes.

*ZAYIN*

<sup>49</sup> Remember your word to your servant,  
&emsp;because you gave me hope.  
<sup>50</sup> This is my comfort in my affliction,  
&emsp;for your word has revived me.  
<sup>51</sup> The arrogant mock me excessively,  
&emsp;but I don’t swerve from your law.  
<sup>52</sup> I remember your ordinances of old, LORD,  
&emsp;and have comforted myself.  
<sup>53</sup> Indignation has taken hold on me,  
&emsp;because of the wicked who forsake your law.  
<sup>54</sup> Your statutes have been my songs  
&emsp;in the house where I live.  
<sup>55</sup> I have remembered your name, LORD, in the night,  
&emsp;and I obey your law.  
<sup>56</sup> This is my way,  
&emsp;that I keep your precepts.

*HETH*

<sup>57</sup> The LORD is my portion.  
&emsp;I promised to obey your words.  
<sup>58</sup> I sought your favor with my whole heart.  
&emsp;Be merciful to me according to your word.  
<sup>59</sup> I considered my ways,  
&emsp;and turned my steps to your statutes.  
<sup>60</sup> I will hurry, and not delay,  
&emsp;to obey your commandments.  
<sup>61</sup> The ropes of the wicked bind me,  
&emsp;but I won’t forget your law.  
<sup>62</sup> At midnight I will rise to give thanks to you,  
&emsp;because of your righteous ordinances.  
<sup>63</sup> I am a friend of all those who fear you,  
&emsp;of those who observe your precepts.  
<sup>64</sup> The earth is full of your loving kindness, LORD.  
&emsp;Teach me your statutes.

*TETH*

<sup>65</sup> You have treated your servant well,  
&emsp;according to your word, LORD.  
<sup>66</sup> Teach me good judgment and knowledge,  
&emsp;for I believe in your commandments.  
<sup>67</sup> Before I was afflicted, I went astray;  
&emsp;but now I observe your word.  
<sup>68</sup> You are good, and do good.  
&emsp;Teach me your statutes.  
<sup>69</sup> The proud have smeared a lie upon me.  
&emsp;With my whole heart, I will keep your precepts.  
<sup>70</sup> Their heart is as callous as the fat,  
&emsp;but I delight in your law.  
<sup>71</sup> It is good for me that I have been afflicted,  
&emsp;that I may learn your statutes.  
<sup>72</sup> The law of your mouth is better to me than thousands of pieces of gold and silver.

*YODH*

<sup>73</sup> Your hands have made me and formed me.  
&emsp;Give me understanding, that I may learn your commandments.  
<sup>74</sup> Those who fear you will see me and be glad,  
&emsp;because I have put my hope in your word.  
<sup>75</sup> LORD, I know that your judgments are righteous,  
&emsp;that in faithfulness you have afflicted me.  
<sup>76</sup> Please let your loving kindness be for my comfort,  
&emsp;according to your word to your servant.  
<sup>77</sup> Let your tender mercies come to me, that I may live;  
&emsp;for your law is my delight.  
<sup>78</sup> Let the proud be disappointed, for they have overthrown me wrongfully.  
&emsp;I will meditate on your precepts.  
<sup>79</sup> Let those who fear you turn to me.  
&emsp;They will know your statutes.  
<sup>80</sup> Let my heart be blameless toward your decrees,  
&emsp;that I may not be disappointed.

*KAPF*

<sup>81</sup> My soul faints for your salvation.  
&emsp;I hope in your word.  
<sup>82</sup> My eyes fail for your word.  
&emsp;I say, “When will you comfort me?”  
<sup>83</sup> For I have become like a wineskin in the smoke.  
&emsp;I don’t forget your statutes.  
<sup>84</sup> How many are the days of your servant?  
&emsp;When will you execute judgment on those who persecute me?  
<sup>85</sup> The proud have dug pits for me,  
&emsp;contrary to your law.  
<sup>86</sup> All of your commandments are faithful.  
&emsp;They persecute me wrongfully.  
&emsp;Help me!  
<sup>87</sup> They had almost wiped me from the earth,  
&emsp;but I didn’t forsake your precepts.  
<sup>88</sup> Preserve my life according to your loving kindness,  
&emsp;so I will obey the statutes of your mouth.

*LAMEDH*

<sup>89</sup> LORD, your word is settled in heaven forever.  
<sup>90</sup> Your faithfulness is to all generations.  
&emsp;You have established the earth, and it remains.  
<sup>91</sup> Your laws remain to this day,  
&emsp;for all things serve you.  
<sup>92</sup> Unless your law had been my delight,  
&emsp;I would have perished in my affliction.  
<sup>93</sup> I will never forget your precepts,  
&emsp;for with them, you have revived me.  
<sup>94</sup> I am yours.  
&emsp;Save me, for I have sought your precepts.  
<sup>95</sup> The wicked have waited for me, to destroy me.  
&emsp;I will consider your statutes.  
<sup>96</sup> I have seen a limit to all perfection,  
&emsp;but your commands are boundless.

*MEM*

<sup>97</sup> How I love your law!  
&emsp;It is my meditation all day.  
<sup>98</sup> Your commandments make me wiser than my enemies,  
&emsp;for your commandments are always with me.  
<sup>99</sup> I have more understanding than all my teachers,  
&emsp;for your testimonies are my meditation.  
<sup>100</sup> I understand more than the aged,  
&emsp;because I have kept your precepts.  
<sup>101</sup> I have kept my feet from every evil way,  
&emsp;that I might observe your word.  
<sup>102</sup> I have not turned away from your ordinances,  
&emsp;for you have taught me.  
<sup>103</sup> How sweet are your promises to my taste,  
&emsp;more than honey to my mouth!  
<sup>104</sup> Through your precepts, I get understanding;  
&emsp;therefore I hate every false way.

*NUN*

<sup>105</sup> Your word is a lamp to my feet,  
&emsp;and a light for my path.  
<sup>106</sup> I have sworn, and have confirmed it,  
&emsp;that I will obey your righteous ordinances.  
<sup>107</sup> I am afflicted very much.  
&emsp;Revive me, LORD, according to your word.  
<sup>108</sup> Accept, I beg you, the willing offerings of my mouth.  
&emsp;LORD, teach me your ordinances.  
<sup>109</sup> My soul is continually in my hand,  
&emsp;yet I won’t forget your law.  
<sup>110</sup> The wicked have laid a snare for me,  
&emsp;yet I haven’t gone astray from your precepts.  
<sup>111</sup> I have taken your testimonies as a heritage forever,  
&emsp;for they are the joy of my heart.  
<sup>112</sup> I have set my heart to perform your statutes forever,  
&emsp;even to the end.

*SAMEKH*

<sup>113</sup> I hate double-minded men,  
&emsp;but I love your law.  
<sup>114</sup> You are my hiding place and my shield.  
&emsp;I hope in your word.  
<sup>115</sup> Depart from me, you evildoers,  
&emsp;that I may keep the commandments of my God.  
<sup>116</sup> Uphold me according to your word, that I may live.  
&emsp;Let me not be ashamed of my hope.  
<sup>117</sup> Hold me up, and I will be safe,  
&emsp;and will ~~have respect for~~ <ins>delight in</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c378"></a>[c378](#c378)</sup> your statutes continually.  
<sup>118</sup> You reject all those who stray from your statutes,  
&emsp;for ~~their deceit is in vain~~ <ins>their thought is a lie</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c379"></a>[c379](#c379)</sup>.  
<sup>119</sup> You put away all the wicked of the earth like dross.  
&emsp;Therefore I love your testimonies.  
<sup>120</sup> My flesh trembles for fear of you.  
&emsp;I am afraid of your judgments.

*AYIN*

<sup>121</sup> I have done what is just and righteous.  
&emsp;Don’t leave me to my oppressors.  
<sup>122</sup> Ensure your servant’s well-being.  
&emsp;Don’t let the proud oppress me.  
<sup>123</sup> My eyes fail looking for your salvation,  
&emsp;for your righteous word.  
<sup>124</sup> Deal with your servant according to your loving kindness.  
&emsp;Teach me your statutes.  
<sup>125</sup> I am your servant. Give me understanding,  
&emsp;that I may know your testimonies.  
<sup>126</sup> It is time to act, LORD,  
&emsp;for they break your law.  
<sup>127</sup> Therefore I love your commandments more than gold,  
&emsp;yes, more than pure gold.  
<sup>128</sup> Therefore I consider all of your precepts to be right.  
&emsp;I hate every false way.

*PE*

<sup>129</sup> Your testimonies are wonderful,  
&emsp;therefore my soul keeps them.  
<sup>130</sup> The entrance of your words gives light.  
&emsp;It gives understanding to the simple.  
<sup>131</sup> I opened my mouth wide and panted,  
&emsp;for I longed for your commandments.  
<sup>132</sup> Turn to me, and have mercy on me,  
&emsp;as you always do to those who love your name.  
<sup>133</sup> Establish my footsteps in your word.  
&emsp;Don’t let any iniquity have dominion over me.  
<sup>134</sup> Redeem me from the oppression of man,  
&emsp;so I will observe your precepts.  
<sup>135</sup> Make your face shine on your servant.  
&emsp;Teach me your statutes.  
<sup>136</sup> Streams of tears run down my eyes,  
&emsp;because they don’t observe your law.

*TZADHE*

<sup>137</sup> You are righteous, LORD.  
&emsp;Your judgments are upright.  
<sup>138</sup> You have commanded your statutes in righteousness.  
&emsp;They are fully trustworthy.  
<sup>139</sup> My zeal wears me out,  
&emsp;because my enemies ignore your words.  
<sup>140</sup> Your promises have been thoroughly tested,  
&emsp;and your servant loves them.  
<sup>141</sup> I am small and despised.  
&emsp;I don’t forget your precepts.  
<sup>142</sup> Your righteousness is an everlasting righteousness.  
&emsp;Your law is truth.  
<sup>143</sup> Trouble and anguish have taken hold of me.  
&emsp;Your commandments are my delight.  
<sup>144</sup> Your testimonies are righteous forever.  
&emsp;Give me understanding, that I may live.

*QOPH*

<sup>145</sup> I have called with my whole heart.  
&emsp;Answer me, LORD!  
&emsp;I will keep your statutes.  
<sup>146</sup> I have called to you. Save me!  
&emsp;I will obey your statutes.  
<sup>147</sup> I rise before dawn and cry for help.  
&emsp;I put my hope in your words.  
<sup>148</sup> My eyes stay open through the night watches,  
&emsp;that I might meditate on your word.  
<sup>149</sup> Hear my voice according to your loving kindness.  
&emsp;Revive me, LORD, according to your ordinances.  
<sup>150</sup> They draw near who follow after wickedness.  
&emsp;They are far from your law.  
<sup>151</sup> You are near, LORD.  
&emsp;All your commandments are truth.  
<sup>152</sup> Of old I have known from your testimonies,  
&emsp;that you have founded them forever.

*RESH*

<sup>153</sup> Consider my affliction, and deliver me,  
&emsp;for I don’t forget your law.  
<sup>154</sup> Plead my cause, and redeem me!  
&emsp;Revive me according to your promise.  
<sup>155</sup> Salvation is far from the wicked,  
&emsp;for they don’t seek your statutes.  
<sup>156</sup> Great are your tender mercies, LORD.  
&emsp;Revive me according to your ordinances.  
<sup>157</sup> Many are my persecutors and my adversaries.  
&emsp;I haven’t swerved from your testimonies.  
<sup>158</sup> I look at the ~~faithless~~ <ins>transgressors</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c380"></a>[c380](#c380)</sup> with loathing,  
&emsp;because they don’t observe your word.  
<sup>159</sup> Consider how I love your precepts.  
&emsp;Revive me, LORD, according to your loving kindness.  
<sup>160</sup> ~~All of your words are truth.~~ <ins>The beginning of your words is truth.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c381"></a>[c381](#c381)</sup>  
&emsp;Every one of your righteous ordinances endures forever.

*SIN AND SHIN*

<sup>161</sup> Princes have persecuted me without a cause,  
&emsp;but my heart stands in awe of your words.  
<sup>162</sup> I rejoice at your word,  
&emsp;as one who finds great plunder.  
<sup>163</sup> I hate and abhor falsehood.  
&emsp;I love your law.  
<sup>164</sup> Seven times a day, I praise you,  
&emsp;because of your righteous ordinances.  
<sup>165</sup> Those who love your law have great peace.  
&emsp;Nothing causes them to stumble.  
<sup>166</sup> I have hoped for your salvation, LORD.  
&emsp;I have done your commandments.  
<sup>167</sup> My soul has observed your testimonies.  
&emsp;I love them exceedingly.  
<sup>168</sup> I have obeyed your precepts and your testimonies,  
&emsp;for all my ways are before you.

*TAV*

<sup>169</sup> Let my cry come before you, LORD.  
&emsp;Give me understanding according to your word.  
<sup>170</sup> Let my supplication come before you.  
&emsp;Deliver me according to your word.  
<sup>171</sup> Let my lips utter praise,  
&emsp;for you teach me your statutes.  
<sup>172</sup> Let my tongue sing of your word,  
&emsp;for all your commandments are righteousness.  
<sup>173</sup> Let your hand be ready to help me,  
&emsp;for I have chosen your precepts.  
<sup>174</sup> I have longed for your salvation, LORD.  
&emsp;Your law is my delight.  
<sup>175</sup> Let my soul live, that I may praise you.  
&emsp;Let your ordinances help me.  
<sup>176</sup> I have gone astray like a lost sheep.  
&emsp;Seek your servant, for I don’t forget your commandments.

## Psalm 120

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> In my distress, I cried to the LORD.  
&emsp;He answered me.  
<sup>2</sup> Deliver my soul, LORD, from lying lips,  
&emsp;from a deceitful tongue.  
<sup>3</sup> What will be given to you, and what will be done more to you,  
&emsp;you deceitful tongue?  
<sup>4</sup> Sharp arrows of the mighty,  
&emsp;with coals of juniper.  
<sup>5</sup> Woe is me, that I live in Meshech,  
&emsp;that I dwell among the tents of Kedar!  
<sup>6</sup> My soul has had her dwelling too long  
&emsp;with him who hates peace.  
<sup>7</sup> I am for peace,  
&emsp;but when I speak, they ~~are for war~~ <ins>attack me</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c382"></a>[c382](#c382)</sup>.

## Psalm 121

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> I will lift up my eyes to the hills.  
&emsp;Where does my help come from?  
<sup>2</sup> My help comes from the LORD,  
&emsp;who made heaven and earth.

<sup>3</sup> He will not allow your foot to be moved.  
&emsp;He who keeps you will not slumber.  
<sup>4</sup> Behold, he who keeps Israel  
&emsp;will neither slumber nor sleep.  
<sup>5</sup> The LORD is your keeper.  
&emsp;The LORD is your shade on your right hand.  
<sup>6</sup> The sun will not harm you by day,  
&emsp;nor the moon by night.  
<sup>7</sup> The LORD will keep you from all evil.  
&emsp;He will keep your soul.  
<sup>8</sup> The LORD will keep your going out and your coming in,  
&emsp;from this time forward, and forever more.

## Psalm 122

*A Song of Ascents. By David.*

<sup>1</sup> I was glad when they said to me,  
&emsp;“Let’s go to the LORD’s house!”  
<sup>2</sup> Our feet are standing within your gates, Jerusalem!  
&emsp;<sup>3</sup> Jerusalem is built as a city that is compact together,  
<sup>4</sup> where the tribes go up, even the LORD’s tribes,  
&emsp;according to an ordinance for Israel,  
&emsp;to give thanks to the LORD’s name.  
<sup>5</sup> For there are set thrones for judgment,  
&emsp;the thrones of David’s house.  
<sup>6</sup> Pray for the peace of Jerusalem.  
&emsp;Those who love you will prosper.  
<sup>7</sup> Peace be within your walls,  
&emsp;and prosperity within your palaces.  
<sup>8</sup> For my brothers’ and companions’ sakes,  
&emsp;I will now say, “Peace be within you.”  
<sup>9</sup> For the sake of the house of the LORD our God,  
&emsp;I will seek your good.

## Psalm 123

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> I lift up my eyes to you,  
&emsp;you who sit in the heavens.  
<sup>2</sup> Behold, as the eyes of servants look to the hand of their master,  
&emsp;as the eyes of a maid to the hand of her mistress,  
&emsp;so our eyes look to the LORD, our God,  
&emsp;until he has mercy on us.  
<sup>3</sup> Have mercy on us, LORD, have mercy on us,  
&emsp;for we have endured much contempt.  
<sup>4</sup> Our soul is exceedingly filled with the scoffing of ~~those who are at ease~~ <ins>the prosperous</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c383"></a>[c383](#c383)</sup>,  
&emsp;with the contempt of the proud.

## Psalm 124

*A Song of Ascents. By David.*

<sup>1</sup> If it had not been the LORD who was on our side,  
&emsp;let Israel now say,  
<sup>2</sup> if it had not been the LORD who was on our side,  
&emsp;when men rose up against us,  
<sup>3</sup> then they would have swallowed us up alive,  
&emsp;when their wrath was kindled against us,  
<sup>4</sup> then the waters would have overwhelmed us,  
&emsp;the stream would have gone over our soul.  
<sup>5</sup> Then the ~~proud~~ <ins>swelling</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c384"></a>[c384](#c384)</sup> waters would have gone over our soul.  
<sup>6</sup> Blessed be the LORD,  
&emsp;who has not given us as a prey to their teeth.  
<sup>7</sup> Our soul has escaped like a bird out of the fowler’s snare.  
&emsp;The snare is broken, and we have escaped.  
<sup>8</sup> Our help is in the LORD’s name,  
&emsp;who made heaven and earth.

## Psalm 125

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> Those who trust in the LORD are as Mount Zion,  
&emsp;which can’t be moved, but remains forever.  
<sup>2</sup> As the mountains surround Jerusalem,  
&emsp;so the LORD surrounds his people from this time forward and forever more.  
<sup>3</sup> For the scepter of wickedness won’t remain over the allotment of the righteous,  
&emsp;so that the righteous won’t use their hands to do evil.  
<sup>4</sup> Do good, LORD, to those who are good,  
&emsp;to those who are upright in their hearts.  
<sup>5</sup> But as for those who turn away to their crooked ways,  
&emsp;the LORD will lead them away with the workers of iniquity.  
Peace be on Israel.

## Psalm 126

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> When the LORD ~~brought back those who returned to Zion~~ <ins>restored the fortunes of Zion</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c385"></a>[c385](#c385)</sup>,  
&emsp;we were like those who dream.  
<sup>2</sup> Then our mouth was filled with laughter,  
&emsp;and our tongue with singing.  
Then they said among the nations,  
&emsp;“The LORD has done great things for them.”  
<sup>3</sup> The LORD has done great things for us,  
&emsp;and we are glad.  
<sup>4</sup> Restore our fortunes again, LORD,  
&emsp;like the streams in the Negev.  
<sup>5</sup> Those who sow in tears will reap in joy.  
&emsp;<sup>6</sup> He who goes out weeping, carrying seed for sowing,  
&emsp;will certainly come again with joy, carrying his sheaves.

## Psalm 127

*A Song of Ascents. By Solomon.*

<sup>1</sup> Unless the LORD builds the house,  
&emsp;they who build it labor in vain.  
Unless the LORD watches over the city,  
&emsp;the watchman guards it in vain.  
<sup>2</sup> It is vain for you to rise up early,  
&emsp;to stay up late,  
&emsp;eating the bread of toil,  
&emsp;for he gives sleep to his loved ones.  
<sup>3</sup> Behold, children are a heritage of the LORD.  
&emsp;The fruit of the womb is his reward.  
<sup>4</sup> As arrows in the hand of a mighty man,  
&emsp;so are the children of youth.  
<sup>5</sup> Happy is the man who has his quiver full of them.  
&emsp;They won’t be disappointed when they speak with their enemies in the gate.

## Psalm 128

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> Blessed is everyone who fears the LORD,  
&emsp;who walks in his ways.  
<sup>2</sup> For you will eat the labor of your hands.  
&emsp;You will be happy, and it will be well with you.  
<sup>3</sup> Your wife will be as a fruitful vine in the innermost parts of your house,  
&emsp;your children like olive shoots around your table.  
<sup>4</sup> Behold, this is how the man who fears the LORD is blessed.  
&emsp;<sup>5</sup> May the LORD bless you out of Zion,  
&emsp;and may you see the good of Jerusalem all the days of your life.  
<sup>6</sup> Yes, may you see your children’s children.  
&emsp;Peace be upon Israel.

## Psalm 129

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> Many times they have afflicted me from my youth up.  
&emsp;Let Israel now say:  
<sup>2</sup> many times they have afflicted me from my youth up,  
&emsp;yet they have not prevailed against me.  
<sup>3</sup> The plowers plowed on my back.  
&emsp;They made their furrows long.  
<sup>4</sup> The LORD is righteous.  
&emsp;He has cut apart the cords of the wicked.  
<sup>5</sup> Let them be disappointed and turned backward,  
&emsp;all those who hate Zion.  
<sup>6</sup> Let them be as the grass on the housetops,  
&emsp;which withers before it grows up,  
<sup>7</sup> with which the reaper doesn’t fill his hand,  
&emsp;nor he who binds sheaves, his bosom.  
<sup>8</sup> Neither do those who go by say,  
&emsp;“The blessing of the LORD be on you.  
&emsp;We bless you in the LORD’s name.”

## Psalm 130

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> Out of the depths I have cried to you, LORD.[^82]  
<sup>2</sup> Lord, hear my voice.  
&emsp;Let your ears be attentive to the voice of my petitions.  
<sup>3</sup> If you, LORD,[^83] kept a record of sins,  
&emsp;Lord, who could stand?  
<sup>4</sup> But there is forgiveness with you,  
&emsp;therefore you are feared.  
<sup>5</sup> I wait for the LORD.  
&emsp;My soul waits.  
&emsp;I hope in his word.  
<sup>6</sup> My soul longs for the Lord more than watchmen long for the morning,  
&emsp;more than watchmen for the morning.  
<sup>7</sup> Israel, hope in the LORD,  
&emsp;for there is loving kindness with the LORD.  
&emsp;Abundant redemption is with him.  
<sup>8</sup> He will redeem Israel from all their sins.

## Psalm 131

*A Song of Ascents. By David.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, my heart isn’t arrogant, nor my eyes lofty;  
&emsp;nor do I concern myself with great matters,  
&emsp;or things too wonderful for me.  
<sup>2</sup> Surely I have stilled and quieted my soul,  
&emsp;like a weaned child with his mother,  
&emsp;like a weaned child is my soul within me.  
<sup>3</sup> Israel, hope in the LORD,  
&emsp;from this time forward and forever more.

## Psalm 132

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, remember David and all his ~~affliction~~ <ins>meekness</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c386"></a>[c386](#c386)</sup>,  
<sup>2</sup> how he swore to the LORD,  
&emsp;and vowed to the Mighty One of Jacob:  
<sup>3</sup> “Surely I will not come into the structure of my house,  
&emsp;nor go up into my bed;  
<sup>4</sup> I will not give sleep to my eyes,  
&emsp;or slumber to my eyelids,  
<sup>5</sup> until I find out a place for the LORD,  
&emsp;a dwelling for the Mighty One of Jacob.”  
<sup>6</sup> Behold, we heard of it in Ephrathah.  
&emsp;We found it in the field of Jaar.  
<sup>7</sup> “We will go into his dwelling place.  
&emsp;We will worship at his footstool.”  
<sup>8</sup> Arise, LORD, into your resting place,  
&emsp;you, and the ark of your strength.  
<sup>9</sup> Let your priests be clothed with righteousness.  
&emsp;Let your saints shout for joy!  
<sup>10</sup> For your servant David’s sake,  
&emsp;don’t turn away the face of your anointed one.  
<sup>11</sup> The LORD has sworn to David in truth.  
&emsp;He will not turn from it:  
&emsp;“I will set the fruit of your body on your throne.  
<sup>12</sup> If your children will keep my covenant,  
&emsp;my testimony that I will teach them,  
&emsp;their children also will sit on your throne forever more.”  
<sup>13</sup> For the LORD has chosen Zion.  
&emsp;He has desired it for his habitation.  
<sup>14</sup> “This is my resting place forever.  
&emsp;I will live here, for I have desired it.  
<sup>15</sup> I will abundantly bless her provision.  
&emsp;I will satisfy her poor with bread.  
<sup>16</sup> I will also clothe her priests with salvation.  
&emsp;Her saints will shout aloud for joy.  
<sup>17</sup> I will make the horn of David to bud there.  
&emsp;I have ordained a lamp for my anointed.  
<sup>18</sup> I will clothe his enemies with shame,  
&emsp;but on himself, his ~~crown will shine~~ <ins>diadem will blossom</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c387"></a>[c387](#c387)</sup>.”

## Psalm 133

*A Song of Ascents. By David.*

<sup>1</sup> See how good and how pleasant it is  
&emsp;for brothers to live together in unity!  
<sup>2</sup> It is like the precious oil on the head,  
&emsp;that ran down on the beard,  
&emsp;even Aaron’s beard,  
&emsp;that came down on the edge of his robes,  
<sup>3</sup> like the dew of Hermon,  
&emsp;that comes down on the hills of Zion;  
&emsp;for there the LORD gives the blessing,  
&emsp;even life forever more.

## Psalm 134

*A Song of Ascents.*

<sup>1</sup> Look! Praise the LORD, all you servants of the LORD,  
&emsp;who stand by night in the LORD’s house!  
<sup>2</sup> Lift up your hands in the sanctuary.  
&emsp;Praise the LORD!  
<sup>3</sup> May the LORD bless you from Zion,  
&emsp;even he who made heaven and earth.

## Psalm 135

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^84]  
&emsp;Praise the LORD’s[^85] name!  
&emsp;Praise him, you servants of the LORD,  
<sup>2</sup> you who stand in the LORD’s house,  
&emsp;in the courts of our God’s house.  
<sup>3</sup> Praise the LORD,[^86] for the LORD is good.  
&emsp;Sing praises to his name, for that is pleasant.  
<sup>4</sup> For the LORD[^87] has chosen Jacob for himself,  
&emsp;Israel for his own possession.  
<sup>5</sup> For I know that the LORD is great,  
&emsp;that our Lord is above all gods.  
<sup>6</sup> Whatever the LORD pleased, that he has done,  
&emsp;in heaven and in earth, in the seas and in all deeps.  
<sup>7</sup> He causes the clouds to rise from the ends of the earth.  
&emsp;He makes lightnings with the rain.  
&emsp;He brings the wind out of his treasuries.  
<sup>8</sup> He struck the firstborn of Egypt,  
&emsp;both of man and animal.  
<sup>9</sup> He sent signs and wonders into the middle of you, Egypt,  
&emsp;on Pharaoh, and on all his servants.  
<sup>10</sup> He struck many nations,  
&emsp;and killed mighty kings—  
<sup>11</sup> Sihon king of the Amorites,  
&emsp;Og king of Bashan,  
&emsp;and all the kingdoms of Canaan—  
<sup>12</sup> and gave their land for a heritage,  
&emsp;a heritage to Israel, his people.  
<sup>13</sup> Your name, LORD, endures forever;  
&emsp;your renown, LORD, throughout all generations.  
<sup>14</sup> For the LORD will judge his people  
&emsp;and have compassion on his servants.

<sup>15</sup> The idols of the nations are silver and gold,  
&emsp;the work of men’s hands.  
<sup>16</sup> They have mouths, but they can’t speak.  
&emsp;They have eyes, but they can’t see.  
<sup>17</sup> They have ears, but they can’t hear,  
&emsp;neither is there any breath in their mouths.  
<sup>18</sup> Those who make them will be like them,  
&emsp;yes, everyone who trusts in them.  
<sup>19</sup> House of Israel, praise the LORD!  
&emsp;House of Aaron, praise the LORD!  
<sup>20</sup> House of Levi, praise the LORD!  
&emsp;You who fear the LORD, praise the LORD!  
<sup>21</sup> Blessed be the LORD from Zion,  
&emsp;who dwells in Jerusalem.  
Praise the LORD![^88]

## Psalm 136

<sup>1</sup> Give thanks to the LORD, for he is good,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>2</sup> Give thanks to the God of gods,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>3</sup> Give thanks to the Lord of lords,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>4</sup> to him who alone does great wonders,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>5</sup> to him who by understanding made the heavens,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>6</sup> to him who spread out the earth above the waters,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>7</sup> to him who made the great lights,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>8</sup> the sun to rule by day,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>9</sup> the moon and stars to rule by night,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>10</sup> to him who struck down the Egyptian firstborn,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>11</sup> and brought out Israel from among them,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>12</sup> with a strong hand, and with an outstretched arm,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>13</sup> to him who divided the Red Sea apart,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>14</sup> and made Israel to pass through the middle of it,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>15</sup> but overthrew Pharaoh and his army in the Red Sea,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>16</sup> to him who led his people through the wilderness,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>17</sup> to him who struck great kings,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>18</sup> and killed mighty kings,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>19</sup> Sihon king of the Amorites,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>20</sup> Og king of Bashan,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>21</sup> and gave their land as an inheritance,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>22</sup> even a heritage to Israel his servant,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>23</sup> who remembered us in our low estate,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>24</sup> and has delivered us from our adversaries,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever;  
<sup>25</sup> who gives food to every creature,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.  
<sup>26</sup> Oh give thanks to the God of heaven,  
&emsp;for his loving kindness endures forever.

## Psalm 137

<sup>1</sup> By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat down.  
&emsp;Yes, we wept, when we remembered Zion.  
<sup>2</sup> On the willows in that land,  
&emsp;we hung up our harps.  
<sup>3</sup> For there, those who led us captive asked us for songs.  
&emsp;Those who tormented us demanded songs of joy:  
&emsp;“Sing us one of the songs of Zion!”  
<sup>4</sup> How can we sing the LORD’s song in a foreign land?  
<sup>5</sup> If I forget you, Jerusalem,  
&emsp;let my right hand forget its skill.  
<sup>6</sup> Let my tongue stick to the roof of my mouth if I don’t remember you,  
&emsp;if I don’t prefer Jerusalem above my chief joy.  
<sup>7</sup> Remember, LORD, against the children of Edom in the day of Jerusalem,  
&emsp;who said, “Raze it!  
&emsp;Raze it even to its foundation!”  
<sup>8</sup> Daughter of Babylon, ~~doomed to destruction~~ <ins>you devastator</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c388"></a>[c388](#c388)</sup>,  
&emsp;he will be happy who repays you,  
&emsp;as you have done to us.  
<sup>9</sup> Happy shall he be,  
&emsp;who takes and dashes your little ones against the rock.

## Psalm 138

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> I will give you thanks with my whole heart.  
&emsp;Before the gods,[^89] I will sing praises to you.  
<sup>2</sup> I will bow down toward your holy temple,  
&emsp;and give thanks to your Name for your loving kindness and for your truth;  
&emsp;for ~~you have exalted your Name and your Word above all~~ <ins>you have exalted your Word above every name</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c389"></a>[c389](#c389)</sup>.  
<sup>3</sup> In the day that I called, you answered me.  
&emsp;You encouraged me with strength in my soul.  
<sup>4</sup> All the kings of the earth will give you thanks, LORD,  
&emsp;for they have heard the words of your mouth.  
<sup>5</sup> Yes, they will sing of the ways of the LORD,  
&emsp;for the LORD’s glory is great!  
<sup>6</sup> For though the LORD is high, yet he looks after the lowly;  
&emsp;but he knows the proud from afar.  
<sup>7</sup> Though I walk in the middle of trouble, you will revive me.  
&emsp;You will stretch out your hand against the wrath of my enemies.  
&emsp;Your right hand will save me.  
<sup>8</sup> The LORD will fulfill that which concerns me.  
&emsp;Your loving kindness, LORD, endures forever.  
&emsp;Don’t forsake the works of your own hands.

## Psalm 139

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, you have searched me,  
&emsp;and you know me.  
<sup>2</sup> You know my sitting down and my rising up.  
&emsp;You perceive my thoughts from afar.  
<sup>3</sup> You search out my path and my lying down,  
&emsp;and are acquainted with all my ways.  
<sup>4</sup> For there is not a word on my tongue,  
&emsp;but behold, LORD, you know it altogether.  
<sup>5</sup> You hem me in behind and before.  
&emsp;You laid your hand on me.  
<sup>6</sup> This knowledge is beyond me.  
&emsp;It’s lofty.  
&emsp;I can’t attain it.  
<sup>7</sup> Where could I go from your Spirit?  
&emsp;Or where could I flee from your presence?  
<sup>8</sup> If I ascend up into heaven, you are there.  
&emsp;If I make my bed in Sheol,[^90] behold, you are there!  
<sup>9</sup> If I take the wings of the dawn,  
&emsp;and settle in the uttermost parts of the sea,  
<sup>10</sup> even there your hand will lead me,  
&emsp;and your right hand will hold me.  
<sup>11</sup> If I say, “Surely the darkness will overwhelm me.  
&emsp;The light around me will be night,”  
<sup>12</sup> even the darkness doesn’t hide from you,  
&emsp;but the night shines as the day.  
&emsp;The darkness is like light to you.  
<sup>13</sup> For you formed my inmost being.  
&emsp;You knit me together in my mother’s womb.  
<sup>14</sup> I will give thanks to you,  
&emsp;for I am fearfully and wonderfully made.  
Your works are wonderful.  
&emsp;My soul knows that very well.  
<sup>15</sup> My frame wasn’t hidden from you,  
&emsp;when I was made in secret,  
&emsp;woven together in the depths of the earth.  
<sup>16</sup> Your eyes saw my body.  
&emsp;In your book they were all written,  
&emsp;the days that were ordained for me,  
&emsp;when as yet there were none of them.  
<sup>17</sup> How precious to me are your thoughts, God!  
&emsp;How vast is their sum!  
<sup>18</sup> If I would count them, they are more in number than the sand.  
&emsp;When I wake up, I am still with you.  
<sup>19</sup> If only you, God, would kill the wicked.  
&emsp;Get away from me, you bloodthirsty men!  
<sup>20</sup> For they speak against you wickedly.  
&emsp;Your enemies ~~take your name in vain~~ <ins>lift themselves up against you in vain</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c390"></a>[c390](#c390)</sup>.  
<sup>21</sup> LORD, don’t I hate those who hate you?  
&emsp;Am I not grieved with those who rise up against you?  
<sup>22</sup> I hate them with perfect hatred.  
&emsp;They have become my enemies.  
<sup>23</sup> Search me, God, and know my heart.  
&emsp;Try me, and know my thoughts.  
<sup>24</sup> See if there is any ~~wicked~~ <ins>vain</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c391"></a>[c391](#c391)</sup> way in me,  
&emsp;and lead me in the everlasting way.

## Psalm 140

*For the Chief Musician. A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> Deliver me, LORD, from evil men.  
&emsp;Preserve me from violent men:  
<sup>2</sup> those who devise mischief in their hearts.  
&emsp;They continually gather themselves together for war.  
<sup>3</sup> They have sharpened their tongues like a serpent.  
&emsp;Viper’s poison is under their lips. *Selah.*  
<sup>4</sup> LORD, keep me from the hands of the wicked.  
&emsp;Preserve me from the violent men who have determined to trip my feet.  
<sup>5</sup> The proud have hidden a snare for me,  
&emsp;they have spread the cords of a net by the path.  
&emsp;They have set traps for me. *Selah.*  
<sup>6</sup> I said to the LORD, “You are my God.”  
&emsp;Listen to the cry of my petitions, LORD.  
<sup>7</sup> LORD, the Lord, the strength of my salvation,  
&emsp;you have covered my head in the day of battle.  
<sup>8</sup> LORD, don’t grant the desires of the wicked.  
&emsp;Don’t let their evil plans succeed, or they will become proud. *Selah.*  
<sup>9</sup> As for the head of those who surround me,  
&emsp;let the mischief of their own lips cover them.  
<sup>10</sup> Let burning coals fall on them.  
&emsp;Let them be thrown into the fire,  
&emsp;into miry pits, from where they never rise.  
<sup>11</sup> An evil speaker won’t be established on the earth.  
&emsp;Evil will hunt the violent man to overthrow him.  
<sup>12</sup> I know that the LORD will maintain the cause of the afflicted,  
&emsp;and justice for the needy.  
<sup>13</sup> Surely the righteous will give thanks to your name.  
&emsp;The upright will dwell in your presence.

## Psalm 141

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> LORD, I have called on you.  
&emsp;Come to me quickly!  
&emsp;Listen to my voice when I call to you.  
<sup>2</sup> Let my prayer be set before you like incense;  
&emsp;the lifting up of my hands like the evening sacrifice.  
<sup>3</sup> Set a watch, LORD, before my mouth.  
&emsp;Keep the door of my lips.  
<sup>4</sup> Don’t incline my heart to any evil thing,  
&emsp;to practice deeds of wickedness with men who work iniquity.  
&emsp;Don’t let me eat of their delicacies.  
<sup>5</sup> Let the righteous strike me, it is kindness;  
&emsp;let him reprove me, it is like oil on the head;  
&emsp;don’t let my head refuse it;  
&emsp;Yet my prayer is always against evil deeds.  
<sup>6</sup> Their judges are thrown down by the sides of the rock.  
&emsp;They will hear my words, for they are well spoken.  
<sup>7</sup> “As when one plows and breaks up the earth,  
&emsp;~~our bones~~ <ins>their bones</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c392"></a>[c392](#c392)</sup> are scattered at the mouth of Sheol.”[^91]  
<sup>8</sup> For my eyes are on you, LORD, the Lord.  
&emsp;I take refuge in you.  
&emsp;Don’t leave my soul destitute.  
<sup>9</sup> Keep me from the snare which they have laid for me,  
&emsp;from the traps of the workers of iniquity.  
<sup>10</sup> Let the wicked fall together into their own nets  
&emsp;while I pass by.

## Psalm 142

*A contemplation by David, when he was in the cave. A Prayer.*

<sup>1</sup> I cry with my voice to the LORD.  
&emsp;With my voice, I ask the LORD for mercy.  
<sup>2</sup> I pour out my complaint before him.  
&emsp;I tell him my troubles.  
<sup>3</sup> When my spirit was overwhelmed within me,  
&emsp;you knew my route.  
On the path in which I walk,  
&emsp;they have hidden a snare for me.  
<sup>4</sup> Look on my right, and see;  
&emsp;for there is no one who is concerned for me.  
&emsp;Refuge has fled from me.  
&emsp;No one cares for my soul.  
<sup>5</sup> I cried to you, LORD.  
&emsp;I said, “You are my refuge,  
&emsp;my portion in the land of the living.”  
<sup>6</sup> Listen to my cry,  
&emsp;for I am in desperate need.  
Deliver me from my persecutors,  
&emsp;for they are too strong for me.  
<sup>7</sup> Bring my soul out of prison,  
&emsp;that I may give thanks to your name.  
The righteous will surround me,  
&emsp;for you will be good to me.

## Psalm 143

*A Psalm by David.*

<sup>1</sup> Hear my prayer, LORD.  
&emsp;Listen to my petitions.  
&emsp;In your faithfulness and righteousness, relieve me.  
<sup>2</sup> Don’t enter into judgment with your servant,  
&emsp;for in your sight no man living is righteous.  
<sup>3</sup> For the enemy pursues my soul.  
&emsp;He has struck my life down to the ground.  
&emsp;He has made me live in dark places, as those who have been long dead.  
<sup>4</sup> Therefore my spirit is overwhelmed within me.  
&emsp;My heart within me is desolate.  
<sup>5</sup> I remember the days of old.  
&emsp;I meditate on all your doings.  
&emsp;I contemplate the work of your hands.  
<sup>6</sup> I spread out my hands to you.  
&emsp;My soul thirsts for you, like a parched land. *Selah.*  
<sup>7</sup> Hurry to answer me, LORD.  
&emsp;My spirit fails.  
Don’t hide your face from me,  
&emsp;so that I don’t become like those who go down into the pit.  
<sup>8</sup> Cause me to hear your loving kindness in the morning,  
&emsp;for I trust in you.  
Cause me to know the way in which I should walk,  
&emsp;for I lift up my soul to you.  
<sup>9</sup> Deliver me, LORD, from my enemies.  
&emsp;I flee to you to hide me.  
<sup>10</sup> Teach me to do your will,  
&emsp;for you are my God.  
Your Spirit is good.  
&emsp;Lead me in the land of uprightness.  
<sup>11</sup> Revive me, LORD, for your name’s sake.  
&emsp;In your righteousness, bring my soul out of trouble.  
<sup>12</sup> In your loving kindness, cut off my enemies,  
&emsp;and destroy all those who afflict my soul,  
&emsp;for I am your servant.

## Psalm 144

*By David.*

<sup>1</sup> Blessed be the LORD, my ~~rock~~ <ins>helper</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c393"></a>[c393](#c393)</sup>,  
&emsp;who trains my hands to war,  
&emsp;and my fingers to battle—  
<sup>2</sup> my loving kindness, my fortress,  
&emsp;my high tower, my deliverer,  
&emsp;my shield, and he in whom I take refuge,  
&emsp;who subdues my people under me.  
<sup>3</sup> LORD, what is man, that you care for him?  
&emsp;Or the son of man, that you think of him?  
<sup>4</sup> Man is like ~~a breath~~ <ins>vanity</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c394"></a>[c394](#c394)</sup>.  
&emsp;His days are like a shadow that passes away.  
<sup>5</sup> Part your heavens, LORD, and come down.  
&emsp;Touch the mountains, and they will smoke.  
<sup>6</sup> Throw out lightning, and scatter them.  
&emsp;Send out your arrows, and ~~rout~~ <ins>confound</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c395"></a>[c395](#c395)</sup> them.  
<sup>7</sup> Stretch out your hand from above,  
&emsp;rescue me, and deliver me out of great waters,  
&emsp;out of the hands of foreigners,  
&emsp;<sup>8</sup> whose mouths speak deceit,  
&emsp;whose right hand is a right hand of falsehood.  
<sup>9</sup> I will sing a new song to you, God.  
&emsp;On a ten-stringed lyre, I will sing praises to you.  
<sup>10</sup> You are he who gives salvation to kings,  
&emsp;who rescues David, his servant, from ~~the deadly sword~~ <ins>the evil sword</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c396"></a>[c396](#c396)</sup>.  
<sup>11</sup> Rescue me, and deliver me out of the hands of foreigners,  
&emsp;whose mouths speak deceit,  
&emsp;whose right hand is a right hand of falsehood.

<sup>12</sup> Then our sons will be like well-nurtured plants,  
&emsp;our daughters like pillars carved to adorn a palace.  
<sup>13</sup> Our barns are full, filled with all kinds of provision.  
&emsp;~~Our sheep produce thousands and ten thousands in our fields.~~ <ins>Our sheep are countless in the thousands in our fields.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c397"></a>[c397](#c397)</sup>  
<sup>14</sup> Our oxen will pull heavy loads.  
&emsp;~~There is no breaking in, and no going away,~~ <ins>There is no breach in the wall, and no going out,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c398"></a>[c398](#c398)</sup>  
&emsp;and no outcry in our streets.  
<sup>15</sup> Happy are the people who are in such a situation.  
&emsp;Happy are the people whose God is the LORD.

## Psalm 145

*A praise psalm by David.[^92]*

<sup>1</sup> I will exalt you, my God, the King.  
&emsp;I will praise your name forever and ever.  
<sup>2</sup> Every day I will praise you.  
&emsp;I will extol your name forever and ever.  
<sup>3</sup> Great is the LORD, and greatly to be praised!  
&emsp;His greatness is unsearchable.  
<sup>4</sup> One generation will commend your works to another,  
&emsp;and will declare your mighty acts.  
<sup>5</sup> ~~I will meditate on the glorious majesty of your honor,~~ <ins>They will speak of the glorious majesty of your honor,</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c399"></a>[c399](#c399)</sup>  
&emsp;~~on your wondrous works.~~ <ins>and recount your wondrous works.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c400"></a>[c400](#c400)</sup>  
<sup>6</sup> Men will speak of the might of your awesome acts.  
&emsp;~~I will declare~~ <ins>They will declare</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c401"></a>[c401](#c401)</sup> your greatness.  
<sup>7</sup> They will utter the memory of your great goodness,  
&emsp;and will sing of your righteousness.  
<sup>8</sup> The LORD is gracious, merciful,  
&emsp;slow to anger, and of great loving kindness.  
<sup>9</sup> The LORD is good to all.  
&emsp;His tender mercies are over all his works.  
<sup>10</sup> All your works will give thanks to you, LORD.  
&emsp;Your saints will extol you.  
<sup>11</sup> They will speak of the glory of your kingdom,  
&emsp;and talk about your power,  
<sup>12</sup> to make known to the sons of men his mighty acts,  
&emsp;the glory of the majesty of his kingdom.  
<sup>13</sup> Your kingdom is an everlasting kingdom.  
&emsp;Your dominion endures throughout all generations.  
The LORD is faithful in all his words,  
&emsp;and ~~loving~~ <ins>holy</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c402"></a>[c402](#c402)</sup> in all his deeds.[^93]  
<sup>14</sup> The LORD upholds all who fall,  
&emsp;and raises up all those who are bowed down.  
<sup>15</sup> The eyes of all wait for you.  
&emsp;You give them their food in due season.  
<sup>16</sup> You open your hand,  
&emsp;and satisfy the desire of every living thing.  
<sup>17</sup> The LORD is righteous in all his ways,  
&emsp;and ~~gracious in all his works~~ <ins>holy in all his works</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c403"></a>[c403](#c403)</sup>.  
<sup>18</sup> The LORD is near to all those who call on him,  
&emsp;to all who call on him in truth.  
<sup>19</sup> He will fulfill the desire of those who fear him.  
&emsp;He also will hear their cry, and will save them.  
<sup>20</sup> The LORD preserves all those who love him,  
&emsp;but he will destroy all the wicked.  
<sup>21</sup> My mouth will speak the praise of the LORD.  
&emsp;Let all flesh bless his holy name forever and ever.

## Psalm 146

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^94]  
&emsp;Praise the LORD,[^95] my soul.  
<sup>2</sup> While I live, I will praise the LORD.  
&emsp;I will sing praises to my God as long as I exist.  
<sup>3</sup> Don’t put your trust in princes,  
&emsp;in a son of man in whom there is no help.  
<sup>4</sup> His spirit departs, and he returns to the earth.  
&emsp;In that very day, his thoughts perish.  
<sup>5</sup> Happy is he who has the God of Jacob for his help,  
&emsp;whose hope is in the LORD, his God,  
<sup>6</sup> who made heaven and earth,  
&emsp;the sea, and all that is in them;  
&emsp;who keeps truth forever;  
<sup>7</sup> who executes justice for the oppressed;  
&emsp;who gives food to the hungry.  
The LORD frees the prisoners.  
&emsp;<sup>8</sup> The LORD opens the eyes of the blind.  
&emsp;The LORD raises up those who are bowed down.  
&emsp;The LORD loves the righteous.  
<sup>9</sup> The LORD preserves the foreigners.  
&emsp;He upholds the fatherless and widow,  
&emsp;but he turns the way of the wicked upside down.  
<sup>10</sup> The LORD will reign forever;  
&emsp;your God, O Zion, to all generations.  
Praise the LORD![^96]

## Psalm 147

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD,[^97]  
&emsp;for it is good to sing praises to our God;  
&emsp;for it is pleasant and fitting to praise him.  
<sup>2</sup> The LORD[^98] builds up Jerusalem.  
&emsp;He gathers together the outcasts of Israel.  
<sup>3</sup> He heals the broken in heart,  
&emsp;and binds up their wounds.  
<sup>4</sup> He counts the number of the stars.  
&emsp;He calls them all by their names.  
<sup>5</sup> Great is our Lord, and mighty in power.  
&emsp;His understanding is infinite.  
<sup>6</sup> The LORD upholds the humble.  
&emsp;He brings the wicked down to the ground.  
<sup>7</sup> Sing to the LORD with thanksgiving.  
&emsp;Sing praises on the harp to our God,  
<sup>8</sup> who covers the sky with clouds,  
&emsp;who prepares rain for the earth,  
&emsp;who makes grass grow on the mountains.  
<sup>9</sup> He provides food for the livestock,  
&emsp;and for the young ravens when they call.  
<sup>10</sup> He doesn’t delight in the strength of the horse.  
&emsp;He takes no pleasure in the legs of a man.
