<sup>11</sup> The LORD takes pleasure in those who fear him,  
&emsp;in those who hope in his loving kindness.  
<sup>12</sup> Praise the LORD, Jerusalem!  
&emsp;Praise your God, Zion!  
<sup>13</sup> For he has strengthened the bars of your gates.  
&emsp;He has blessed your children within you.  
<sup>14</sup> He makes peace in your borders.  
&emsp;He fills you with the finest of the wheat.  
<sup>15</sup> He sends out his commandment to the earth.  
&emsp;His word runs very swiftly.  
<sup>16</sup> He gives snow like wool,  
&emsp;and scatters frost like ashes.  
<sup>17</sup> He hurls down his hail like pebbles.  
&emsp;Who can stand before his cold?  
<sup>18</sup> He sends out his word, and melts them.  
&emsp;~~He causes his wind to blow, and the waters flow.~~ <ins>His wind will blow, and the waters will flow.</ins><sup>NV</sup><sup><a name="ref-c404"></a>[c404](#c404)</sup>  
<sup>19</sup> He shows his word to Jacob,  
&emsp;his statutes and his ordinances to Israel.  
<sup>20</sup> He has not done this for just any nation.  
&emsp;They don’t know his ordinances.  
Praise the LORD![^99]

## Psalm 148

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^100]  
&emsp;Praise the LORD[^101] from the heavens!  
&emsp;Praise him in the heights!  
<sup>2</sup> Praise him, all his angels!  
&emsp;Praise him, all his army!  
<sup>3</sup> Praise him, sun and moon!  
&emsp;Praise him, all you shining stars!  
<sup>4</sup> Praise him, you heavens of heavens,  
&emsp;you waters that are above the heavens.  
<sup>5</sup> Let them praise the LORD’s name,  
&emsp;for he commanded, and they were created.  
<sup>6</sup> He has also established them forever and ever.  
&emsp;He has made a decree which will not pass away.  
<sup>7</sup> Praise the LORD from the earth,  
&emsp;you great sea creatures, and all depths,  
<sup>8</sup> lightning and hail, snow and clouds,  
&emsp;stormy wind, fulfilling his word,  
<sup>9</sup> mountains and all hills,  
&emsp;fruit trees and all cedars,  
<sup>10</sup> wild animals and all livestock,  
&emsp;small creatures and flying birds,  
<sup>11</sup> kings of the earth and all peoples,  
&emsp;princes and all judges of the earth,  
<sup>12</sup> both young men and maidens,  
&emsp;old men and children.  
<sup>13</sup> Let them praise the LORD’s name,  
&emsp;for his name alone is exalted.  
&emsp;His glory is above the earth and the heavens.  
<sup>14</sup> He has lifted up the horn of his people,  
&emsp;the praise of all his saints,  
&emsp;even of the children of Israel, a people near to him.  
Praise the LORD![^102]

## Psalm 149

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^103]  
&emsp;Sing to the LORD a new song,  
&emsp;his praise in the assembly of the saints.  
<sup>2</sup> Let Israel rejoice in him who made them.  
&emsp;Let the children of Zion be joyful in their King.  
<sup>3</sup> Let them praise his name in the dance!  
&emsp;Let them sing praises to him with tambourine and harp!  
<sup>4</sup> For the LORD takes pleasure in his people.  
&emsp;He crowns the humble with salvation.  
<sup>5</sup> Let the saints rejoice in honor.  
&emsp;Let them sing for joy on their beds.  
<sup>6</sup> May the high praises of God be in their mouths,  
&emsp;and a two-edged sword in their hand,  
<sup>7</sup> to execute vengeance on the nations,  
&emsp;and punishments on the peoples;  
<sup>8</sup> to bind their kings with chains,  
&emsp;and their nobles with fetters of iron;  
<sup>9</sup> to execute on them the written judgment.  
&emsp;All his saints have this honor.  
Praise the LORD![^104]

## Psalm 150

<sup>1</sup> Praise the LORD![^105]  
&emsp;Praise God in his sanctuary!  
&emsp;Praise him in his heavens for his acts of power!  
<sup>2</sup> Praise him for his mighty acts!  
&emsp;Praise him according to his excellent greatness!  
<sup>3</sup> Praise him with the sounding of the trumpet!  
&emsp;Praise him with harp and lyre!  
<sup>4</sup> Praise him with tambourine and dancing!  
&emsp;Praise him with stringed instruments and flute!  
<sup>5</sup> Praise him with loud cymbals!  
&emsp;Praise him with resounding cymbals!  
<sup>6</sup> Let everything that has breath praise the LORD![^106]  
&emsp;Praise the LORD![^107]

[^1]: **1:2** When rendered in ALL CAPITAL LETTERS, “LORD” or “GOD” is the translation of God’s Proper Name (Hebrew “יהוה”, usually pronounced Yahweh).
[^2]: **2:2** The word “Anointed” is the same as the word for “Messiah” or “Christ”
